10	import { useModal, type ModalSize } from '@/shared/hooks/useModal';
11	import { Button } from '@/shared/components/ui/button';
12	import { cn } from '@/shared/components/ui/contracts/cn';
13	
14	interface ModalContainerProps {
15	  open: boolean;
16	  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
17	  size?: ModalSize;
18	  title: ReactNode;
19	  description?: ReactNode;
20	  children: ReactNode;
21	  footer?: ReactNode;
22	  className?: string;
23	  contentClassName?: string;
24	  hideCloseButton?: boolean;
25	  preventOutsideClose?: boolean;
26	}
27	
28	export function ModalContainer({
29	  open,
30	  onOpenChange,
31	  size = 'medium',
32	  title,
33	  description,
34	  children,
35	  footer,
36	  className,
37	  contentClassName,
38	  hideCloseButton = false,
39	  preventOutsideClose = false,
40	}: ModalContainerProps) {
41	  const modal = useModal(size);
42	
43	  const handleOpenChange = (newOpen: boolean) => {
44	    if (!newOpen && preventOutsideClose) return;
45	    onOpenChange(newOpen);
46	  };
47	
48	  return (
49	    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={handleOpenChange}>
50	      <DialogContent
51	        className={cn(modal.className, className)}
52	        style={modal.style}
53	        data-hide-close={hideCloseButton || undefined}
54	      >
55	        <div className={modal.headerClass}>
56	          <DialogHeader
57	            className={cn(
58	              modal.isMobile ? 'px-4 pt-2 pb-1' : 'px-6 pt-2 pb-1',
59	              'flex-shrink-0'
60	            )}
61	          >
62	            <DialogTitle>{title}</DialogTitle>
63	            {description && <DialogDescription>{description}</DialogDescription>}
64	          </DialogHeader>
65	        </div>
66	
67	        <div
68	          className={cn(
69	            modal.scrollClass,
70	            modal.isMobile ? 'px-4' : 'px-6',
71	            contentClassName
72	          )}
73	        >
74	          {children}
75	        </div>
76	
77	        {footer && (
78	          <div className={modal.footerClass}>
79	            <DialogFooter
80	              className={cn(
81	                modal.isMobile ? 'px-4 pt-4 pb-0 flex-row justify-between' : 'px-6 pt-5 pb-0',
82	                'border-t'
83	              )}
84	            >
85	              {footer}
86	            </DialogFooter>
87	          </div>
88	        )}
89	      </DialogContent>
90	    </Dialog>

92	}

104	export function ModalFooterButtons({

106	  confirmText = 'Confirm',
107	  onCancel,
108	  onConfirm,
109	  isLoading = false,
110	  destructive = false,
111	  disabled = false,
112	}: ModalFooterButtonsProps) {
113	  return (
114	    <>
115	      <Button
116	        variant="retro-secondary"
117	        size="retro-sm"
118	        onClick={onCancel}
119	        disabled={disabled || isLoading}
120	        className="mr-auto sm:mr-0"
121	      >
122	        {cancelText}
123	      </Button>
124	      <Button
125	        variant={destructive ? 'destructive' : 'retro'}
126	        size="retro-sm"
127	        onClick={onConfirm}
128	        disabled={disabled || isLoading}
129	      >
130	        {isLoading ? 'Loading...' : confirmText}
131	      </Button>
132	    </>
133	  );
134	}

1	/*
2	 * Keep `<Dialog open={...}>` mounted until `open` flips false.
3	 * Base UI releases focus scope and body pointer-events during close, not on unmount.
4	 * Returning `null` above an open dialog can strand the page in a blocked state.
5	 * If closed-state children are expensive, gate `<DialogContent>` instead.
6	 */

1	import * as React from 'react';
2	import {
3	  useIsOverlayTopmost,
4	  useOverlayStackApi,
5	  useTopmostOverlayContainingElement,
6	} from '@/shared/state/overlayStack';
7	
8	export type OverlayElementKind = 'popup' | 'backdrop';
9	
10	interface OverlayElements {
11	  popup: HTMLElement | null;
12	  backdrop: HTMLElement | null;
13	}
14	
15	export interface OverlayHandle {
16	  id: string;
17	  getType: () => string;
18	  isModal: () => boolean;
19	  getElement: (kind: OverlayElementKind) => HTMLElement | null;
20	  getElements: () => readonly HTMLElement[];
21	  isRegistered: () => boolean;
22	  containsElement: (element: Element | null) => boolean;
23	}
24	
25	export interface UseOverlayBridgeOptions {
26	  id?: string;
27	  type: string;
28	  modal?: boolean;
29	  restoreFocusTo?: HTMLElement | null;
30	}
31	
32	interface OverlayBridgeContextValue {
33	  handle: OverlayHandle;
34	  registerElement: (kind: OverlayElementKind, element: HTMLElement | null) => void;
35	}
36	
37	const OverlayBridgeContext = React.createContext<OverlayBridgeContextValue | null>(null);
38	
39	const OVERLAY_ID_PREFIX = 'overlay';
40	
41	function isHTMLElement(value: unknown): value is HTMLElement {
42	  return value instanceof HTMLElement;
43	}
44	
45	function sanitizeOverlayId(id: string): string {
46	  return id.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, '-');
47	}
48	
49	function setOverlayMetadata(
50	  element: HTMLElement,
51	  handle: OverlayHandle,
52	  kind: OverlayElementKind,
53	): void {
54	  element.dataset.overlayStackId = handle.id;
55	  element.dataset.overlayStackType = handle.getType();
56	  element.dataset.overlayStackKind = kind;
57	}
58	
59	function clearOverlayMetadata(element: HTMLElement): void {
60	  delete element.dataset.overlayStackId;
61	  delete element.dataset.overlayStackType;
62	  delete element.dataset.overlayStackKind;
63	}
64	
65	function getTrackedElements(elements: OverlayElements): readonly HTMLElement[] {
66	  return [elements.popup, elements.backdrop].filter(isHTMLElement);
67	}
68	
69	function getRestoreFocusTarget(
70	  explicitTarget: HTMLElement | null | undefined,
71	  elements: readonly HTMLElement[],
72	): HTMLElement | null {
73	  if (explicitTarget !== undefined) {
74	    return explicitTarget;
75	  }
76	
77	  const activeElement = document.activeElement;
78	  if (!(activeElement instanceof HTMLElement)) {
79	    return null;
80	  }
81	
82	  if (elements.some((element) => element === activeElement || element.contains(activeElement))) {
83	    return null;
84	  }
85	
86	  return activeElement;
87	}
88	
89	function useOverlayBridgeContextValue(): OverlayBridgeContextValue {
90	  const context = React.useContext(OverlayBridgeContext);
91	  if (!context) {
92	    throw new Error('Overlay bridge hooks must be used within an OverlayInstanceProvider.');
93	  }
94	
95	  return context;
96	}
97	
98	/**
99	 * Creates a stable overlay bridge that registers the overlay only while popup/backdrop
100	 * DOM is actually present and keeps the stack entry updated as refs change.
101	 */
102	export function useOverlayBridge(options: UseOverlayBridgeOptions): OverlayBridgeContextValue {
103	  const stackApi = useOverlayStackApi();
104	  const reactId = React.useId();
105	  const overlayId = React.useMemo(
106	    () => sanitizeOverlayId(options.id ?? `${OVERLAY_ID_PREFIX}-${reactId}`),
107	    [options.id, reactId],
108	  );
109	  const elementsRef = React.useRef<OverlayElements>({ popup: null, backdrop: null });
110	  const registeredRef = React.useRef(false);
111	  const typeRef = React.useRef(options.type);
112	  const modalRef = React.useRef(options.modal ?? false);
113	  const restoreFocusToRef = React.useRef<HTMLElement | null | undefined>(options.restoreFocusTo);
114	
115	  typeRef.current = options.type;
116	  modalRef.current = options.modal ?? false;
117	  restoreFocusToRef.current = options.restoreFocusTo;
118	
119	  const handleRef = React.useRef<OverlayHandle | null>(null);
120	  if (!handleRef.current) {
121	    handleRef.current = {
122	      id: overlayId,
123	      getType: () => typeRef.current,
124	      isModal: () => modalRef.current,
125	      getElement: (kind) => elementsRef.current[kind],
126	      getElements: () => getTrackedElements(elementsRef.current),
127	      isRegistered: () => registeredRef.current,
128	      containsElement: (element) =>
129	        getTrackedElements(elementsRef.current).some(
130	          (candidate) => candidate === element || candidate.contains(element),
131	        ),
132	    };
133	  }
134	  const handle = handleRef.current;
135	
136	  const syncRegistration = React.useCallback(() => {
137	    const elements = getTrackedElements(elementsRef.current);
138	
139	    if (elements.length === 0) {
140	      if (registeredRef.current) {
141	        stackApi.getState().popOverlay(handle.id);
142	        registeredRef.current = false;
143	      }
144	      return;
145	    }
146	
147	    const payload = {
148	      type: handle.getType(),
149	      modal: handle.isModal(),
150	      restoreFocusTo: getRestoreFocusTarget(restoreFocusToRef.current, elements),
151	      elements,
152	    };
153	
154	    if (!registeredRef.current) {
155	      stackApi.getState().pushOverlay({ id: handle.id, ...payload });
156	      registeredRef.current = true;
157	      return;
158	    }
159	
160	    stackApi.getState().updateOverlay(handle.id, payload);
161	  }, [handle, stackApi]);
162	
163	  const registerElement = React.useCallback(
164	    (kind: OverlayElementKind, element: HTMLElement | null) => {
165	      const previous = elementsRef.current[kind];
166	      if (previous === element) {
167	        syncRegistration();
168	        return;
169	      }
170	
171	      if (previous) {
172	        clearOverlayMetadata(previous);
173	      }
174	
175	      elementsRef.current[kind] = element;
176	
177	      if (element) {
178	        setOverlayMetadata(element, handle, kind);
179	      }
180	
181	      syncRegistration();
182	    },
183	    [handle, syncRegistration],
184	  );
185	
186	  React.useEffect(() => {
187	    syncRegistration();
188	  }, [syncRegistration, options.modal, options.restoreFocusTo, options.type]);
189	
190	  React.useEffect(() => {
191	    return () => {
192	      const elements = elementsRef.current;
193	      if (elements.popup) {
194	        clearOverlayMetadata(elements.popup);
195	      }
196	      if (elements.backdrop) {
197	        clearOverlayMetadata(elements.backdrop);
198	      }
199	      if (registeredRef.current) {
200	        stackApi.getState().popOverlay(handle.id);
201	        registeredRef.current = false;
202	      }
203	    };
204	  }, [handle, stackApi]);
205	
206	  return React.useMemo(
207	    () => ({
208	      handle,
209	      registerElement,
210	    }),
211	    [handle, registerElement],
212	  );
213	}
214	
215	/** Provides the current overlay bridge to popup/backdrop descendants. */
216	export function OverlayInstanceProvider({
217	  value,
218	  children,
219	}: React.PropsWithChildren<{ value: OverlayBridgeContextValue }>) {
220	  return (
221	    <OverlayBridgeContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</OverlayBridgeContext.Provider>
222	  );
223	}
224	
225	/** Returns the current overlay handle for bridge-aware descendants. */
226	export function useCurrentOverlayHandle(): OverlayHandle {
227	  return useOverlayBridgeContextValue().handle;
228	}
229	
230	/** Returns a ref callback that registers the popup or backdrop with the overlay stack. */
231	export function useOverlayElementRegistration(
232	  kind: OverlayElementKind,
233	): React.RefCallback<HTMLElement> {
234	  const { registerElement } = useOverlayBridgeContextValue();
235	
236	  return React.useCallback(
237	    (element: HTMLElement | null) => {
238	      registerElement(kind, element);
239	    },
240	    [kind, registerElement],
241	  );
242	}
243	
244	/** Subscribes to whether the current overlay is the topmost stack entry. */
245	export function useCurrentOverlayTopmost(): boolean {
246	  const handle = useCurrentOverlayHandle();
247	  return useIsOverlayTopmost(handle.id);
248	}
249	
250	/** Subscribes to whether the given element belongs to the current topmost overlay. */
251	export function useCurrentOverlayTopmostForElement(element: Element | null): boolean {
252	  const handle = useCurrentOverlayHandle();
253	  const topmostOverlay = useTopmostOverlayContainingElement(element);
254	  return topmostOverlay?.id === handle.id;
255	}

3	import { createStore } from 'zustand/vanilla';
4	
5	export interface OverlayStackOpenInput {
6	  id: string;
7	  type: string;
8	  modal?: boolean;
9	  restoreFocusTo?: HTMLElement | null;
10	  elements?: Iterable<Element | null | undefined>;
11	}
12	
13	export interface OverlayStackUpdateInput {
14	  type?: string;
15	  modal?: boolean;
16	  restoreFocusTo?: HTMLElement | null;
17	  elements?: Iterable<Element | null | undefined>;
18	}
19	
20	export interface OverlayStackEntry {
21	  id: string;
22	  type: string;
23	  modal: boolean;
24	  restoreFocusTo: HTMLElement | null;
25	  elements: readonly HTMLElement[];
26	}
27	
28	export interface OverlayStackState {
29	  overlays: OverlayStackEntry[];
30	  /** Registers or re-registers an overlay at the top of the ordering stack. */
31	  pushOverlay: (input: OverlayStackOpenInput) => void;
32	  /** Updates a registered overlay without changing its ordering position. */
33	  updateOverlay: (id: string, patch: OverlayStackUpdateInput) => void;
34	  /** Unregisters an overlay and applies any pending cleanup side effects. */
35	  popOverlay: (id: string) => void;
36	  /** Returns the current topmost overlay, regardless of modality. */
37	  getTopOverlay: () => OverlayStackEntry | null;
38	  /** Returns the current topmost modal overlay, if one is registered. */
39	  getTopModalOverlay: () => OverlayStackEntry | null;
40	  /** Returns true when the given overlay is the topmost registered overlay. */
41	  isOverlayTopmost: (id: string) => boolean;
42	  /** Returns true when the given overlay is the topmost modal overlay. */
43	  isTopmostModalOverlay: (id: string) => boolean;
44	  /** Returns the overlay's 1-based stack layer, or null when not registered. */
45	  getOverlayLayer: (id: string) => number | null;
46	  /** Returns true when the element is the overlay or a descendant of any tracked overlay element. */
47	  overlayContainsElement: (id: string, element: Element | null) => boolean;
48	  /** Returns true when any overlay contains the given element. */
49	  isElementWithinAnyOverlay: (element: Element | null) => boolean;
50	  /** Returns the topmost overlay containing the given element, if one exists. */
51	  getTopmostOverlayContainingElement: (element: Element | null) => OverlayStackEntry | null;
52	}
53	
54	type OverlayStackSnapshot = Pick<OverlayStackState, 'overlays'>;
55	
56	let bodyPointerEventsSnapshot: string | null = null;
57	
58	function isBrowser(): boolean {
59	  return typeof document !== 'undefined';
60	}
61	
62	function isHTMLElement(value: unknown): value is HTMLElement {
63	  return value instanceof HTMLElement;
64	}
65	
66	function normalizeElements(
67	  elements?: Iterable<Element | null | undefined>,
68	): readonly HTMLElement[] {
69	  if (!elements) {
70	    return [];
71	  }
72	
73	  const nextElements: HTMLElement[] = [];
74	  for (const element of elements) {
75	    if (!isHTMLElement(element) || nextElements.includes(element)) {
76	      continue;
77	    }
78	    nextElements.push(element);
79	  }
80	
81	  return nextElements;
82	}
83	
84	function getTopOverlay(overlays: readonly OverlayStackEntry[]): OverlayStackEntry | null {
85	  return overlays.at(-1) ?? null;
86	}
87	
88	function isOverlayActuallyOpen(overlay: OverlayStackEntry): boolean {
89	  if (overlay.elements.length === 0) {
90	    return false;
91	  }
92	  return overlay.elements.some((element) => {
93	    const state = element.getAttribute('data-state');
94	    if (state === 'closed') return false;
95	    if (element.hasAttribute('data-open')) return true;
96	    if (state === 'open') return true;
97	    // Fallback for overlays without base-ui open/close state markers:
98	    // treat as open only when rendered with non-zero box.
99	    return element.offsetParent !== null || element.getClientRects().length > 0;
100	  });
101	}
102	
103	function getTopModalOverlay(overlays: readonly OverlayStackEntry[]): OverlayStackEntry | null {
104	  for (let index = overlays.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
105	    const overlay = overlays[index];
106	    if (overlay.modal && isOverlayActuallyOpen(overlay)) {
107	      return overlay;
108	    }
109	  }
110	
111	  return null;
112	}
113	
114	function canRestoreFocusTo(element: HTMLElement | null): element is HTMLElement {
115	  return isHTMLElement(element) && element.isConnected && typeof element.focus === 'function';
116	}
117	
118	function restoreFocus(element: HTMLElement | null): void {
119	  if (!canRestoreFocusTo(element)) {
120	    return;
121	  }
122	
123	  queueMicrotask(() => {
124	    if (!canRestoreFocusTo(element)) {
125	      return;
126	    }
127	
128	    try {
129	      element.focus({ preventScroll: true });
130	    } catch {
131	      element.focus();
132	    }
133	  });
134	}
135	
136	function syncBodyPointerEvents(
137	  previousOverlays: readonly OverlayStackEntry[],
138	  nextOverlays: readonly OverlayStackEntry[],
139	): void {
140	  if (!isBrowser()) {
141	    return;
142	  }
143	
144	  const previousTopModal = getTopModalOverlay(previousOverlays);
145	  const nextTopModal = getTopModalOverlay(nextOverlays);
146	
147	  if (!previousTopModal && nextTopModal) {
148	    bodyPointerEventsSnapshot = document.body.style.pointerEvents;
149	    document.body.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
150	    console.warn('[OverlayStack] body pointer-events LOCKED (none)', {
151	      modalId: nextTopModal.id,
152	      modalType: nextTopModal.type,
153	      totalOverlays: nextOverlays.length,
154	      snapshot: bodyPointerEventsSnapshot,
155	    });
156	    return;
157	  }
158	
159	  if (previousTopModal && !nextTopModal) {
160	    document.body.style.pointerEvents = bodyPointerEventsSnapshot ?? '';
161	    console.log('[OverlayStack] body pointer-events RELEASED', {
162	      prevModalId: previousTopModal.id,
163	      restoredTo: bodyPointerEventsSnapshot ?? '(empty)',
164	      totalOverlays: nextOverlays.length,
165	    });
166	    bodyPointerEventsSnapshot = null;
167	    return;
168	  }
169	
170	  if (nextTopModal) {
171	    document.body.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
172	    console.log('[OverlayStack] body stays LOCKED (modal swap)', {
173	      modalId: nextTopModal.id,
174	      modalType: nextTopModal.type,
175	    });
176	  }
177	}
178	
179	function createOverlayEntry(input: OverlayStackOpenInput): OverlayStackEntry {
180	  return {
181	    id: input.id,
182	    type: input.type,
183	    modal: input.modal ?? false,
184	    restoreFocusTo: input.restoreFocusTo ?? null,
185	    elements: normalizeElements(input.elements),
186	  };
187	}
188	
189	function containsElement(
190	  overlay: OverlayStackEntry | undefined,
191	  element: Element | null,
192	): boolean {
193	  if (!overlay || !element) {
194	    return false;
195	  }
196	
197	  return overlay.elements.some((candidate) => candidate === element || candidate.contains(element));
198	}
199	
200	const overlayStackStore = createStore<OverlayStackState>((set, get) => ({
201	  overlays: [],
202	
203	  pushOverlay: (input) => {
204	    console.log('[OverlayStack] PUSH', { id: input.id, type: input.type, modal: input.modal, stackSizeBefore: get().overlays.length });
205	    const nextEntry = createOverlayEntry(input);
206	    set((state) => {
207	      const nextOverlays = [
208	        ...state.overlays.filter((overlay) => overlay.id !== input.id),
209	        nextEntry,
210	      ];
211	      syncBodyPointerEvents(state.overlays, nextOverlays);
212	      return { overlays: nextOverlays };
213	    });
214	  },
215	
216	  updateOverlay: (id, patch) => {
217	    set((state) => {
218	      const index = state.overlays.findIndex((overlay) => overlay.id === id);
219	      if (index === -1) {
220	        return state;
221	      }
222	
223	      const current = state.overlays[index];
224	      const nextOverlay: OverlayStackEntry = {
225	        ...current,
226	        ...(patch.type === undefined ? null : { type: patch.type }),
227	        ...(patch.modal === undefined ? null : { modal: patch.modal }),
228	        ...(patch.restoreFocusTo === undefined
229	          ? null
230	          : { restoreFocusTo: patch.restoreFocusTo }),
231	        ...(patch.elements === undefined
232	          ? null
233	          : { elements: normalizeElements(patch.elements) }),
234	      };
235	      const nextOverlays = [...state.overlays];
236	      nextOverlays[index] = nextOverlay;
237	      syncBodyPointerEvents(state.overlays, nextOverlays);
238	      return { overlays: nextOverlays };
239	    });
240	  },
241	
242	  popOverlay: (id) => {
243	    const previousOverlays = get().overlays;
244	    const removedOverlay = previousOverlays.find((overlay) => overlay.id === id);
245	    if (!removedOverlay) {
246	      console.log('[OverlayStack] POP skipped (id not found)', { id });
247	      return;
248	    }
249	    console.log('[OverlayStack] POP', { id, type: removedOverlay.type, modal: removedOverlay.modal, stackSizeBefore: previousOverlays.length });
250	
251	    const previousTopModal = getTopModalOverlay(previousOverlays);
252	    const nextOverlays = previousOverlays.filter((overlay) => overlay.id !== id);
253	
254	    set(() => {
255	      syncBodyPointerEvents(previousOverlays, nextOverlays);
256	      return { overlays: nextOverlays };
257	    });
258	
259	    if (previousTopModal?.id === id && removedOverlay.modal) {
260	      restoreFocus(removedOverlay.restoreFocusTo);
261	    }
262	  },
263	
264	  getTopOverlay: () => getTopOverlay(get().overlays),
265	
266	  getTopModalOverlay: () => getTopModalOverlay(get().overlays),
267	
268	  isOverlayTopmost: (id) => getTopOverlay(get().overlays)?.id === id,
269	
270	  isTopmostModalOverlay: (id) => getTopModalOverlay(get().overlays)?.id === id,
271	
272	  getOverlayLayer: (id) => {
273	    const index = get().overlays.findIndex((overlay) => overlay.id === id);
274	    return index === -1 ? null : index + 1;
275	  },
276	
277	  overlayContainsElement: (id, element) => {
278	    return containsElement(
279	      get().overlays.find((overlay) => overlay.id === id),
280	      element,
281	    );
282	  },
283	
284	  isElementWithinAnyOverlay: (element) => {
285	    return get().overlays.some((overlay) => containsElement(overlay, element));
286	  },
287	
288	  getTopmostOverlayContainingElement: (element) => {
289	    const { overlays } = get();
290	    for (let index = overlays.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
291	      const overlay = overlays[index];
292	      if (containsElement(overlay, element)) {
293	        return overlay;
294	      }
295	    }
296	
297	    return null;
298	  },
299	}));
300	
301	/** Returns the vanilla store API for imperative overlay registration and queries. */
302	export function useOverlayStackApi() {
303	  return overlayStackStore;
304	}
305	
306	function useOverlayStack<T>(
307	  selector: (state: OverlayStackState) => T,
308	  equalityFn?: (left: T, right: T) => boolean,
309	): T {
310	  return useStoreWithEqualityFn(overlayStackStore, selector, equalityFn);
311	}
312	
313	/** Subscribes to the current ordered overlay snapshot. */
314	export function useOverlayStackState(): OverlayStackSnapshot {
315	  return useOverlayStack((state) => ({ overlays: state.overlays }), shallow);
316	}
317	
318	/** Subscribes to the current topmost overlay, regardless of modality. */
319	export function useTopOverlay(): OverlayStackEntry | null {
320	  return useOverlayStack((state) => state.getTopOverlay());
321	}
322	
323	/** Subscribes to the current topmost modal overlay. */
324	export function useTopModalOverlay(): OverlayStackEntry | null {
325	  return useOverlayStack((state) => state.getTopModalOverlay());
326	}
327	
328	/** Subscribes to the overlay's 1-based stack layer. */
329	export function useOverlayLayer(id: string): number | null {
330	  return useOverlayStack((state) => state.getOverlayLayer(id));
331	}
332	
333	/** Subscribes to whether the overlay is the current topmost entry. */
334	export function useIsOverlayTopmost(id: string): boolean {
335	  return useOverlayStack((state) => state.isOverlayTopmost(id));
336	}
337	
338	/** Subscribes to whether the overlay is the current topmost modal entry. */
339	export function useIsTopmostModalOverlay(id: string): boolean {
340	  return useOverlayStack((state) => state.isTopmostModalOverlay(id));
341	}
342	
343	/** Subscribes to the topmost overlay that currently owns the provided element. */
344	export function useTopmostOverlayContainingElement(
345	  element: Element | null,
346	): OverlayStackEntry | null {
347	  return useOverlayStack((state) => state.getTopmostOverlayContainingElement(element));
348	}
349	
350	/** Resets overlay ordering and global side effects for isolated tests. */
351	export function __resetOverlayStackForTests(): void {
352	  overlayStackStore.setState({ overlays: [] });
353	  if (isBrowser()) {
354	    document.body.style.pointerEvents = '';
355	  }
356	  bodyPointerEventsSnapshot = null;
357	}

1	import * as React from 'react';
2	
3	const OVERLAY_BASE_Z_INDEX = 1000;
4	const OVERLAY_LAYER_STEP = 10;
5	
6	const OVERLAY_LAYER_SLOT_OFFSET = {
7	  backdrop: 0,
8	  positioner: 1,
9	  popup: 2,
10	} as const;
11	
12	type OverlayLayerSlot = keyof typeof OVERLAY_LAYER_SLOT_OFFSET;
13	
14	export function composeRefs<T>(
15	  ...refs: Array<React.Ref<T> | undefined | null>
16	): React.RefCallback<T> {
17	  return (node) => {
18	    refs.forEach((ref) => {
19	      if (typeof ref === 'function') {
20	        ref(node);
21	      } else if (ref) {
22	        (ref as React.MutableRefObject<T | null>).current = node;
23	      }
24	    });
25	  };
26	}
27	
28	export function getOverlayLayerStyle(
29	  layer: number | null,
30	  slot: OverlayLayerSlot,
31	  style?: React.CSSProperties,
32	): React.CSSProperties {
33	  const resolvedLayer = Math.max(layer ?? 1, 1);
34	
35	  return {
36	    ...(style ?? {}),
37	    zIndex:
38	      OVERLAY_BASE_Z_INDEX +
39	      resolvedLayer * OVERLAY_LAYER_STEP +
40	      OVERLAY_LAYER_SLOT_OFFSET[slot],
41	  };
42	}
43

9	export function useSettingsModal() {

11	  const [settingsInitialTab, setSettingsInitialTab] = useState<string | undefined>(undefined);
12	  const [settingsCreditsTab, setSettingsCreditsTab] = useState<SettingsCreditsTab | undefined>(undefined);
13	  const location = useLocation();
14	
15	  const handleOpenSettings = useCallback((initialTab?: string, creditsTab?: SettingsCreditsTab) => {
16	    setSettingsInitialTab(initialTab);
17	    setSettingsCreditsTab(creditsTab);
18	    setIsSettingsModalOpen(true);
19	  }, []);
20	
21	  useSettingsRouteIntent(location.state, useCallback(({ settingsTab, creditsTab }) => {
22	    handleOpenSettings(settingsTab, creditsTab);
23	  }, [handleOpenSettings]));
24	
25	  useSettingsEventIntent(useCallback(({ settingsTab }) => {
26	    handleOpenSettings(settingsTab);
27	  }, [handleOpenSettings]));
28	
29	  return {
30	    isSettingsModalOpen,
31	    setIsSettingsModalOpen,
32	    settingsInitialTab,
33	    settingsCreditsTab,
34	    handleOpenSettings,
35	  };
36	}

## Bug Debugging Report: Modal Click Disabled Issue

Found the culprit and the flow. Here are the key files to instrument with logs:

### 1. **Settings Modal Component**
- **File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/components/SettingsModal/SettingsModal.tsx` (lines 134–265)
- **Details:** Opens via `Dialog` component from `/src/shared/components/ui/dialog.tsx`

### 2. **Modal Infrastructure: Base UI Dialog**
- **Library:** Using `@base-ui/react` Dialog (not shadcn or Radix)
- **Dialog implementation:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/components/ui/overlay/dialog.tsx` (lines 1–163)
- Base UI Dialog includes `DialogPrimitive.Root`, `DialogPrimitive.Popup`, `DialogPrimitive.Backdrop`

### 3. **THE CULPRIT: Body pointer-events manipulation**
- **File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/state/overlayStack.ts` (lines 136–177)
- **Function:** `syncBodyPointerEvents()` at lines 136–177
- **Suspicious code:**
  - Line 149: `document.body.style.pointerEvents = 'none'` — locks clicks when modal opens
  - Line 160: `document.body.style.pointerEvents = bodyPointerEventsSnapshot ?? ''` — should release on close
  - Lines 170–176: Handles modal swap (non-standard modals may not exit cleanly)

### 4. **Overlay Stack State Management**
- **File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/state/overlayStack.ts` (entire file)
- **Key functions:**
  - `pushOverlay()` at line 203 — registers overlay and locks body
  - `popOverlay()` at line 242 — should unregister and unlock body
  - `getTopModalOverlay()` at line 103 — determines if modal is "actually open" (checks `data-state`, `data-open`, `offsetParent`)

### 5. **Overlay Bridge (Registration Logic)**
- **File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/components/ui/overlay/overlayBridge.tsx`
- **Lines 136–181:** `syncRegistration()` — syncs popup/backdrop elements to overlay stack
- **Issue:** If popup/backdrop elements don't properly unregister on close, `popOverlay()` won't be called

### 6. **Top-Level Mounting**
- **File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/app/Layout.tsx` (lines 141–146)
- SettingsModal mounted at root level with controlled `isOpen` state

### Likely Bug Path:
1. Settings modal opens → backdrop/popup register → `syncBodyPointerEvents()` sets `document.body.style.pointerEvents = 'none'`
2. Click outside modal → modal closes → **backdrop/popup fails to unregister OR `popOverlay()` not called**
3. Body stays with `pointerEvents = 'none'` → all clicks disabled

### Where to Add Logs:
- **overlayStack.ts line 149:** Log when body locks
- **overlayStack.ts line 160:** Log when body should unlock (but likely doesn't fire)
- **overlayStack.ts line 242:** Log `popOverlay()` calls
- **overlayBridge.tsx line 141:** Log when overlay is removed from stack
- **overlayStack.ts line 88–101:** Check `isOverlayActuallyOpen()` — may be returning `true` when modal is closed

The issue is the body never gets `pointerEvents` restored because `popOverlay()` likely isn't firing when it should.

278	  ]);
279	
280	  const controlsPanelContent = (
281	    <ImageLightboxControlsPanel
282	      media={props.media}
283	      features={props.features}
284	      env={env}
285	      editModel={editModel}
286	      showPanel={showPanel}
287	      panelVariant={panelVariant}
288	      panelTaskId={panelTaskId}
289	    />
290	  );
291	
292	  const layoutProps = useMemo(() => ({
293	    showPanel,
294	    shouldShowSidePanel: sharedState.layout.shouldShowSidePanel,
295	    effectiveTasksPaneOpen: env.effectiveTasksPaneOpen,
296	    effectiveTasksPaneWidth: env.effectiveTasksPaneWidth,
297	    workflowBar,
298	    buttonGroups: {
299	      bottomLeft: sharedState.buttonGroupProps.bottomLeft,
300	      bottomRight: sharedState.buttonGroupProps.bottomRight,
301	      topRight: {
302	        ...sharedState.buttonGroupProps.topRight,
303	        handleDownload, handleDelete,
304	      },
305	    },
306	    adjacentSegments,
307	  } satisfies LightboxLayoutProps), [
308	    showPanel, sharedState.layout.shouldShowSidePanel,
309	    env.effectiveTasksPaneOpen, env.effectiveTasksPaneWidth, workflowBar,
310	    sharedState.buttonGroupProps.bottomLeft, sharedState.buttonGroupProps.bottomRight,
311	    sharedState.buttonGroupProps.topRight, handleDownload, handleDelete, adjacentSegments,
312	  ]);
313	
314	  return {
315	    lightboxStateValue, layoutProps, controlsPanelContent,
316	    needsFullscreenLayout, needsTasksPaneOffset,
317	    accessibilityTitle: `Image Lightbox - ${media?.id?.substring(0, 8)}`,
318	    accessibilityDescription: 'View and interact with image in full screen. Use arrow keys to navigate, Escape to close.',
319	  };
320	}
321	
322	export const ImageLightbox: React.FC<ImageLightboxProps> = (props) => {
323	  useRenderBudget('ImageLightbox', 5);
324	  const renderCountRef = useRef(0);
325	  renderCountRef.current += 1;
326	  const instanceRef = useRef<string>();
327	  if (!instanceRef.current) {
328	    instanceRef.current = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6);
329	  }
330	  const prevPropsRef = useRef<unknown>();
331	  const changed: string[] = [];
332	  if (prevPropsRef.current) {
333	    const prev = prevPropsRef.current as Record<string, unknown>;
334	    for (const k of Object.keys(props) as Array<keyof typeof props>) {
335	      if (prev[k as string] !== (props as Record<string, unknown>)[k as string]) changed.push(k as string);
336	    }
337	  }
338	  prevPropsRef.current = props;
339	  console.log(
340	    `[ImageLightbox] r${renderCountRef.current} mediaId=${props.media?.id?.slice(0, 8)} changedProps=[${changed.join(', ')}]`,
341	  );
342	
343	  const { onClose: onRequestClose } = props;
344	  const env = useImageLightboxEnvironment(props);
345	  const prevEnvRef = useRef<Record<string, unknown> | null>(null);
346	  const envChanged: string[] = [];
347	  if (prevEnvRef.current) {
348	    for (const k of Object.keys(env) as Array<keyof typeof env>) {
349	      if (prevEnvRef.current[k as string] !== (env as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)[k as string]) {
350	        envChanged.push(k as string);
351	      }
352	    }
353	  }
354	  prevEnvRef.current = env as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;
355	  if (envChanged.length > 0) {
356	    console.log(`[ImageLightbox] env changed r${renderCountRef.current}: ${envChanged.join(', ')}`);
357	  }
358	  const flushTextFields = env.editSettingsPersistence.flushTextFields;
359	  const handleClose = useCallback(() => {
360	    void flushTextFields();
361	    onRequestClose();
362	  }, [flushTextFields, onRequestClose]);
363	  const propsWithFlushOnClose = useMemo(
364	    () => ({ ...props, onClose: handleClose }),
365	    [props, handleClose],
366	  );
367	  const sharedModel = useImageLightboxSharedState(propsWithFlushOnClose, env);
368	  const editModel = useImageLightboxEditing(propsWithFlushOnClose, env, sharedModel);
369	  const renderModel = useImageLightboxRenderModel(propsWithFlushOnClose, env, sharedModel, editModel);
370	  const overlayViewport: OverlayViewportConstraints = {
371	    tasksPaneOpen: env.effectiveTasksPaneOpen,
372	    tasksPaneWidth: env.effectiveTasksPaneWidth,
373	    tasksPaneLocked: env.isTasksPaneLocked,
374	    isTabletOrLarger: sharedModel.sharedState.layout.isTabletOrLarger,
375	    needsFullscreenLayout: renderModel.needsFullscreenLayout,
376	    needsTasksPaneOffset: renderModel.needsTasksPaneOffset,
377	  };
378	
379	  return (
380	    <LightboxProviders stateValue={renderModel.lightboxStateValue}>
381	      <ImageEditProvider value={editModel.editOrchestrator.imageEditValue}>
382	        <LightboxShell
383	          onClose={handleClose}
384	          hasCanvasOverlay={editModel.editOrchestrator.isInpaintMode}
385	          isRepositionMode={editModel.editOrchestrator.isInpaintMode && editModel.editOrchestrator.editMode === 'reposition'}
386	          isMobile={env.isMobile}
387	          overlayViewport={overlayViewport}
388	          contentRef={env.contentRef}
389	          accessibilityTitle={renderModel.accessibilityTitle}
390	          accessibilityDescription={renderModel.accessibilityDescription}
391	        >
392	          <LightboxLayout {...renderModel.layoutProps} controlsPanelContent={renderModel.controlsPanelContent} />
393	        </LightboxShell>
394	      </ImageEditProvider>
395	    </LightboxProviders>
396	  );
397	};

1	/**
2	 * LightboxShell - Dialog/Overlay container for MediaLightbox
3	 *
4	 * Encapsulates all the complex event handling for the lightbox modal:
5	 * - Dialog root/portal/overlay
6	 * - Pointer/touch/click event handling with z-index awareness
7	 * - Body scroll locking
8	 * - Accessibility elements
9	 *
10	 * Note: Tasks pane controls are handled by the existing PaneControlTab from TasksPane,
11	 * which is visible above the lightbox at z-[100001]. The overlay adjusts its size
12	 * to account for the pane when it's open or locked.
13	 *
14	 * This allows the main MediaLightbox to focus on content orchestration.
15	 */
16	
17	import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
18	import { cn } from '@/shared/components/ui/contracts/cn';
19	import {
20	  LightboxDialog,
21	  LightboxDialogBackdrop,
22	  LightboxDialogDescription,
23	  LightboxDialogPopup,
24	  LightboxDialogPortal,
25	  LightboxDialogTitle,
26	} from '@/shared/components/ui/overlay';
27	import { useLightboxShellInteractionHandlers } from '@/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useLightboxShellInteractionHandlers';
28	import { useLightboxViewportLock } from '@/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useLightboxViewportLock';
29	import { useLightboxPaneLayout } from '@/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useLightboxPaneLayout';
30	import type { OverlayViewportConstraints } from '@/shared/lib/layout/overlayViewportConstraints';
31	import { useRenderBudget } from '@/shared/dev/useRenderBudget';
32	
33	interface LightboxShellProps {
34	  children: React.ReactNode;
35	  onClose: () => void;
36	
37	  // Edit mode
38	  /** Canvas/brush edit overlay is active (inpaint, annotate, reposition, text).
39	   *  Used to allow canvas touch events to pass through. */
40	  hasCanvasOverlay: boolean;
41	  /** Reposition (move) mode — the only edit mode that blocks background click-to-close
42	   *  (because dragging could cause accidental closes). */
43	  isRepositionMode: boolean;
44	
45	  // Responsive
46	  isMobile: boolean;
47	  isTabletOrLarger: boolean;
48	
49	  // Layout constraints from the layout subsystem.
50	  overlayViewport: OverlayViewportConstraints;
51	
52	  // Ref for content
53	  contentRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>;
54	
55	  // Accessibility
56	  accessibilityTitle: string;
57	  accessibilityDescription: string;
58	}
59	
60	export const LightboxShell: React.FC<LightboxShellProps> = ({
61	  children,
62	  onClose,
63	  hasCanvasOverlay,
64	  isRepositionMode,
65	  isMobile,
66	  isTabletOrLarger,
67	  overlayViewport,
68	  contentRef,
69	  accessibilityTitle,
70	  accessibilityDescription,
71	}) => {
72	  useRenderBudget('LightboxShell', 5);
73	  const isActuallyModal = isMobile && !isTabletOrLarger;
74	  useLightboxViewportLock({ isActuallyModal });
75	
76	  const {
77	    handleOverlayPointerDown,
78	    handleOverlayPointerUp,
79	    handleOverlayClick,
80	    handleBgPointerDownCapture,
81	    handleBgClickCapture,
82	    handleContentPointerDown,
83	    handleContentClick,
84	    handleTouchEvent,
85	    handleTouchCancel,
86	  } = useLightboxShellInteractionHandlers({
87	    hasCanvasOverlay,
88	    isRepositionMode,
89	    isMobile,
90	    onClose,
91	    popupRef: contentRef,
92	  });
93	
94	  // Focus the content element on mount
95	  useEffect(() => {
96	    contentRef.current?.focus();
97	  }, [contentRef]);
98	
99	  const { overlayStyle, contentStyle } = useLightboxPaneLayout({
100	    overlayViewport,
101	  });
102	
103	  return (
104	    <LightboxDialog
105	      open={true}
106	      modal={isActuallyModal}
107	      disablePointerDismissal
108	      onOpenChange={(_open, eventDetails) => {
109	        // Prevent Base UI from updating internal state (which corrupts controlled open={true})
110	        // and from calling stopPropagation on the native event (which blocks our custom handlers).
111	        // Our own handlers in useLightboxNavigation (Escape) and handleOverlayClick (outside press)
112	        // manage all closing logic.
113	        eventDetails.cancel();
114	        eventDetails.allowPropagation();
115	      }}
116	    >
117	      <LightboxDialogPortal>
118	        <LightboxDialogBackdrop
119	          data-dialog-backdrop
120	          className={cn(
121	            "fixed bg-black/80 p-0 border-none shadow-none",
122	            "data-[open]:animate-in data-[ending-style]:animate-out data-[ending-style]:fade-out-0 data-[open]:fade-in-0",
123	            !isMobile && "duration-200"
124	          )}
125	          onPointerDown={handleOverlayPointerDown}
126	          onPointerUp={handleOverlayPointerUp}
127	          onClick={handleOverlayClick}
128	          onTouchStart={handleTouchEvent}
129	          onTouchMove={handleTouchEvent}
130	          onTouchEnd={handleTouchEvent}
131	          onTouchCancel={handleTouchCancel}
132	          style={{
133	            ...overlayStyle,
134	          }}
135	        />
136	
137	        {/* Task pane handle removed - the existing PaneControlTab from TasksPane
138	            is now visible above the lightbox and handles all pane controls.
139	            The overlay correctly accounts for the pane via shouldAccountForTasksPane. */}
140	
141	        <LightboxDialogPopup
142	          ref={contentRef}
143	          data-lightbox-popup
144	          tabIndex={-1}
145	          onPointerDownCapture={handleBgPointerDownCapture}
146	          onClickCapture={handleBgClickCapture}
147	          onPointerDown={handleContentPointerDown}
148	          onClick={handleContentClick}
149	          onTouchStart={handleTouchEvent}
150	          onTouchMove={handleTouchEvent}
151	          onTouchEnd={handleTouchEvent}
152	          onTouchCancel={handleTouchCancel}
153	          className={cn(
154	            "fixed",
155	            isMobile
156	              ? ""
157	              : "duration-200 data-[open]:animate-in data-[ending-style]:animate-out data-[ending-style]:fade-out-0 data-[open]:fade-in-0 data-[ending-style]:zoom-out-95 data-[open]:zoom-in-95",
158	            "p-0 border-none bg-transparent shadow-none",
159	            overlayViewport.needsFullscreenLayout
160	              ? "inset-0 w-full h-full"
161	              : "left-[50%] top-[50%] translate-x-[-50%] translate-y-[-50%] w-auto h-auto data-[ending-style]:slide-out-to-left-1/2 data-[ending-style]:slide-out-to-top-[48%] data-[open]:slide-in-from-left-1/2 data-[open]:slide-in-from-top-[48%]"
162	          )}
163	          style={{
164	            ...contentStyle,
165	          }}
166	        >
167	          {/* Accessibility: Hidden dialog title for screen readers */}
168	          <LightboxDialogTitle className="sr-only">
169	            {accessibilityTitle}
170	          </LightboxDialogTitle>
171	          <LightboxDialogDescription className="sr-only">
172	            {accessibilityDescription}
173	          </LightboxDialogDescription>
174	
175	          {children}
176	        </LightboxDialogPopup>
177	      </LightboxDialogPortal>
178	    </LightboxDialog>
179	  );
180	};

1	import * as React from 'react';
2	import {
3	  useIsOverlayTopmost,
4	  useOverlayStackApi,
5	  useTopmostOverlayContainingElement,
6	} from '@/shared/state/overlayStack';
7	
8	export type OverlayElementKind = 'popup' | 'backdrop';
9	
10	interface OverlayElements {
11	  popup: HTMLElement | null;
12	  backdrop: HTMLElement | null;
13	}
14	
15	export interface OverlayHandle {
16	  id: string;
17	  getType: () => string;
18	  isModal: () => boolean;
19	  getElement: (kind: OverlayElementKind) => HTMLElement | null;
20	  getElements: () => readonly HTMLElement[];
21	  isRegistered: () => boolean;
22	  containsElement: (element: Element | null) => boolean;
23	}
24	
25	export interface UseOverlayBridgeOptions {
26	  id?: string;
27	  type: string;
28	  modal?: boolean;
29	  restoreFocusTo?: HTMLElement | null;
30	}
31	
32	interface OverlayBridgeContextValue {
33	  handle: OverlayHandle;
34	  registerElement: (kind: OverlayElementKind, element: HTMLElement | null) => void;
35	}
36	
37	const OverlayBridgeContext = React.createContext<OverlayBridgeContextValue | null>(null);
38	
39	const OVERLAY_ID_PREFIX = 'overlay';
40	
41	function isHTMLElement(value: unknown): value is HTMLElement {
42	  return value instanceof HTMLElement;
43	}
44	
45	function sanitizeOverlayId(id: string): string {
46	  return id.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, '-');
47	}
48	
49	function setOverlayMetadata(
50	  element: HTMLElement,
51	  handle: OverlayHandle,
52	  kind: OverlayElementKind,
53	): void {
54	  element.dataset.overlayStackId = handle.id;
55	  element.dataset.overlayStackType = handle.getType();
56	  element.dataset.overlayStackKind = kind;
57	}
58	
59	function clearOverlayMetadata(element: HTMLElement): void {
60	  delete element.dataset.overlayStackId;
61	  delete element.dataset.overlayStackType;
62	  delete element.dataset.overlayStackKind;
63	}
64	
65	function getTrackedElements(elements: OverlayElements): readonly HTMLElement[] {
66	  return [elements.popup, elements.backdrop].filter(isHTMLElement);
67	}
68	
69	function getRestoreFocusTarget(
70	  explicitTarget: HTMLElement | null | undefined,
71	  elements: readonly HTMLElement[],
72	): HTMLElement | null {
73	  if (explicitTarget !== undefined) {
74	    return explicitTarget;
75	  }
76	
77	  const activeElement = document.activeElement;
78	  if (!(activeElement instanceof HTMLElement)) {
79	    return null;
80	  }
81	
82	  if (elements.some((element) => element === activeElement || element.contains(activeElement))) {
83	    return null;
84	  }
85	
86	  return activeElement;
87	}
88	
89	function useOverlayBridgeContextValue(): OverlayBridgeContextValue {
90	  const context = React.useContext(OverlayBridgeContext);
91	  if (!context) {
92	    throw new Error('Overlay bridge hooks must be used within an OverlayInstanceProvider.');
93	  }
94	
95	  return context;
96	}
97	
98	/**
99	 * Creates a stable overlay bridge that registers the overlay only while popup/backdrop
100	 * DOM is actually present and keeps the stack entry updated as refs change.
101	 */
102	export function useOverlayBridge(options: UseOverlayBridgeOptions): OverlayBridgeContextValue {
103	  const stackApi = useOverlayStackApi();
104	  const reactId = React.useId();
105	  const overlayId = React.useMemo(
106	    () => sanitizeOverlayId(options.id ?? `${OVERLAY_ID_PREFIX}-${reactId}`),
107	    [options.id, reactId],
108	  );
109	  const elementsRef = React.useRef<OverlayElements>({ popup: null, backdrop: null });
110	  const registeredRef = React.useRef(false);
111	  const typeRef = React.useRef(options.type);
112	  const modalRef = React.useRef(options.modal ?? false);
113	  const restoreFocusToRef = React.useRef<HTMLElement | null | undefined>(options.restoreFocusTo);
114	  const dataStateObserversRef = React.useRef<Map<HTMLElement, MutationObserver>>(new Map());
115	
116	  typeRef.current = options.type;
117	  modalRef.current = options.modal ?? false;
118	  restoreFocusToRef.current = options.restoreFocusTo;
119	
120	  const handleRef = React.useRef<OverlayHandle | null>(null);
121	  if (!handleRef.current) {
122	    handleRef.current = {
123	      id: overlayId,
124	      getType: () => typeRef.current,
125	      isModal: () => modalRef.current,
126	      getElement: (kind) => elementsRef.current[kind],
127	      getElements: () => getTrackedElements(elementsRef.current),
128	      isRegistered: () => registeredRef.current,
129	      containsElement: (element) =>
130	        getTrackedElements(elementsRef.current).some(
131	          (candidate) => candidate === element || candidate.contains(element),
132	        ),
133	    };
134	  }
135	  const handle = handleRef.current;
136	
137	  const syncRegistration = React.useCallback(() => {
138	    const elements = getTrackedElements(elementsRef.current);
139	    const elementSummary = elements.map((el) => ({
140	      kind: el.dataset.overlayStackKind,
141	      dataState: el.getAttribute('data-state'),
142	      hasDataOpen: el.hasAttribute('data-open'),
143	    }));
144	
145	    if (elements.length === 0) {
146	      if (registeredRef.current) {
147	        console.log('[OverlayDiag] syncRegistration → popOverlay (no elements)', { id: handle.id, type: handle.getType() });
148	        stackApi.getState().popOverlay(handle.id);
149	        registeredRef.current = false;
150	      } else {
151	        console.log('[OverlayDiag] syncRegistration no-op (no elements, not registered)', { id: handle.id });
152	      }
153	      return;
154	    }
155	
156	    const payload = {
157	      type: handle.getType(),
158	      modal: handle.isModal(),
159	      restoreFocusTo: getRestoreFocusTarget(restoreFocusToRef.current, elements),
160	      elements,
161	    };
162	
163	    if (!registeredRef.current) {
164	      console.log('[OverlayDiag] syncRegistration → pushOverlay', { id: handle.id, type: handle.getType(), modal: handle.isModal(), elements: elementSummary });
165	      stackApi.getState().pushOverlay({ id: handle.id, ...payload });
166	      registeredRef.current = true;
167	      return;
168	    }
169	
170	    console.log('[OverlayDiag] syncRegistration → updateOverlay', { id: handle.id, type: handle.getType(), elements: elementSummary });
171	    stackApi.getState().updateOverlay(handle.id, payload);
172	  }, [handle, stackApi]);
173	
174	  const registerElement = React.useCallback(
175	    (kind: OverlayElementKind, element: HTMLElement | null) => {
176	      const previous = elementsRef.current[kind];
177	      if (previous === element) {
178	        syncRegistration();
179	        return;
180	      }
181	
182	      if (previous) {
183	        clearOverlayMetadata(previous);
184	        const observer = dataStateObserversRef.current.get(previous);
185	        if (observer) {
186	          observer.disconnect();
187	          dataStateObserversRef.current.delete(previous);
188	        }
189	      }
190	
191	      elementsRef.current[kind] = element;
192	
193	      if (element) {
194	        setOverlayMetadata(element, handle, kind);
195	        const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
196	          console.log('[OverlayDiag] data-state mutation → resync', { id: handle.id, kind, newState: element.getAttribute('data-state') });
197	          syncRegistration();
198	        });
199	        observer.observe(element, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['data-state'] });
200	        dataStateObserversRef.current.set(element, observer);
201	      }
202	
203	      syncRegistration();
204	    },
205	    [handle, syncRegistration],
206	  );
207	
208	  React.useEffect(() => {
209	    syncRegistration();
210	  }, [syncRegistration, options.modal, options.restoreFocusTo, options.type]);
211	
212	  React.useEffect(() => {
213	    return () => {
214	      const elements = elementsRef.current;
215	      console.log('[OverlayDiag] bridge unmount cleanup', {
216	        id: handle.id,
217	        type: handle.getType(),
218	        registered: registeredRef.current,
219	        hadPopup: !!elements.popup,
220	        hadBackdrop: !!elements.backdrop,
221	        popupDataState: elements.popup?.getAttribute('data-state') ?? null,
222	        backdropDataState: elements.backdrop?.getAttribute('data-state') ?? null,
223	      });
224	      if (elements.popup) {
225	        clearOverlayMetadata(elements.popup);
226	      }
227	      if (elements.backdrop) {
228	        clearOverlayMetadata(elements.backdrop);
229	      }
230	      for (const observer of dataStateObserversRef.current.values()) {
231	        observer.disconnect();
232	      }
233	      dataStateObserversRef.current.clear();
234	      if (registeredRef.current) {
235	        stackApi.getState().popOverlay(handle.id);
236	        registeredRef.current = false;
237	      }
238	    };
239	  }, [handle, stackApi]);
240	
241	  return React.useMemo(
242	    () => ({
243	      handle,
244	      registerElement,
245	    }),
246	    [handle, registerElement],
247	  );
248	}
249	
250	/** Provides the current overlay bridge to popup/backdrop descendants. */
251	export function OverlayInstanceProvider({
252	  value,
253	  children,
254	}: React.PropsWithChildren<{ value: OverlayBridgeContextValue }>) {
255	  return (
256	    <OverlayBridgeContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</OverlayBridgeContext.Provider>
257	  );
258	}
259	
260	/** Returns the current overlay handle for bridge-aware descendants. */
261	export function useCurrentOverlayHandle(): OverlayHandle {
262	  return useOverlayBridgeContextValue().handle;
263	}
264	
265	/** Returns a ref callback that registers the popup or backdrop with the overlay stack. */
266	export function useOverlayElementRegistration(
267	  kind: OverlayElementKind,
268	): React.RefCallback<HTMLElement> {
269	  const { registerElement } = useOverlayBridgeContextValue();
270	
271	  return React.useCallback(
272	    (element: HTMLElement | null) => {
273	      registerElement(kind, element);
274	    },
275	    [kind, registerElement],
276	  );
277	}
278	
279	/** Subscribes to whether the current overlay is the topmost stack entry. */
280	export function useCurrentOverlayTopmost(): boolean {
281	  const handle = useCurrentOverlayHandle();
282	  return useIsOverlayTopmost(handle.id);
283	}
284	
285	/** Subscribes to whether the given element belongs to the current topmost overlay. */
286	export function useCurrentOverlayTopmostForElement(element: Element | null): boolean {
287	  const handle = useCurrentOverlayHandle();
288	  const topmostOverlay = useTopmostOverlayContainingElement(element);
289	  return topmostOverlay?.id === handle.id;
290	}

7	const LightboxDialogModalContext = React.createContext(true);

1	import { useRef } from 'react';
2	import {
3	  isFloatingOverlayElement,
4	  shouldAllowTouchThrough,
5	} from '@/shared/lib/interactions/elementPolicy';
6	import { isElementWithinTopmostOverlay } from '@/shared/components/ui/overlay';
7	
8	interface UseLightboxShellInteractionHandlersArgs {
9	  hasCanvasOverlay: boolean;
10	  isRepositionMode: boolean;
11	  isMobile: boolean;
12	  onClose: () => void;
13	  popupRef: React.RefObject<HTMLElement | null>;
14	}
15	
16	export function useLightboxShellInteractionHandlers({
17	  hasCanvasOverlay,
18	  isRepositionMode,
19	  isMobile,
20	  onClose,
21	  popupRef,
22	}: UseLightboxShellInteractionHandlersArgs) {
23	  const pointerDownTargetRef = useRef<EventTarget | null>(null);
24	  const bgPointerDownTargetRef = useRef<EventTarget | null>(null);
25	  const isCurrentLightboxTopmost = () => {
26	    const popup = popupRef.current;
27	    return !popup || isElementWithinTopmostOverlay(popup);
28	  };
29	
30	  const handleOverlayPointerDown = (e: React.PointerEvent) => {
31	    pointerDownTargetRef.current = e.target;
32	
33	    if (!isCurrentLightboxTopmost()) {
34	      return;
35	    }
36	
37	    e.preventDefault();
38	    e.stopPropagation();
39	    if (e.nativeEvent && typeof e.nativeEvent.stopImmediatePropagation === 'function') {
40	      e.nativeEvent.stopImmediatePropagation();
41	    }
42	  };
43	
44	  const handleOverlayPointerUp = (e: React.PointerEvent) => {
45	    if (!isCurrentLightboxTopmost()) {
46	      return;
47	    }
48	
49	    if (!isRepositionMode) {
50	      const clickStartedOnOverlay = pointerDownTargetRef.current === e.currentTarget;
51	      const clickEndedOnOverlay = e.target === e.currentTarget;
52	
53	      if (clickStartedOnOverlay && clickEndedOnOverlay) {
54	        onClose();
55	      }
56	    }
57	
58	    pointerDownTargetRef.current = null;
59	
60	    e.preventDefault();
61	    e.stopPropagation();
62	    if (e.nativeEvent && typeof e.nativeEvent.stopImmediatePropagation === 'function') {
63	      e.nativeEvent.stopImmediatePropagation();
64	    }
65	  };
66	
67	  const handleOverlayClick = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
68	    if (!isCurrentLightboxTopmost()) {
69	      return;
70	    }
71	
72	    if (isRepositionMode) {
73	      pointerDownTargetRef.current = null;
74	      return;
75	    }
76	
77	    const clickStartedOnOverlay = pointerDownTargetRef.current === e.currentTarget;
78	    const clickEndedOnOverlay = e.target === e.currentTarget;
79	
80	    if (clickStartedOnOverlay && clickEndedOnOverlay) {
81	      onClose();
82	    }
83	
84	    pointerDownTargetRef.current = null;
85	  };
86	
87	  const handleBgPointerDownCapture = (e: React.PointerEvent) => {
88	    bgPointerDownTargetRef.current = e.target;
89	  };
90	
91	  const handleBgClickCapture = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
92	    const downTarget = bgPointerDownTargetRef.current as HTMLElement | null;
93	    const upTarget = e.target as HTMLElement;
94	    bgPointerDownTargetRef.current = null;
95	
96	    if (!isCurrentLightboxTopmost()) return;
97	    if (isRepositionMode) return;
98	
99	    if (
100	      downTarget?.hasAttribute?.('data-lightbox-bg') &&
101	      upTarget.hasAttribute?.('data-lightbox-bg')
102	    ) {
103	      e.stopPropagation();
104	      onClose();
105	    }
106	  };
107	
108	  const handleContentPointerDown = (e: React.PointerEvent) => {
109	    const target = e.target as Element;
110	    if (isFloatingOverlayElement(target)) {
111	      return;
112	    }
113	    e.stopPropagation();
114	  };
115	
116	  const handleContentClick = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
117	    e.stopPropagation();
118	  };
119	
120	  const handleTouchEvent = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {
121	    if (shouldAllowTouchThrough(e.target as HTMLElement, { hasCanvasOverlay })) return;
122	    if (isMobile) e.stopPropagation();
123	  };
124	
125	  const handleTouchCancel = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {
126	    if (!isCurrentLightboxTopmost()) return;
127	    e.preventDefault();
128	    e.stopPropagation();
129	    if (e.nativeEvent && typeof e.nativeEvent.stopImmediatePropagation === 'function') {
130	      e.nativeEvent.stopImmediatePropagation();
131	    }
132	  };
133	
134	  return {
135	    handleOverlayPointerDown,
136	    handleOverlayPointerUp,
137	    handleOverlayClick,
138	    handleBgPointerDownCapture,
139	    handleBgClickCapture,
140	    handleContentPointerDown,
141	    handleContentClick,
142	    handleTouchEvent,
143	    handleTouchCancel,
144	  };
145	}
146

1	import { useOverlayStackApi } from '@/shared/state/overlayStack';

3	export const REGISTERED_OVERLAY_SELECTOR = '[data-overlay-stack-id]';
4	
5	export const OVERLAY_POPUP_SELECTOR = [
6	  '[data-overlay-stack-kind="popup"]',
7	  '[data-lightbox-popup]',
8	  '[data-dialog-content]',
9	  '[data-popup]',
10	  '[role="listbox"]',
11	  '[role="menu"]',
12	].join(', ');
13	
14	export const OVERLAY_SURFACE_SELECTOR = [
15	  REGISTERED_OVERLAY_SELECTOR,
16	  '[data-lightbox-popup]',
17	  '[data-dialog-content]',
18	  '[data-dialog-backdrop]',
19	  '[data-popup]',
20	  '[role="listbox"]',
21	  '[role="menu"]',
22	  '[data-side]',
23	].join(', ');

25	function isBrowser(): boolean {
26	  return typeof document !== 'undefined' && typeof window !== 'undefined';
27	}
28	
29	function isHTMLElement(value: unknown): value is HTMLElement {
30	  return value instanceof HTMLElement;
31	}
32	
33	function isVisible(element: HTMLElement): boolean {
34	  const style = window.getComputedStyle(element);
35	  return style.display !== 'none' && style.visibility !== 'hidden';
36	}
37	
38	function getComputedZIndex(element: HTMLElement): number {
39	  const zIndex = Number.parseInt(window.getComputedStyle(element).zIndex || '0', 10);
40	  return Number.isFinite(zIndex) ? zIndex : 0;
41	}
42	
43	function getBestOverlayElement(
44	  popup: HTMLElement | null,
45	  fallbackElements: readonly HTMLElement[],
46	): HTMLElement | null {
47	  if (popup) {
48	    return popup;
49	  }
50	
51	  return fallbackElements.find(isHTMLElement) ?? null;
52	}
53	
54	/** Returns the closest known overlay popup/container ancestor for portal attachment. */
55	export function getClosestOverlayContainer(element: Element | null): HTMLElement | null {
56	  if (!element || !(element instanceof HTMLElement)) {
57	    return null;
58	  }
59	
60	  const markerMatch = element.closest<HTMLElement>(OVERLAY_POPUP_SELECTOR);
61	  if (markerMatch) {
62	    return markerMatch;
63	  }
64	
65	  const overlay = useOverlayStackApi().getState().getTopmostOverlayContainingElement(element);
66	  if (!overlay) {
67	    return null;
68	  }
69	
70	  const popup = overlay.elements.find(
71	    (candidate) => candidate.dataset.overlayStackKind === 'popup',
72	  ) ?? null;
73	  return getBestOverlayElement(popup, overlay.elements);
74	}
75	
76	/** Returns the topmost registered overlay type, or null when no overlay is active. */
77	export function getTopmostKnownOverlayType(): string | null {
78	  return useOverlayStackApi().getState().getTopOverlay()?.type ?? null;
79	}
80	
81	/**
82	 * Returns the topmost *actually-open modal* overlay type, or null when no modal overlay is open.
83	 * Use this (not `getTopmostKnownOverlayType`) for keyboard/focus gating: non-modal overlays
84	 * (tooltips, hover-cards) and stale/unopened modal entries should never suppress key handling
85	 * on the underlying surface.
86	 */
87	export function getTopmostKnownModalOverlayType(): string | null {
88	  return useOverlayStackApi().getState().getTopModalOverlay()?.type ?? null;
89	}
90	
91	/** Returns true when a registered modal overlay is currently active. */
92	export function hasKnownModalOverlay(): boolean {
93	  return useOverlayStackApi().getState().getTopModalOverlay() !== null;
94	}
95	
96	/** Returns true when the target is inside any known overlay surface or popup marker. */
97	export function isElementWithinKnownOverlay(element: Element | null): boolean {
98	  if (!element) {
99	    return false;
100	  }
101	
102	  if (useOverlayStackApi().getState().isElementWithinAnyOverlay(element)) {
103	    return true;
104	  }
105	
106	  return element instanceof HTMLElement && element.closest(OVERLAY_SURFACE_SELECTOR) !== null;
107	}
108	
109	/** Returns the current topmost known overlay surface, preferring registered stack entries. */
110	export function getTopmostKnownOverlaySurface(): HTMLElement | null {
111	  if (!isBrowser()) {
112	    return null;
113	  }
114	
115	  const topOverlay = useOverlayStackApi().getState().getTopOverlay();
116	  if (topOverlay) {
117	    const popup = topOverlay.elements.find(
118	      (candidate) => candidate.dataset.overlayStackKind === 'popup',
119	    ) ?? null;
120	    const bestRegistered = getBestOverlayElement(popup, topOverlay.elements);
121	    if (bestRegistered) {
122	      return bestRegistered;
123	    }
124	  }
125	
126	  const elements = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(OVERLAY_SURFACE_SELECTOR))
127	    .filter(isVisible);
128	
129	  if (elements.length === 0) {
130	    return null;
131	  }
132	
133	  return elements.reduce<HTMLElement | null>((top, element) => {
134	    if (!top) {
135	      return element;
136	    }
137	
138	    return getComputedZIndex(element) >= getComputedZIndex(top) ? element : top;
139	  }, null);
140	}
141	
142	/** Returns true when the given element belongs to the current topmost known overlay. */
143	export function isElementWithinTopmostOverlay(element: Element | null): boolean {
144	  if (!element) {
145	    return false;
146	  }
147	
148	  const stackApi = useOverlayStackApi();
149	  const topOverlay = stackApi.getState().getTopOverlay();
150	  const containingOverlay = stackApi.getState().getTopmostOverlayContainingElement(element);
151	  if (topOverlay && containingOverlay) {
152	    return topOverlay.id === containingOverlay.id;
153	  }
154	
155	  const topmostSurface = getTopmostKnownOverlaySurface();
156	  if (!topmostSurface || !(element instanceof HTMLElement)) {
157	    return false;
158	  }
159	
160	  return topmostSurface === element || topmostSurface.contains(element);
161	}
162	
163	/** Returns true for floating overlay elements used by current marker-based callers during M2. */
164	export function isFloatingOverlayElement(target: Element | null): boolean {
165	  return target instanceof HTMLElement && target.closest(OVERLAY_SURFACE_SELECTOR) !== null;
166	}

22	  type: string;

24	  restoreFocusTo: HTMLElement | null;

28	export interface OverlayStackState {
29	  overlays: OverlayStackEntry[];
30	  /** Registers or re-registers an overlay at the top of the ordering stack. */
31	  pushOverlay: (input: OverlayStackOpenInput) => void;
32	  /** Updates a registered overlay without changing its ordering position. */
33	  updateOverlay: (id: string, patch: OverlayStackUpdateInput) => void;
34	  /** Unregisters an overlay and applies any pending cleanup side effects. */
35	  popOverlay: (id: string) => void;
36	  /** Returns the current topmost overlay, regardless of modality. */
37	  getTopOverlay: () => OverlayStackEntry | null;
38	  /** Returns the current topmost modal overlay, if one is registered. */
39	  getTopModalOverlay: () => OverlayStackEntry | null;
40	  /** Returns true when the given overlay is the topmost registered overlay. */
41	  isOverlayTopmost: (id: string) => boolean;
42	  /** Returns true when the given overlay is the topmost modal overlay. */
43	  isTopmostModalOverlay: (id: string) => boolean;
44	  /** Returns the overlay's 1-based stack layer, or null when not registered. */
45	  getOverlayLayer: (id: string) => number | null;
46	  /** Returns true when the element is the overlay or a descendant of any tracked overlay element. */
47	  overlayContainsElement: (id: string, element: Element | null) => boolean;
48	  /** Returns true when any overlay contains the given element. */
49	  isElementWithinAnyOverlay: (element: Element | null) => boolean;
50	  /** Returns the topmost overlay containing the given element, if one exists. */
51	  getTopmostOverlayContainingElement: (element: Element | null) => OverlayStackEntry | null;

62	function isHTMLElement(value: unknown): value is HTMLElement {
63	  return value instanceof HTMLElement;
64	}

66	function normalizeElements(
67	  elements?: Iterable<Element | null | undefined>,
68	): readonly HTMLElement[] {
69	  if (!elements) {
70	    return [];
71	  }
72	
73	  const nextElements: HTMLElement[] = [];
74	  for (const element of elements) {
75	    if (!isHTMLElement(element) || nextElements.includes(element)) {
76	      continue;
77	    }
78	    nextElements.push(element);
79	  }
80	
81	  return nextElements;

86	}
87	
88	function isOverlayActuallyOpen(overlay: OverlayStackEntry): boolean {
89	  if (overlay.elements.length === 0) {
90	    console.log('[OverlayDiag] isOverlayActuallyOpen=false (no elements)', { id: overlay.id, type: overlay.type });
91	    return false;
92	  }
93	  const perElement = overlay.elements.map((element) => {
94	    const state = element.getAttribute('data-state');
95	    const hasDataOpen = element.hasAttribute('data-open');
96	    const hasEndingStyle = element.hasAttribute('data-ending-style');
97	    let decision: boolean;
98	    let reason: string;
99	    if (state === 'closed') { decision = false; reason = 'data-state=closed'; }
100	    else if (hasDataOpen) { decision = true; reason = 'has data-open'; }
101	    else if (state === 'open') { decision = true; reason = 'data-state=open'; }
102	    else {
103	      const visible = element.offsetParent !== null || element.getClientRects().length > 0;
104	      decision = visible;
105	      reason = `fallback visible=${visible}`;
106	    }
107	    return { tag: element.tagName, kind: element.dataset.overlayStackKind, state, hasDataOpen, hasEndingStyle, decision, reason };
108	  });
109	  const result = perElement.some((e) => e.decision);
110	  console.log('[OverlayDiag] isOverlayActuallyOpen', { id: overlay.id, type: overlay.type, result, perElement });
111	  return result;
112	}
113	
114	function getTopModalOverlay(overlays: readonly OverlayStackEntry[]): OverlayStackEntry | null {
115	  for (let index = overlays.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
116	    const overlay = overlays[index];
117	    if (overlay.modal && isOverlayActuallyOpen(overlay)) {
118	      return overlay;
119	    }
120	  }
121	
122	  return null;
123	}
124	
125	function canRestoreFocusTo(element: HTMLElement | null): element is HTMLElement {
126	  return isHTMLElement(element) && element.isConnected && typeof element.focus === 'function';
127	}
128	
129	function restoreFocus(element: HTMLElement | null): void {
130	  if (!canRestoreFocusTo(element)) {
131	    return;
132	  }
133	
134	  queueMicrotask(() => {
135	    if (!canRestoreFocusTo(element)) {
136	      return;
137	    }
138	
139	    try {
140	      element.focus({ preventScroll: true });
141	    } catch {
142	      element.focus();
143	    }
144	  });
145	}
146	
147	function syncBodyPointerEvents(
148	  previousOverlays: readonly OverlayStackEntry[],
149	  nextOverlays: readonly OverlayStackEntry[],
150	): void {
151	  if (!isBrowser()) {
152	    return;
153	  }
154	
155	  const bodyBefore = document.body.style.pointerEvents;
156	  const previousTopModal = getTopModalOverlay(previousOverlays);
157	  const nextTopModal = getTopModalOverlay(nextOverlays);
158	
159	  console.log('[OverlayDiag] syncBodyPointerEvents start', {
160	    bodyPointerEvents: bodyBefore,
161	    snapshot: bodyPointerEventsSnapshot,
162	    prevOverlayIds: previousOverlays.map((o) => `${o.id}(modal=${o.modal})`),
163	    nextOverlayIds: nextOverlays.map((o) => `${o.id}(modal=${o.modal})`),
164	    previousTopModalId: previousTopModal?.id ?? null,
165	    nextTopModalId: nextTopModal?.id ?? null,
166	  });
167	
168	  const isLocked = bodyPointerEventsSnapshot !== null;
169	
170	  if (nextTopModal && !isLocked) {
171	    bodyPointerEventsSnapshot = document.body.style.pointerEvents;
172	    document.body.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
173	    console.warn('[OverlayStack] body pointer-events LOCKED (none)', {
174	      modalId: nextTopModal.id,
175	      modalType: nextTopModal.type,
176	      totalOverlays: nextOverlays.length,
177	      snapshot: bodyPointerEventsSnapshot,
178	    });
179	    return;
180	  }
181	
182	  if (!nextTopModal && isLocked) {
183	    document.body.style.pointerEvents = bodyPointerEventsSnapshot ?? '';
184	    console.log('[OverlayStack] body pointer-events RELEASED', {
185	      prevModalId: previousTopModal?.id ?? '(dom-already-closed)',
186	      restoredTo: bodyPointerEventsSnapshot ?? '(empty)',
187	      totalOverlays: nextOverlays.length,
188	    });
189	    bodyPointerEventsSnapshot = null;
190	    return;
191	  }
192	
193	  if (nextTopModal) {
194	    document.body.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
195	    console.log('[OverlayStack] body stays LOCKED (modal active)', {
196	      modalId: nextTopModal.id,
197	      modalType: nextTopModal.type,
198	    });
199	    return;
200	  }
201	
202	  console.log('[OverlayDiag] syncBodyPointerEvents no-op', { bodyBefore, snapshot: bodyPointerEventsSnapshot });
203	}
204	
205	function createOverlayEntry(input: OverlayStackOpenInput): OverlayStackEntry {
206	  return {
207	    id: input.id,
208	    type: input.type,
209	    modal: input.modal ?? false,
210	    restoreFocusTo: input.restoreFocusTo ?? null,
211	    elements: normalizeElements(input.elements),
212	  };
213	}
214	
215	function containsElement(
216	  overlay: OverlayStackEntry | undefined,
217	  element: Element | null,
218	): boolean {
219	  if (!overlay || !element) {
220	    return false;
221	  }
222	
223	  return overlay.elements.some((candidate) => candidate === element || candidate.contains(element));
224	}
225	
226	const overlayStackStore = createStore<OverlayStackState>((set, get) => ({
227	  overlays: [],
228	
229	  pushOverlay: (input) => {
230	    console.log('[OverlayStack] PUSH', { id: input.id, type: input.type, modal: input.modal, stackSizeBefore: get().overlays.length });
231	    const nextEntry = createOverlayEntry(input);
232	    set((state) => {
233	      const nextOverlays = [
234	        ...state.overlays.filter((overlay) => overlay.id !== input.id),
235	        nextEntry,
236	      ];
237	      syncBodyPointerEvents(state.overlays, nextOverlays);
238	      return { overlays: nextOverlays };
239	    });
240	  },
241	
242	  updateOverlay: (id, patch) => {
243	    set((state) => {
244	      const index = state.overlays.findIndex((overlay) => overlay.id === id);
245	      if (index === -1) {
246	        return state;
247	      }
248	
249	      const current = state.overlays[index];
250	      const nextOverlay: OverlayStackEntry = {
251	        ...current,
252	        ...(patch.type === undefined ? null : { type: patch.type }),
253	        ...(patch.modal === undefined ? null : { modal: patch.modal }),
254	        ...(patch.restoreFocusTo === undefined
255	          ? null
256	          : { restoreFocusTo: patch.restoreFocusTo }),
257	        ...(patch.elements === undefined
258	          ? null
259	          : { elements: normalizeElements(patch.elements) }),
260	      };
261	      const nextOverlays = [...state.overlays];
262	      nextOverlays[index] = nextOverlay;
263	      syncBodyPointerEvents(state.overlays, nextOverlays);
264	      return { overlays: nextOverlays };
265	    });
266	  },
267	
268	  popOverlay: (id) => {
269	    const previousOverlays = get().overlays;
270	    const removedOverlay = previousOverlays.find((overlay) => overlay.id === id);
271	    if (!removedOverlay) {
272	      console.log('[OverlayStack] POP skipped (id not found)', { id });
273	      return;
274	    }
275	    console.log('[OverlayStack] POP', { id, type: removedOverlay.type, modal: removedOverlay.modal, stackSizeBefore: previousOverlays.length });
276	
277	    const previousTopModal = getTopModalOverlay(previousOverlays);
278	    const nextOverlays = previousOverlays.filter((overlay) => overlay.id !== id);
279	
280	    set(() => {
281	      syncBodyPointerEvents(previousOverlays, nextOverlays);
282	      return { overlays: nextOverlays };
283	    });
284	
285	    if (previousTopModal?.id === id && removedOverlay.modal) {
286	      restoreFocus(removedOverlay.restoreFocusTo);
287	    }
288	  },
289	
290	  getTopOverlay: () => getTopOverlay(get().overlays),
291	
292	  getTopModalOverlay: () => getTopModalOverlay(get().overlays),
293	
294	  isOverlayTopmost: (id) => getTopOverlay(get().overlays)?.id === id,
295	
296	  isTopmostModalOverlay: (id) => getTopModalOverlay(get().overlays)?.id === id,
297	
298	  getOverlayLayer: (id) => {
299	    const index = get().overlays.findIndex((overlay) => overlay.id === id);
300	    return index === -1 ? null : index + 1;
301	  },
302	
303	  overlayContainsElement: (id, element) => {
304	    return containsElement(
305	      get().overlays.find((overlay) => overlay.id === id),
306	      element,
307	    );
308	  },
309	
310	  isElementWithinAnyOverlay: (element) => {
311	    return get().overlays.some((overlay) => containsElement(overlay, element));
312	  },
313	
314	  getTopmostOverlayContainingElement: (element) => {
315	    const { overlays } = get();
316	    for (let index = overlays.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
317	      const overlay = overlays[index];
318	      if (containsElement(overlay, element)) {
319	        return overlay;
320	      }
321	    }
322	
323	    return null;
324	  },
325	}));
326	
327	/** Returns the vanilla store API for imperative overlay registration and queries. */
328	export function useOverlayStackApi() {
329	  return overlayStackStore;
330	}
331	
332	function useOverlayStack<T>(
333	  selector: (state: OverlayStackState) => T,
334	  equalityFn?: (left: T, right: T) => boolean,
335	): T {
336	  return useStoreWithEqualityFn(overlayStackStore, selector, equalityFn);
337	}
338	
339	/** Subscribes to the current ordered overlay snapshot. */

357	}
358

Perfect! Now I have the complete picture. Let me create a concise summary of the bug path:

## Bug Report: Click Inside Lightbox Closes It

**Files & Line Numbers:**

1. **Main dismiss logic:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useLightboxShellInteractionHandlers.ts`
   - Lines 25-28: `isCurrentLightboxTopmost()` function uses `isElementWithinTopmostOverlay(popup)` to check if lightbox is topmost
   - Lines 44-65: `handleOverlayPointerUp()` calls `onClose()` when overlay is topmost AND pointer down/up both hit the overlay (lines 50-55)
   - Lines 67-85: `handleOverlayClick()` mirrors the same logic

2. **Overlay stack check:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/components/ui/overlay/overlayDom.ts`
   - Lines 143-161: `isElementWithinTopmostOverlay()` checks if element is in the topmost registered overlay (lines 149-152 via overlay stack), falls back to DOM z-index check (lines 155-160)

3. **Overlay registration:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/components/ui/overlay/overlayBridge.tsx`
   - Lines 137-172: `syncRegistration()` registers the lightbox's popup AND backdrop as overlay elements
   - Lines 193-203: Elements get `data-overlay-stack-id`, `data-overlay-stack-type`, and `data-overlay-stack-kind` attributes

**The Bug:**
When click events bubble from inside the lightbox content up to the backdrop, `handleOverlayPointerUp()` fires and checks `isCurrentLightboxTopmost()`, which calls `isElementWithinTopmostOverlay(popup)` with the lightbox popup ref. However, the overlay stack now includes **both the popup AND backdrop elements** (line 165 in overlayBridge.tsx). If the stack's `getTopmostOverlayContainingElement()` returns the backdrop instead of the popup for clicks originating inside content, or if the topmost check succeeds but then compares against the wrong overlay entry ID, the lightbox incorrectly closes.

**Root Cause:** The overlay stack stores multiple elements per overlay entry (lines 138-143 in overlayBridge.tsx, lines 26 in overlayStack.ts). The `isElementWithinTopmostOverlay()` check in overlayDom.ts (lines 149-152) validates containment correctly, but recent changes to how the overlay state is registered may have introduced a race condition or element-tracking bug where the backdrop or popup gets registered to a different overlay ID temporarily, causing the topmost check to fail for content clicks.

7	const LightboxDialogModalContext = React.createContext(true);

14	const LightboxDialogLayerContext = React.createContext<LightboxDialogLayerContextValue | null>(null);
15	
16	function useLightboxDialogLayerContext(): LightboxDialogLayerContextValue {
17	  const value = React.useContext(LightboxDialogLayerContext);
18	  if (!value) {
19	    throw new Error('Lightbox dialog overlay primitives must be used within LightboxDialogPortal.');

24	const LightboxDialog = ({
25	  modal = true,
26	  ...props
27	}: React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof DialogPrimitive.Root>) => (
28	  <LightboxDialogModalContext.Provider value={modal}>

33	const LightboxDialogPortal = ({

39	  const layer = useOverlayLayer(bridge.handle.id);

41	    () => ({ layer, registerElement: bridge.registerElement }),
42	    [bridge.registerElement, layer],
43	  );

45	  return (

47	      <OverlayInstanceProvider value={bridge}>
