```
1	import { useRef } from 'react';
2	import {
3	  isFloatingOverlayElement,
4	  shouldAllowTouchThrough,
5	} from '@/shared/lib/interactions/elementPolicy';
6	import { isElementWithinTopmostOverlay } from '@/shared/components/ui/overlay';
7	
8	interface UseLightboxShellInteractionHandlersArgs {
9	  hasCanvasOverlay: boolean;
10	  isRepositionMode: boolean;
11	  isMobile: boolean;
12	  onClose: () => void;
13	  popupRef: React.RefObject<HTMLElement | null>;
14	}
15	
16	export function useLightboxShellInteractionHandlers({
17	  hasCanvasOverlay,
18	  isRepositionMode,
19	  isMobile,
20	  onClose,
21	  popupRef,
22	}: UseLightboxShellInteractionHandlersArgs) {
23	  const pointerDownTargetRef = useRef<EventTarget | null>(null);
24	  const bgPointerDownTargetRef = useRef<EventTarget | null>(null);
25	  const isCurrentLightboxTopmost = () => {
26	    const popup = popupRef.current;
27	    return !popup || isElementWithinTopmostOverlay(popup);
28	  };
29	
30	  const handleOverlayPointerDown = (e: React.PointerEvent) => {
31	    pointerDownTargetRef.current = e.target;
32	
33	    if (!isCurrentLightboxTopmost()) {
34	      return;
35	    }
36	
37	    e.preventDefault();
38	    e.stopPropagation();
39	    if (e.nativeEvent && typeof e.nativeEvent.stopImmediatePropagation === 'function') {
40	      e.nativeEvent.stopImmediatePropagation();
41	    }
42	  };
43	
44	  const handleOverlayPointerUp = (e: React.PointerEvent) => {
45	    if (!isCurrentLightboxTopmost()) {
46	      return;
47	    }
48	
49	    if (!isRepositionMode) {
50	      const clickStartedOnOverlay = pointerDownTargetRef.current === e.currentTarget;
51	      const clickEndedOnOverlay = e.target === e.currentTarget;
52	
53	      if (clickStartedOnOverlay && clickEndedOnOverlay) {
54	        onClose();
55	      }
56	    }
57	
58	    pointerDownTargetRef.current = null;
59	
60	    e.preventDefault();
61	    e.stopPropagation();
62	    if (e.nativeEvent && typeof e.nativeEvent.stopImmediatePropagation === 'function') {
63	      e.nativeEvent.stopImmediatePropagation();
64	    }
65	  };
66	
67	  const handleOverlayClick = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
68	    if (!isCurrentLightboxTopmost()) {
69	      return;
70	    }
71	
72	    if (isRepositionMode) {
73	      pointerDownTargetRef.current = null;
74	      return;
75	    }
76	
77	    const clickStartedOnOverlay = pointerDownTargetRef.current === e.currentTarget;
78	    const clickEndedOnOverlay = e.target === e.currentTarget;
79	
80	    if (clickStartedOnOverlay && clickEndedOnOverlay) {
81	      onClose();
82	    }
83	
84	    pointerDownTargetRef.current = null;
85	  };
86	
87	  const handleBgPointerDownCapture = (e: React.PointerEvent) => {
88	    bgPointerDownTargetRef.current = e.target;
89	  };
90	
91	  const handleBgClickCapture = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
92	    const downTarget = bgPointerDownTargetRef.current as HTMLElement | null;
93	    const upTarget = e.target as HTMLElement;
94	    bgPointerDownTargetRef.current = null;
95	
96	    if (!isCurrentLightboxTopmost()) return;
97	    if (isRepositionMode) return;
98	
99	    if (
100	      downTarget?.hasAttribute?.('data-lightbox-bg') &&
101	      upTarget.hasAttribute?.('data-lightbox-bg')
102	    ) {
103	      e.stopPropagation();
104	      onClose();
105	    }
106	  };
107	
108	  const handleContentPointerDown = (e: React.PointerEvent) => {
109	    const target = e.target as Element;
110	    if (isFloatingOverlayElement(target)) {
111	      return;
112	    }
113	    e.stopPropagation();
114	  };
115	
116	  const handleContentClick = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
117	    e.stopPropagation();
118	  };
119	
120	  const handleTouchEvent = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {
121	    if (shouldAllowTouchThrough(e.target as HTMLElement, { hasCanvasOverlay })) return;
122	    if (isMobile) e.stopPropagation();
123	  };
124	
125	  const handleTouchCancel = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {
126	    if (!isCurrentLightboxTopmost()) return;
127	    e.preventDefault();
128	    e.stopPropagation();
129	    if (e.nativeEvent && typeof e.nativeEvent.stopImmediatePropagation === 'function') {
130	      e.nativeEvent.stopImmediatePropagation();
131	    }
132	  };
133	
134	  return {
135	    handleOverlayPointerDown,
136	    handleOverlayPointerUp,
137	    handleOverlayClick,
138	    handleBgPointerDownCapture,
139	    handleBgClickCapture,
140	    handleContentPointerDown,
141	    handleContentClick,
142	    handleTouchEvent,
143	    handleTouchCancel,
144	  };
145	}
146
```

Perfect. Now I have all the information I need. Let me provide you with a comprehensive analysis:

## Lightbox Component Analysis

**1. Main Lightbox Component**

The primary lightbox UI is in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/components/ui/overlay/lightbox.tsx`. It's a **Base UI Dialog** wrapper (`DialogPrimitive` from `@base-ui/react/dialog`) with these components:
- `LightboxDialog` (Dialog.Root)
- `LightboxDialogPortal` (Dialog.Portal)
- `LightboxDialogBackdrop` (Dialog.Backdrop)
- `LightboxDialogPopup` (Dialog.Popup)

**2. useOverlayBridge Hook**

Yes, it uses `useOverlayBridge({ type: 'lightbox', modal })` in `LightboxDialogPortal` (line 38). The type passed is `'lightbox'`.

**3. Backdrop/Overlay Structure**

The backdrop element is `<DialogPrimitive.Backdrop>` with `data-dialog-backdrop` attribute (not `data-lightbox-bg`). The popup has `data-lightbox-popup` (line 143).

**4. Element Tree & Event Handlers (30-50 lines of JSX)**

```jsx
<LightboxDialogBackdrop
  data-dialog-backdrop
  className={...}
  onPointerDown={handleOverlayPointerDown}      // Stops propagation
  onPointerUp={handleOverlayPointerUp}          // Closes if click started & ended on backdrop
  onClick={handleOverlayClick}                  // Redundant close on direct click
  onTouchStart={handleTouchEvent}
  onTouchMove={handleTouchEvent}
  onTouchEnd={handleTouchEvent}
  onTouchCancel={handleTouchCancel}
  style={{...overlayStyle}}
/>

<LightboxDialogPopup
  ref={contentRef}
  data-lightbox-popup
  tabIndex={-1}
  onPointerDownCapture={handleBgPointerDownCapture}  // Capture: records target
  onClickCapture={handleBgClickCapture}             // Capture: closes if both targets have data-lightbox-bg
  onPointerDown={handleContentPointerDown}          // Bubbling: stops propagation
  onClick={handleContentClick}                      // Bubbling: stops propagation
  onTouchStart={handleTouchEvent}
  onTouchMove={handleTouchEvent}
  onTouchEnd={handleTouchEvent}
  onTouchCancel={handleTouchCancel}
  className={...}
  style={{...contentStyle}}
>
  {children}
</LightboxDialogPopup>
```

**The Problem**: The popup is the `data-lightbox-popup` container (line 143), and it has **both capture-phase handlers** (`onPointerDownCapture`, `onClickCapture`) that record the click target, **and bubbling-phase handlers** (`onPointerDown`, `onClick`) that stop propagation. However, `handleBgClickCapture` checks if targets have `data-lightbox-bg` attribute (lines 100-101), but the popup itself only has `data-lightbox-popup`. This mismatch means clicks on buttons/images inside the popup will propagate up and potentially trigger the backdrop's close handlers if the event bubbles back out or if a parent element has `data-lightbox-bg` incorrectly set.

**Dismiss Chain**: Clicks on overlay backdrop → `handleOverlayPointerUp` checks if pointerDown and pointerUp both occurred on `e.currentTarget` (the backdrop itself) → calls `onClose()`. Clicks inside the popup should be stopped by `handleContentClick` (line 117), but only if the event reaches it.

## 1. Role & Mission
Drive megaplan workflow through the CLI until done or breakpoint. Priorities: user notes > CLI state > workflow rules > memory. Use CLI only. Keep outer conversation clean.

## 2. Startup
Run: `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 megaplan init --project-dir [REDACTED] --robustness light "Clean up overlay pointer-events dead code and vestigial defenses per tasks/2026-04-18-overlay-pointer-events-cleanup.md. Single milestone, T1-T6: delete neutralised syncBodyPointerEvents function + call sites, audit/remove vestigial pointer-events-auto overrides in media-lightbox domain, port overlayStack.test.ts updates from sibling commit 1b9045cc5, dedupe redundant handleOverlayPointerUp/handleOverlayClick close paths in useLightboxShellInteractionHandlers.ts, grep for any other body.style.pointerEvents writers, add docstring on pushOverlay/popOverlay noting the stack does not manage body pointer-events."`

Capture plan name, emit `PLAN_NAME: <name>` on its own line, then run `megaplan status --plan <name>`.

## 3. Phase Routing (Light)
init → plan → critique → revise → finalize → execute → done. No gate, no review.

## 4. After Every Phase
Run `megaplan status --plan <name>`, re-read state/next_step/valid_next, read full notes array if notes_count > 0.

## 5. Breakpoints
Only for: PHASE_ESCALATE (phase fails after fresh retry), EXECUTE_ESCALATE (3 consecutive no-progress executes). No GATE or EXECUTE_APPROVAL breakpoints at light + auto-approve.

Format:
BREAKPOINT: <type>
Plan: <name>
State: <state>
Summary: <reason>
Context: <artifacts/warnings/decision needed>

## 6. Safeguards
- Retry any failing non-execute phase once with --fresh.
- Same error twice → next retry must be --fresh.
- 3 consecutive executes without forward progress → EXECUTE_ESCALATE.
- Execute timeout → retry within same 3-attempt cap.

## 7. Resume Protocol
On SendMessage: re-read message, status, notes array, resume from CLI state.

## 8. Execution
Light robustness: after revise, CLI goes to gated → run finalize → run execute (auto-approve configured, no --user-approved needed, but do pass --confirm-destructive).

## 9. Overrides
Available: add-note, force-proceed (from critiqued → gated), replan (from critiqued/gated/finalized), abort. Use only when user direction requires.

## 10. Completion
COMPLETE: megaplan run finished

Audit existing zustand usage in /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src.

Find every file that imports from 'zustand' (including 'zustand/vanilla', 'zustand/traditional', 'zustand/shallow', 'zustand/middleware'). For each store, report:

1. **File path** + **store name** (exported name or creator function).
2. **What state it holds** — 3-5 bullet description of the fields (don't paste the full interface; summarize).
3. **How it's consumed** — `useStore(selector)` hook, vanilla `getState()` calls, subscribe callbacks, hook wrapper (e.g. `usePanesStore`)?
4. **Scope classification**: `global-ui` (theme/toasts/overlays), `global-domain` (project/session data), `feature-scoped` (one tool), `cross-cutting` (used across many domains), or `infrastructure` (auth/network/telemetry).
5. **Appropriateness verdict**: `appropriate` (fits zustand's sweet spot: shared client state, no server source-of-truth), `questionable` (could be React Query / Context / useState — explain in one line why), or `anti-pattern` (actively wrong — e.g. duplicating server state, or holding only values that never change).

Also note:
- Any store that uses `subscribe` with imperative side effects (these are usually coupling hotspots).
- Any store wrapped in a `useStoreWithEqualityFn` pattern (suggests they care about selector stability — good sign).
- Any store with a `Bootstrap` or `Provider` component that owns its lifecycle — that's the current project's pattern (see `panesStore.ts`, `overlayStack.ts`).

Background: the repo uses React Query for server state (Supabase-backed), React Context for DI, useState for local UI, and zustand for shared client state that neither React Query nor Context handles well. The team is in the middle of migrating more things to zustand under commits tagged M1a/M1b/M1c (see recent git log). I want a baseline of what exists today so I can judge whether the migration is hitting the right targets.

Output format: one section per store, plus a short summary table at the end (store name | scope | verdict). Keep the whole report under 900 words.

```
78	const MIN_EDITOR_HEIGHT = 200;

91	function getViewportHeight(): number {
92	  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
93	    return 0;
94	  }
95	
96	  return window.innerHeight;
97	}
98	
99	function getEffectivePaneHeights(params: {
100	  viewportHeight: number;
101	  idealEditorPaneHeight: number;
102	  generationsPaneHeight: number;
103	}) {
104	  const {
105	    viewportHeight,
106	    idealEditorPaneHeight,
107	    generationsPaneHeight,
108	  } = params;
109	
110	  if (idealEditorPaneHeight + generationsPaneHeight <= viewportHeight) {
111	    return {
112	      effectiveEditorPaneHeight: idealEditorPaneHeight,
113	      effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight: generationsPaneHeight,
114	    };
115	  }
116	
117	  if (viewportHeight >= generationsPaneHeight + MIN_EDITOR_HEIGHT) {
118	    return {
119	      effectiveEditorPaneHeight: viewportHeight - generationsPaneHeight,
120	      effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight: generationsPaneHeight,
121	    };
122	  }
123	
124	  const denom = 2 * (idealEditorPaneHeight + generationsPaneHeight) - viewportHeight;
125	  const effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight = Math.round((viewportHeight * generationsPaneHeight) / denom);
126	
127	  return {
128	    effectiveEditorPaneHeight: Math.max(viewportHeight - effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight, 0),
129	    effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight,
130	  };
131	}
132	
133	function deriveLayout(params: {
134	  viewportHeight: number;
135	  generationsPaneHeight: number;
136	  isGenerationsPaneLocked: boolean;
137	  isGenerationsPaneOpen: boolean;
138	  isEditorPaneLocked: boolean;
139	  isEditorPaneOpen: boolean;
140	}) {
141	  const {
142	    viewportHeight,
143	    generationsPaneHeight,
144	    isGenerationsPaneLocked,
145	    isGenerationsPaneOpen,
146	    isEditorPaneLocked,
147	    isEditorPaneOpen,
148	  } = params;
149	
150	  const editorVisible = isEditorPaneLocked || isEditorPaneOpen;
151	  const generationsVisible = isGenerationsPaneLocked || isGenerationsPaneOpen;
152	  const editorPaneHeight = Math.round(viewportHeight * 0.5);
153	
154	  if (!(editorVisible && generationsVisible)) {
155	    return {
156	      editorPaneHeight,
157	      effectiveEditorPaneHeight: editorPaneHeight,
158	      effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight: generationsPaneHeight,
159	    };
160	  }
161	
162	  const { effectiveEditorPaneHeight, effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight } = getEffectivePaneHeights({
163	    viewportHeight,
164	    idealEditorPaneHeight: editorPaneHeight,
165	    generationsPaneHeight,
166	  });
167	
168	  return {
169	    editorPaneHeight,
170	    effectiveEditorPaneHeight,
171	    effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight,
172	  };
173	}
174	
175	function createInitialSnapshot() {
176	  const viewportHeight = getViewportHeight();
177	  const generationsPaneHeight = PANE_CONFIG.dimensions.DEFAULT_HEIGHT;
178	  const layout = deriveLayout({
179	    viewportHeight,
180	    generationsPaneHeight,
181	    isGenerationsPaneLocked: false,
182	    isGenerationsPaneOpen: false,
183	    isEditorPaneLocked: false,
184	    isEditorPaneOpen: false,
185	  });
186

189	    isGenerationsPaneLocked: false,
190	    isGenerationsPaneOpen: false,
191	    generationsPaneHeight,
192	    effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight: layout.effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight,

210	  return previous?.owner === next.owner
211	    && previous.viewportHeight === next.viewportHeight
212	    && previous.locks.shots === next.locks.shots
213	    && previous.locks.tasks === next.locks.tasks
214	    && previous.locks.gens === next.locks.gens
215	    && previous.locks.editor === next.locks.editor
216	    && previous.isGenerationsPaneOpen === next.isGenerationsPaneOpen
217	    && previous.isEditorPaneOpen === next.isEditorPaneOpen
218	    && previous.isTasksPaneOpen === next.isTasksPaneOpen
219	    && previous.setIsGenerationsPaneLocked === next.setIsGenerationsPaneLocked
220	    && previous.setIsEditorPaneLocked === next.setIsEditorPaneLocked
221	    && previous.setIsShotsPaneLocked === next.setIsShotsPaneLocked
222	    && previous.setIsTasksPaneLocked === next.setIsTasksPaneLocked
223	    && previous.setIsGenerationsPaneOpen === next.setIsGenerationsPaneOpen
224	    && previous.setIsEditorPaneOpen === next.setIsEditorPaneOpen
225	    && previous.setIsTasksPaneOpen === next.setIsTasksPaneOpen
226	    && previous.resetAllPaneLocks === next.resetAllPaneLocks;
227	}
228	
229	function updateBootstrapRuntime(input: PanesStoreBootstrapInput | null): void {
230	  if (!input) {
231	    activeBootstrapRuntime = null;
232	    return;
233	  }
234	
235	  activeBootstrapRuntime = {
236	    setIsGenerationsPaneLocked: input.setIsGenerationsPaneLocked,
237	    setIsEditorPaneLocked: input.setIsEditorPaneLocked,
238	    setIsShotsPaneLocked: input.setIsShotsPaneLocked,
239	    setIsTasksPaneLocked: input.setIsTasksPaneLocked,
240	    setIsGenerationsPaneOpen: input.setIsGenerationsPaneOpen,
241	    setIsEditorPaneOpen: input.setIsEditorPaneOpen,
242	    setIsTasksPaneOpen: input.setIsTasksPaneOpen,
243	    resetAllPaneLocks: input.resetAllPaneLocks,
244	  };
245	}
246	
247	function buildBootstrappedState(
248	  currentState: Pick<
249	    PanesStoreState,
250	    | 'generationsPaneHeight'
251	    | 'shotsPaneWidth'
252	    | 'tasksPaneWidth'
253	    | 'activeTaskId'
254	  >,
255	  input: PanesStoreBootstrapInput,
256	) {
257	  const layout = deriveLayout({
258	    viewportHeight: input.viewportHeight,
259	    generationsPaneHeight: currentState.generationsPaneHeight,
260	    isGenerationsPaneLocked: input.locks.gens,
261	    isGenerationsPaneOpen: input.isGenerationsPaneOpen,
262	    isEditorPaneLocked: input.locks.editor,
263	    isEditorPaneOpen: input.isEditorPaneOpen,
264	  });
265	
266	  return {
267	    availability: { bootstrapped: true } as PanesStoreAvailability,
268	    isGenerationsPaneLocked: input.locks.gens,
269	    isGenerationsPaneOpen: input.isGenerationsPaneOpen,
270	    generationsPaneHeight: currentState.generationsPaneHeight,
271	    effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight: layout.effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight,
272	    isEditorPaneLocked: input.locks.editor,
273	    isEditorPaneOpen: input.isEditorPaneOpen,
274	    editorPaneHeight: layout.editorPaneHeight,
275	    effectiveEditorPaneHeight: layout.effectiveEditorPaneHeight,
276	    isShotsPaneLocked: input.locks.shots,
277	    shotsPaneWidth: currentState.shotsPaneWidth,
278	    isTasksPaneLocked: input.locks.tasks,
279	    tasksPaneWidth: currentState.tasksPaneWidth,
280	    activeTaskId: currentState.activeTaskId,
281	    isTasksPaneOpen: input.isTasksPaneOpen,
282	  };
283	}
284	
285	function buildStateWithUpdatedGenerationsHeight(
286	  state: Pick<
287	    PanesStoreState,
288	    | 'availability'
289	    | 'generationsPaneHeight'
290	    | 'shotsPaneWidth'
291	    | 'tasksPaneWidth'
292	    | 'activeTaskId'
293	    | 'isGenerationsPaneLocked'
294	    | 'isGenerationsPaneOpen'
295	    | 'isEditorPaneLocked'
296	    | 'isEditorPaneOpen'
297	    | 'isShotsPaneLocked'
298	    | 'isTasksPaneLocked'
299	    | 'isTasksPaneOpen'
300	  >,
301	  generationsPaneHeight: number,
302	) {
303	  const viewportHeight = activeBootstrapSnapshot?.viewportHeight ?? getViewportHeight();
304	  const layout = deriveLayout({
305	    viewportHeight,
306	    generationsPaneHeight,
307	    isGenerationsPaneLocked: state.isGenerationsPaneLocked,
308	    isGenerationsPaneOpen: state.isGenerationsPaneOpen,
309	    isEditorPaneLocked: state.isEditorPaneLocked,
310	    isEditorPaneOpen: state.isEditorPaneOpen,
311	  });
312	
313	  return {
314	    availability: state.availability,
315	    isGenerationsPaneLocked: state.isGenerationsPaneLocked,
316	    isGenerationsPaneOpen: state.isGenerationsPaneOpen,
317	    generationsPaneHeight,
318	    effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight: layout.effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight,
319	    isEditorPaneLocked: state.isEditorPaneLocked,
320	    isEditorPaneOpen: state.isEditorPaneOpen,
321	    editorPaneHeight: layout.editorPaneHeight,
322	    effectiveEditorPaneHeight: layout.effectiveEditorPaneHeight,
323	    isShotsPaneLocked: state.isShotsPaneLocked,
324	    shotsPaneWidth: state.shotsPaneWidth,
325	    isTasksPaneLocked: state.isTasksPaneLocked,
326	    tasksPaneWidth: state.tasksPaneWidth,
327	    activeTaskId: state.activeTaskId,
328	    isTasksPaneOpen: state.isTasksPaneOpen,
329	  };
330	}
331	
332	const initialSnapshot = createInitialSnapshot();
333	
334	const panesStore = createStore<PanesStoreState>((set, get) => ({
335	  ...initialSnapshot,
336	  setIsGenerationsPaneLocked: (isLocked) => {
337	    activeBootstrapRuntime?.setIsGenerationsPaneLocked(isLocked);
338	  },
339	  setIsGenerationsPaneOpen: (isOpen) => {
340	    activeBootstrapRuntime?.setIsGenerationsPaneOpen(isOpen);
341	  },
342	  setGenerationsPaneHeight: (height) => {
343	    set((state) => {
344	      if (state.generationsPaneHeight === height) {
345	        return state;
346	      }
347	
348	      return buildStateWithUpdatedGenerationsHeight(state, height);
349	    });
350	  },
351	  setIsEditorPaneLocked: (isLocked) => {
352	    activeBootstrapRuntime?.setIsEditorPaneLocked(isLocked);
353	  },
354	  setIsEditorPaneOpen: (isOpen) => {
355	    activeBootstrapRuntime?.setIsEditorPaneOpen(isOpen);
356	  },
357	  setIsShotsPaneLocked: (isLocked) => {
358	    activeBootstrapRuntime?.setIsShotsPaneLocked(isLocked);
359	  },
360	  setShotsPaneWidth: (width) => {
361	    set((state) => (
362	      state.shotsPaneWidth === width
363	        ? state
364	        : { shotsPaneWidth: width }
365	    ));
366	  },
367	  setIsTasksPaneLocked: (isLocked) => {
368	    activeBootstrapRuntime?.setIsTasksPaneLocked(isLocked);
369	  },
370	  setTasksPaneWidth: (width) => {
371	    set((state) => (
372	      state.tasksPaneWidth === width
373	        ? state
374	        : { tasksPaneWidth: width }
375	    ));
376	  },
377	  setActiveTaskId: (taskId) => {
378	    set((state) => (
379	      state.activeTaskId === taskId
380	        ? state
381	        : { activeTaskId: taskId }
382	    ));
383	  },
384	  setIsTasksPaneOpen: (isOpen) => {
385	    activeBootstrapRuntime?.setIsTasksPaneOpen(isOpen);
386	  },
387	  resetAllPaneLocks: () => {
388	    activeBootstrapRuntime?.resetAllPaneLocks();
389	  },
390	  bootstrap: (input) => {
391	    if (areBootstrapInputsEqual(activeBootstrapSnapshot, input)) {
392	      console.log('[PanesBoot] bootstrap short-circuit (inputs equal)', { owner: input.owner.toString() });
393	      return;
394	    }
395	
396	    console.log('[PanesBoot] bootstrap APPLY set()', {
397	      owner: input.owner.toString(),
398	      prevOwner: activeBootstrapOwner?.toString() ?? null,
399	      locks: input.locks,
400	      isGenerationsPaneOpen: input.isGenerationsPaneOpen,
401	      isEditorPaneOpen: input.isEditorPaneOpen,
402	      isTasksPaneOpen: input.isTasksPaneOpen,
403	    });
404	    activeBootstrapOwner = input.owner;
405	    activeBootstrapSnapshot = input;
406	    updateBootstrapRuntime(input);
407	
408	    set((state) => buildBootstrappedState(state, input));
409	  },
410	  clearBootstrap: (owner) => {
411	    if (owner && activeBootstrapOwner && owner !== activeBootstrapOwner) {
412	      return;
413	    }
414	
415	    activeBootstrapOwner = null;
416	    activeBootstrapSnapshot = null;
417	    updateBootstrapRuntime(null);
418	    set(() => createInitialSnapshot());
419	  },
420	  resetStore: () => {
421	    activeBootstrapOwner = null;
422	    activeBootstrapSnapshot = null;
423	    updateBootstrapRuntime(null);
424	    set(() => createInitialSnapshot());
425	  },
426	}));
427	
428	export function usePanesStoreApi() {
429	  return panesStore;
430	}
431	
432	export function usePanesStore<T>(
433	  selector: (state: PanesStoreState) => T,
434	  equalityFn?: (left: T, right: T) => boolean,
435	): T {
436	  return useStoreWithEqualityFn(panesStore, selector, equalityFn);
437	}
438	
439	export function usePanesStoreAvailability(): PanesStoreAvailability {
440	  return usePanesStore((state) => state.availability, shallow);
441	}
442	
443	export function bootstrapPanesStore(input: PanesStoreBootstrapInput): void {
444	  panesStore.getState().bootstrap(input);
445	}
446	
447	export function clearPanesStoreBootstrap(owner?: symbol): void {
448	  panesStore.getState().clearBootstrap(owner);
449	}
450	
451	export function resetPanesStore(): void {
452	  panesStore.getState().resetStore();
453	}
454	
455	/**
456	 * Singleton panes lifecycle contract:
457	 * - exactly one mounted bootstrap owner should drive runtime-backed pane state
458	 * - repeated bootstraps from that owner are idempotent
459	 * - reads outside bootstrap intentionally return the unlocked default snapshot
460	 */
461	export function useBootstrapPanesStore(): void {
462	  const ownerRef = useRef<symbol | null>(null);
463	  if (ownerRef.current === null) {
464	    ownerRef.current = Symbol('panes-store-bootstrap-owner');
465	  }
466	
467	  const {
468	    locks,
469	    isGenerationsPaneOpenState,
470	    isEditorPaneOpenState,
471	    isTasksPaneOpenState,
472	    setIsGenerationsPaneLocked,
473	    setIsEditorPaneLocked,
474	    setIsShotsPaneLocked,
475	    setIsTasksPaneLocked,
476	    setIsGenerationsPaneOpen,
477	    setIsEditorPaneOpen,
478	    setIsTasksPaneOpen,
479	    resetAllPaneLocks,
480	  } = usePaneLockPolicyState();
481	
482	  const bootstrapInput = useMemo<PanesStoreBootstrapInput>(() => ({
483	    owner: ownerRef.current as symbol,
484	    viewportHeight: getViewportHeight(),
485	    locks,
486	    isGenerationsPaneOpen: isGenerationsPaneOpenState,
487	    isEditorPaneOpen: isEditorPaneOpenState,
488	    isTasksPaneOpen: isTasksPaneOpenState,
489	    setIsGenerationsPaneLocked,
490	    setIsEditorPaneLocked,
491	    setIsShotsPaneLocked,
492	    setIsTasksPaneLocked,
493	    setIsGenerationsPaneOpen,
494	    setIsEditorPaneOpen,
495	    setIsTasksPaneOpen,
496	    resetAllPaneLocks,
497	  }), [
498	    locks,
499	    isGenerationsPaneOpenState,
500	    isEditorPaneOpenState,
501	    isTasksPaneOpenState,
502	    setIsGenerationsPaneLocked,
503	    setIsEditorPaneLocked,
504	    setIsShotsPaneLocked,
505	    setIsTasksPaneLocked,
506	    setIsGenerationsPaneOpen,
507	    setIsEditorPaneOpen,
508	    setIsTasksPaneOpen,
509	    resetAllPaneLocks,
510	  ]);
511	
512	  console.log('[PanesBoot] useBootstrapPanesStore render', {
513	    owner: ownerRef.current?.toString(),
514	    inputId: bootstrapInput && (bootstrapInput as unknown as { __id?: number }).__id,
515	  });
516	
517	  useEffect(() => {
518	    console.log('[PanesBoot] effect fires (bootstrapInput changed identity)');
519	    bootstrapPanesStore(bootstrapInput);
520	  }, [bootstrapInput]);
521	
522	  useEffect(() => {
523	    return () => {
524	      console.log('[PanesBoot] CLEANUP unmount', { owner: ownerRef.current?.toString() });
525	      clearPanesStoreBootstrap(ownerRef.current ?? undefined);
526	    };
527	  }, []);
528	}
529	
530	export function PanesStoreBootstrapBoundary({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
531	  console.log('[PanesBoot] PanesStoreBootstrapBoundary render');
532	  useBootstrapPanesStore();
533	  return children;
534	}
535	
536	export function __resetPanesStoreForTests(): void {
537	  resetPanesStore();
538	}
```

```
54	type OverlayStackSnapshot = Pick<OverlayStackState, 'overlays'>;

56	function isHTMLElement(value: unknown): value is HTMLElement {
57	  return value instanceof HTMLElement;
58	}
59	
60	function normalizeElements(
61	  elements?: Iterable<Element | null | undefined>,
62	): readonly HTMLElement[] {
63	  if (!elements) {
64	    return [];
65	  }
66	
67	  const nextElements: HTMLElement[] = [];
68	  for (const element of elements) {
69	    if (!isHTMLElement(element) || nextElements.includes(element)) {
70	      continue;
71	    }
72	    nextElements.push(element);
73	  }
74	
75	  return nextElements;
76	}
77	
78	function getTopOverlay(overlays: readonly OverlayStackEntry[]): OverlayStackEntry | null {
79	  return overlays.at(-1) ?? null;
80	}
81	
82	function isOverlayActuallyOpen(overlay: OverlayStackEntry): boolean {
83	  if (overlay.elements.length === 0) {
84	    return false;
85	  }
86	  return overlay.elements.some((element) => {
87	    const state = element.getAttribute('data-state');
88	    if (state === 'closed') return false;
89	    if (element.hasAttribute('data-open')) return true;
90	    if (state === 'open') return true;
91	    // Fallback for overlays without base-ui open/close state markers:
92	    // treat as open only when rendered with non-zero box.
93	    return element.offsetParent !== null || element.getClientRects().length > 0;
94	  });
95	}
96	
97	function getTopModalOverlay(overlays: readonly OverlayStackEntry[]): OverlayStackEntry | null {
98	  for (let index = overlays.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
99	    const overlay = overlays[index];
100	    if (overlay.modal && isOverlayActuallyOpen(overlay)) {
101	      return overlay;
102	    }
103	  }
104	
105	  return null;
106	}
107	
108	function canRestoreFocusTo(element: HTMLElement | null): element is HTMLElement {
109	  return isHTMLElement(element) && element.isConnected && typeof element.focus === 'function';
110	}
111	
112	function restoreFocus(element: HTMLElement | null): void {
113	  if (!canRestoreFocusTo(element)) {
114	    return;
115	  }
116	
117	  queueMicrotask(() => {
118	    if (!canRestoreFocusTo(element)) {
119	      return;
120	    }
121	
122	    try {
123	      element.focus({ preventScroll: true });
124	    } catch {
125	      element.focus();
126	    }
127	  });
128	}
129	
130	function createOverlayEntry(input: OverlayStackOpenInput): OverlayStackEntry {
131	  return {
132	    id: input.id,
133	    type: input.type,
134	    modal: input.modal ?? false,
135	    restoreFocusTo: input.restoreFocusTo ?? null,
136	    elements: normalizeElements(input.elements),
137	  };
138	}
139	
140	function containsElement(
141	  overlay: OverlayStackEntry | undefined,
142	  element: Element | null,
143	): boolean {
144	  if (!overlay || !element) {
145	    return false;
146	  }
147	
148	  return overlay.elements.some((candidate) => candidate === element || candidate.contains(element));
149	}

151	const overlayStackStore = createStore<OverlayStackState>((set, get) => ({
152	  overlays: [],
153	
154	  pushOverlay: (input) => {
155	    const nextEntry = createOverlayEntry(input);
156	    set((state) => {
157	      const nextOverlays = [
158	        ...state.overlays.filter((overlay) => overlay.id !== input.id),
159	        nextEntry,
160	      ];
161	      return { overlays: nextOverlays };
162	    });
163	  },
164	
165	  updateOverlay: (id, patch) => {
166	    set((state) => {
167	      const index = state.overlays.findIndex((overlay) => overlay.id === id);
168	      if (index === -1) {
169	        return state;
170	      }
171	
172	      const current = state.overlays[index];
173	      const nextOverlay: OverlayStackEntry = {
174	        ...current,
175	        ...(patch.type === undefined ? null : { type: patch.type }),
176	        ...(patch.modal === undefined ? null : { modal: patch.modal }),
177	        ...(patch.restoreFocusTo === undefined
178	          ? null
179	          : { restoreFocusTo: patch.restoreFocusTo }),
180	        ...(patch.elements === undefined
181	          ? null
182	          : { elements: normalizeElements(patch.elements) }),
183	      };
184	      const nextOverlays = [...state.overlays];
185	      nextOverlays[index] = nextOverlay;
186	      return { overlays: nextOverlays };
187	    });
188	  },
189	
190	  popOverlay: (id) => {
191	    const previousOverlays = get().overlays;
192	    const removedOverlay = previousOverlays.find((overlay) => overlay.id === id);
193	    if (!removedOverlay) {
194	      return;
195	    }
196

205	  },

211	  isOverlayTopmost: (id) => getTopOverlay(get().overlays)?.id === id,
212	
213	  isTopmostModalOverlay: (id) => getTopModalOverlay(get().overlays)?.id === id,
214	
215	  getOverlayLayer: (id) => {

217	    return index === -1 ? null : index + 1;

219	
220	  overlayContainsElement: (id, element) => {

225	  },

238	    }
239	
240	    return null;
241	  },

244	/** Returns the vanilla store API for imperative overlay registration and queries. */
245	export function useOverlayStackApi() {
246	  return overlayStackStore;
247	}
248	
249	function useOverlayStack<T>(
250	  selector: (state: OverlayStackState) => T,
251	  equalityFn?: (left: T, right: T) => boolean,
252	): T {
253	  return useStoreWithEqualityFn(overlayStackStore, selector, equalityFn);
254	}
255	
256	/** Subscribes to the current ordered overlay snapshot. */
257	export function useOverlayStackState(): OverlayStackSnapshot {
258	  return useOverlayStack((state) => ({ overlays: state.overlays }), shallow);
259	}
260	
261	/** Subscribes to the current topmost overlay, regardless of modality. */
262	export function useTopOverlay(): OverlayStackEntry | null {
263	  return useOverlayStack((state) => state.getTopOverlay());
264	}
265	
266	/** Subscribes to the current topmost modal overlay. */
267	export function useTopModalOverlay(): OverlayStackEntry | null {
268	  return useOverlayStack((state) => state.getTopModalOverlay());
269	}
270	
271	/** Subscribes to the overlay's 1-based stack layer. */
272	export function useOverlayLayer(id: string): number | null {
273	  return useOverlayStack((state) => state.getOverlayLayer(id));
274	}
275	
276	/** Subscribes to whether the overlay is the current topmost entry. */
277	export function useIsOverlayTopmost(id: string): boolean {
278	  return useOverlayStack((state) => state.isOverlayTopmost(id));
279	}
280	
281	/** Subscribes to whether the overlay is the current topmost modal entry. */
282	export function useIsTopmostModalOverlay(id: string): boolean {
283	  return useOverlayStack((state) => state.isTopmostModalOverlay(id));
284	}
285	
286	/** Subscribes to the topmost overlay that currently owns the provided element. */
287	export function useTopmostOverlayContainingElement(
288	  element: Element | null,
289	): OverlayStackEntry | null {
290	  return useOverlayStack((state) => state.getTopmostOverlayContainingElement(element));
291	}
292	
293	/** Resets overlay ordering for isolated tests. */
294	export function __resetOverlayStackForTests(): void {
295	  overlayStackStore.setState({ overlays: [] });
```

```
8	
9	export interface PendingGenerationTaskSnapshot {
10	  id: string;
11	  status: Task['status'];
12	  task_type: Task['taskType'];
13	}
14	
15	interface RealtimeTaskScopeState {
16	  tasks: Record<string, Task>;
17	  taskGenerationIds: Record<string, readonly string[]>;
18	  pendingTaskIdsByGeneration: Record<string, readonly string[]>;
19	  pendingTasksByGeneration: Record<string, readonly PendingGenerationTaskSnapshot[]>;
20	}
21	
22	interface RealtimeStoreState {
23	  scopes: Record<string, RealtimeTaskScopeState>;
24	  upsertTask: (task: Task, projectId?: string | null) => Task | null;
25	  upsertTasks: (tasks: readonly Task[], projectId?: string | null) => Task[];
26	  resetScope: (projectId?: string | null) => void;
27	  resetAll: () => void;
28	}
29	
30	const EMPTY_TASKS = Object.freeze({}) as Record<string, Task>;
31	const EMPTY_GENERATION_IDS = Object.freeze({}) as Record<string, readonly string[]>;
32	const EMPTY_PENDING_IDS = Object.freeze({}) as Record<string, readonly string[]>;
33	const EMPTY_PENDING_TASKS = Object.freeze({}) as Record<string, readonly PendingGenerationTaskSnapshot[]>;
34	const EMPTY_STRING_ARRAY = Object.freeze([]) as readonly string[];
35	const EMPTY_PENDING_TASK_ARRAY = Object.freeze([]) as readonly PendingGenerationTaskSnapshot[];
36	
37	function createEmptyScopeState(): RealtimeTaskScopeState {
38	  return {
39	    tasks: EMPTY_TASKS,
40	    taskGenerationIds: EMPTY_GENERATION_IDS,
41	    pendingTaskIdsByGeneration: EMPTY_PENDING_IDS,
42	    pendingTasksByGeneration: EMPTY_PENDING_TASKS,
43	  };
44	}
45	
46	function isPendingTaskStatus(status: Task['status']): boolean {
47	  return status === TASK_STATUS.QUEUED || status === TASK_STATUS.IN_PROGRESS;
48	}
49	
50	function addGenerationId(target: Set<string>, value: unknown): void {
51	  if (typeof value !== 'string') {
52	    return;
53	  }
54	
55	  const trimmed = value.trim();
56	  if (trimmed) {
57	    target.add(trimmed);
58	  }
59	}
60	
61	function addGenerationIdsFromArray(target: Set<string>, value: unknown): void {
62	  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
63	    return;
64	  }
65	
66	  value.forEach((item) => addGenerationId(target, item));
67	}
68	
69	function addGenerationIdsFromRecord(target: Set<string>, value: unknown): void {
70	  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) {
71	    return;
72	  }
73	
74	  const record = value as Record<string, unknown>;
75	  addGenerationId(target, record.based_on);
76	  addGenerationId(target, record.source_generation_id);
77	  addGenerationId(target, record.generation_id);
78	  addGenerationId(target, record.input_generation_id);
79	  addGenerationId(target, record.parent_generation_id);
80	  addGenerationId(target, record.start_image_generation_id);
81	  addGenerationId(target, record.end_image_generation_id);
82	  addGenerationId(target, record.pair_shot_generation_id);
83	  addGenerationIdsFromArray(target, record.input_image_generation_ids);
84	  addGenerationIdsFromArray(target, record.pair_shot_generation_ids);
85	}
86	
87	export function collectTaskGenerationIds(task: Task): readonly string[] {
88	  if (!isPendingTaskStatus(task.status)) {
89	    return EMPTY_STRING_ARRAY;
90	  }
91	
92	  const generationIds = new Set<string>();
93	  addGenerationIdsFromRecord(generationIds, task.params);
94	
95	  const params = task.params as Record<string, unknown>;
96	  addGenerationIdsFromRecord(generationIds, params.orchestrator_details);
97	  addGenerationIdsFromRecord(generationIds, params.full_orchestrator_payload);
98	  addGenerationIdsFromRecord(generationIds, params.individual_segment_params);
99	
100	  if (generationIds.size === 0) {
101	    return EMPTY_STRING_ARRAY;
102	  }
103	
104	  return Object.freeze(Array.from(generationIds));
105	}
106	
107	function areStringArraysEqual(left: readonly string[], right: readonly string[]): boolean {
108	  if (left === right) {
109	    return true;
110	  }
111	
112	  if (left.length !== right.length) {
113	    return false;
114	  }
115	
116	  for (let index = 0; index < left.length; index += 1) {
117	    if (left[index] !== right[index]) {
118	      return false;
119	    }
120	  }
121	
122	  return true;
123	}
124	
125	function buildPendingTaskSummaries(
126	  taskIds: readonly string[],
127	  tasks: Record<string, Task>,
128	  previous: readonly PendingGenerationTaskSnapshot[],
129	): readonly PendingGenerationTaskSnapshot[] {
130	  if (taskIds.length === 0) {
131	    return EMPTY_PENDING_TASK_ARRAY;
132	  }
133	
134	  const previousById = new Map(previous.map((task) => [task.id, task]));
135	  const next = taskIds.flatMap((taskId) => {
136	    const task = tasks[taskId];
137	    if (!task || !isPendingTaskStatus(task.status)) {
138	      return [];
139	    }
140	
141	    const existing = previousById.get(task.id);
142	    if (existing && existing.status === task.status && existing.task_type === task.taskType) {
143	      return [existing];
144	    }
145	
146	    return [{
147	      id: task.id,
148	      status: task.status,
149	      task_type: task.taskType,
150	    }];
151	  });
152	
153	  if (next.length === 0) {
154	    return EMPTY_PENDING_TASK_ARRAY;
155	  }
156	
157	  if (next.length === previous.length && next.every((task, index) => task === previous[index])) {
158	    return previous;
159	  }
160	
161	  return Object.freeze(next);
162	}
163	
164	function resolveScopeKey(projectId?: string | null): string | null {
165	  return resolveTaskProjectScope(projectId);
166	}
167	
168	function resolveTaskScopeKey(task: Task, projectId?: string | null): string | null {
169	  return resolveTaskProjectScope(projectId ?? task.projectId);
170	}
171	
172	function getScopeState(
173	  scopes: Record<string, RealtimeTaskScopeState>,
174	  scopeKey: string,
175	): RealtimeTaskScopeState {
176	  return scopes[scopeKey] ?? createEmptyScopeState();
177	}
178	
179	function buildUpdatedScopeState(scopeState: RealtimeTaskScopeState, task: Task): RealtimeTaskScopeState {
180	  const previousTask = scopeState.tasks[task.id];
181	  const nextTask = previousTask && deepEqual(previousTask, task) ? previousTask : task;
182	  const previousGenerationIds = scopeState.taskGenerationIds[task.id] ?? EMPTY_STRING_ARRAY;
183	  const nextGenerationIds = collectTaskGenerationIds(nextTask);
184	  const taskChanged = previousTask !== nextTask;
185	  const generationIdsChanged = !areStringArraysEqual(previousGenerationIds, nextGenerationIds);
186	
187	  if (!taskChanged && !generationIdsChanged) {
188	    return scopeState;
189	  }
190	
191	  const nextTasks = taskChanged
192	    ? { ...scopeState.tasks, [task.id]: nextTask }
193	    : scopeState.tasks;
194	
195	  const nextTaskGenerationIds = { ...scopeState.taskGenerationIds };
196	  if (nextGenerationIds.length > 0) {
197	    nextTaskGenerationIds[task.id] = nextGenerationIds;
198	  } else {
199	    delete nextTaskGenerationIds[task.id];
200	  }
201	
202	  const nextPendingTaskIdsByGeneration = { ...scopeState.pendingTaskIdsByGeneration };
203	  const nextPendingTasksByGeneration = { ...scopeState.pendingTasksByGeneration };
204	
205	  const affectedGenerationIds = new Set<string>([
206	    ...previousGenerationIds,
207	    ...nextGenerationIds,
208	  ]);
209	
210	  affectedGenerationIds.forEach((generationId) => {
211	    const previousTaskIds = scopeState.pendingTaskIdsByGeneration[generationId] ?? EMPTY_STRING_ARRAY;
212	    const shouldInclude = nextGenerationIds.includes(generationId);
213	    const alreadyIncluded = previousTaskIds.includes(task.id);
214	
215	    let nextTaskIds = previousTaskIds;
216	    if (shouldInclude && !alreadyIncluded) {
217	      nextTaskIds = Object.freeze([...previousTaskIds, task.id]);
218	    } else if (!shouldInclude && alreadyIncluded) {
219	      nextTaskIds = Object.freeze(previousTaskIds.filter((pendingTaskId) => pendingTaskId !== task.id));
220	    }
221	
222	    if (nextTaskIds.length === 0) {
223	      delete nextPendingTaskIdsByGeneration[generationId];
224	    } else {
225	      nextPendingTaskIdsByGeneration[generationId] = nextTaskIds;
226	    }
227	
228	    const previousPendingTasks = scopeState.pendingTasksByGeneration[generationId] ?? EMPTY_PENDING_TASK_ARRAY;
229	    const nextPendingTasks = buildPendingTaskSummaries(nextTaskIds, nextTasks, previousPendingTasks);
230	
231	    if (nextPendingTasks.length === 0) {
232	      delete nextPendingTasksByGeneration[generationId];
233	    } else {
234	      nextPendingTasksByGeneration[generationId] = nextPendingTasks;
235	    }
236	  });
237	
238	  return {
239	    tasks: nextTasks,
240	    taskGenerationIds: nextTaskGenerationIds,
241	    pendingTaskIdsByGeneration: nextPendingTaskIdsByGeneration,
242	    pendingTasksByGeneration: nextPendingTasksByGeneration,
243	  };
244	}
245	
246	function upsertTaskIntoScopes(
247	  scopes: Record<string, RealtimeTaskScopeState>,
248	  task: Task,
249	  projectId?: string | null,
250	): Record<string, RealtimeTaskScopeState> {
251	  const [REDACTED](task, projectId);
252	  if (!scopeKey) {
253	    return scopes;
254	  }
255	
256	  const scopeState = getScopeState(scopes, scopeKey);
257	  const nextScopeState = buildUpdatedScopeState(scopeState, task);
258	  if (nextScopeState === scopeState) {
259	    return scopes;
260	  }
261	
262	  return {
263	    ...scopes,
264	    [scopeKey]: nextScopeState,
265	  };
266	}
267	
268	const realtimeTaskStore = createStore<RealtimeStoreState>((set, get) => ({
269	  scopes: {},
270	  upsertTask: (task, projectId) => {
271	    const [REDACTED](task, projectId);
272	    if (!scopeKey) {
273	      return null;
274	    }
275	
276	    set((state) => ({
277	      scopes: upsertTaskIntoScopes(state.scopes, task, projectId),
278	    }));
279	
280	    return get().scopes[scopeKey]?.tasks[task.id] ?? null;
281	  },
282	  upsertTasks: (tasks, projectId) => {
283	    if (tasks.length === 0) {
284	      return [];
285	    }
286	
287	    set((state) => {
288	      let nextScopes = state.scopes;
289	      tasks.forEach((task) => {
290	        nextScopes = upsertTaskIntoScopes(nextScopes, task, projectId);
291	      });
292	
293	      return nextScopes === state.scopes
294	        ? state
295	        : { scopes: nextScopes };
296	    });
297	
298	    return tasks.flatMap((task) => {
299	      const [REDACTED](task, projectId);
300	      if (!scopeKey) {
301	        return [];
302	      }
303	
304	      const seededTask = get().scopes[scopeKey]?.tasks[task.id];
305	      return seededTask ? [seededTask] : [];
306	    });
307	  },
308	  resetScope: (projectId) => {
309	    const scopeKey = resolveScopeKey(projectId);
310	    if (!scopeKey) {
311	      return;
312	    }
313	
314	    set((state) => {
315	      if (!(scopeKey in state.scopes)) {
316	        return state;
317	      }
318	
319	      const nextScopes = { ...state.scopes };
320	      delete nextScopes[scopeKey];
321	      return { scopes: nextScopes };
322	    });
323	  },
324	  resetAll: () => {
325	    set({ scopes: {} });
326	  },
327	}));
328	
329	function useRealtimeTaskStore<T>(
330	  selector: (state: RealtimeStoreState) => T,
331	  equalityFn?: (left: T, right: T) => boolean,
332	): T {
333	  return useStoreWithEqualityFn(realtimeTaskStore, selector, equalityFn);
334	}
335	
336	export function useRealtimeTask(taskId: string, projectId?: string | null): Task | null {
337	  return useRealtimeTaskStore((state) => {
338	    const scopeKey = resolveScopeKey(projectId);
339	    if (!scopeKey || !taskId) {
340	      return null;
341	    }
342	
343	    return state.scopes[scopeKey]?.tasks[taskId] ?? null;
344	  });
345	}
346	
347	export function getRealtimeTaskSnapshot(taskId: string, projectId?: string | null): Task | null {
348	  const scopeKey = resolveScopeKey(projectId);
349	  if (!scopeKey || !taskId) {
350	    return null;
351	  }
352	
353	  return realtimeTaskStore.getState().scopes[scopeKey]?.tasks[taskId] ?? null;
354	}
355	
356	export function useRealtimePendingGenerationTasks(
357	  generationId: string | null | undefined,
358	  projectId?: string | null,
359	): { pendingCount: number; pendingTasks: readonly PendingGenerationTaskSnapshot[] } {
360	  return useRealtimeTaskStore((state) => {
361	    const scopeKey = resolveScopeKey(projectId);
362	    if (!scopeKey || !generationId) {
363	      return {
364	        pendingCount: 0,
365	        pendingTasks: EMPTY_PENDING_TASK_ARRAY,
366	      };
367	    }
368	
369	    const pendingTasks = state.scopes[scopeKey]?.pendingTasksByGeneration[generationId] ?? EMPTY_PENDING_TASK_ARRAY;
370	    return {
371	      pendingCount: pendingTasks.length,
372	      pendingTasks,
373	    };
374	  }, shallow);
375	}
376	
377	export function upsertRealtimeTaskSnapshot(task: Task, projectId?: string | null): Task | null {
378	  return realtimeTaskStore.getState().upsertTask(task, projectId);
379	}
380	
381	export function upsertRealtimeTaskSnapshots(tasks: readonly Task[], projectId?: string | null): Task[] {
382	  return realtimeTaskStore.getState().upsertTasks(tasks, projectId);
383	}
384	
385	export function seedRealtimeTaskFromRow(row: unknown, projectId?: string | null): Task | null {
386	  if (!isTaskDbRow(row)) {
387	    return null;
388	  }
389	
390	  return upsertRealtimeTaskSnapshot(mapTaskDbRowToTask(row), projectId);
391	}
392	
393	export function seedRealtimeTasksFromRows(rows: readonly unknown[], projectId?: string | null): Task[] {
394	  const tasks = rows.flatMap((row) => (isTaskDbRow(row) ? [mapTaskDbRowToTask(row)] : []));
395	  return upsertRealtimeTaskSnapshots(tasks, projectId);
396	}
397	
398	export function resetRealtimeTaskScope(projectId?: string | null): void {
399	  realtimeTaskStore.getState().resetScope(projectId);
400	}
401	
402	export function __resetRealtimeTaskStoreForTests(): void {
403	  realtimeTaskStore.getState().resetAll();
404	}
```

```
276	    set((state) => (
277	      state.gallery.gallerySelectionMap.size === 0
278	        ? state
279	        : { gallery: initialGalleryState() }
280	    ));
281	  },
282	
283	  selectGalleryItem: (id, meta, options) => {
284	    const normalized = normalizeSelectionItem({ id, ...meta });
285	    if (!normalized) {
286	      return;
287	    }
288	
289	    set((state) => {
290	      const previous = state.gallery.gallerySelectionMap;
291	      if (!options?.toggle) {
292	        if (hasSameSelection(previous, [normalized])) {
293	          return state;
294	        }
295	
296	        return {
297	          gallery: buildGalleryState(new Map([
298	            [
299	              normalized.id,
300	              {
301	                url: normalized.url,
302	                mediaType: normalized.mediaType,
303	                generationId: normalized.generationId,
304	                variantId: normalized.variantId,
305	              },
306	            ],
307	          ])),
308	          timeline: clearTimelineClipSelection(state.timeline),
309	        };
310	      }
311	
312	      const next = new Map(previous);
313	      if (next.has(normalized.id)) {
314	        next.delete(normalized.id);
315	      } else {
316	        next.set(normalized.id, {
317	          url: normalized.url,
318	          mediaType: normalized.mediaType,
319	          generationId: normalized.generationId,
320	          variantId: normalized.variantId,
321	        });
322	      }
323	
324	      return { gallery: buildGalleryState(next) };
325	    });
326	  },
327	
328	  selectGalleryItems: (items, options) => {
329	    const normalizedItems = items
330	      .map(normalizeSelectionItem)
331	      .filter((item): item is GallerySelectionEntry & { id: string } => item !== null);
332	
333	    set((state) => {
334	      const previous = state.gallery.gallerySelectionMap;
335	      if (normalizedItems.length === 0) {
336	        if (options?.append || previous.size === 0) {
337	          return state;
338	        }
339	
340	        return {
341	          gallery: initialGalleryState(),
342	          timeline: clearTimelineClipSelection(state.timeline),
343	        };
344	      }
345	
346	      const next = options?.append ? new Map(previous) : new Map<string, GallerySelectionEntry>();
347	      let changed = !options?.append && !hasSameSelection(previous, normalizedItems);
348	
349	      for (const item of normalizedItems) {
350	        const nextEntry = {
351	          url: item.url,
352	          mediaType: item.mediaType,
353	          generationId: item.generationId,
354	          variantId: item.variantId,
355	        };
356	        const previousEntry = previous.get(item.id);
357	        if (
358	          !previousEntry
359	          || previousEntry.url !== nextEntry.url
360	          || previousEntry.mediaType !== nextEntry.mediaType
361	          || previousEntry.generationId !== nextEntry.generationId
362	          || previousEntry.variantId !== nextEntry.variantId
363	        ) {
364	          changed = true;
365	        }
366	        next.set(item.id, nextEntry);
367	      }
368	
369	      if (!changed && next.size === previous.size) {
370	        return state;
371	      }
372	
373	      return options?.append
374	        ? { gallery: buildGalleryState(next) }
375	        : {
376	            gallery: buildGalleryState(next),
377	            timeline: clearTimelineClipSelection(state.timeline),
378	          };
379	    });
380	  },
381	
382	  deselectGalleryItems: (ids) => {
383	    const idsToRemove = new Set(ids);
384	    if (idsToRemove.size === 0) {
385	      return;
386	    }
387	
388	    set((state) => {
389	      let changed = false;
390	      const next = new Map(state.gallery.gallerySelectionMap);
391	
392	      idsToRemove.forEach((id) => {
393	        if (next.delete(id)) {
394	          changed = true;
395	        }
396	      });
397	
398	      return changed ? { gallery: buildGalleryState(next) } : state;
399	    });
400	  },
401	
402	  clearTimelineSelection: (options) => {
403	    set((state) => {
404	      const nextTimeline = clearTimelineClipSelection(state.timeline);
405	      const nextGallery = options?.clearGallery ? initialGalleryState() : state.gallery;
406	      if (nextTimeline === state.timeline && nextGallery === state.gallery) {
407	        return state;
408	      }
409	      return {
410	        timeline: nextTimeline,
411	        gallery: nextGallery,
412	      };
413	    });
414	  },
415	
416	  selectTimelineClip: (clipId, options, syncOptions) => {
417	    set((state) => {
418	      const currentSelection = state.timeline.selectedClipIds;
419	      const currentPrimary = state.timeline.primaryClipId;
420	
421	      if (options?.preserveSelection && currentSelection.has(clipId)) {
422	        return state;
423	      }
424	
425	      let nextSelection: ReadonlySet<string>;
426	      let nextPrimary = clipId;
427	
428	      if (!options?.toggle) {
429	        nextSelection = new Set([clipId]);
430	      } else {
431	        const mutableSelection = new Set(currentSelection);
432	        if (mutableSelection.has(clipId)) {
433	          mutableSelection.delete(clipId);
434	          nextPrimary = getPrimaryClipId(
435	            mutableSelection,
436	            currentPrimary === clipId ? null : currentPrimary,
437	          ) ?? null;
438	        } else {
439	          mutableSelection.add(clipId);
440	        }
441	        nextSelection = mutableSelection;
442	      }
443	
444	      const nextTimeline = buildTimelineState(
445	        nextSelection,
446	        nextPrimary,
447	        state.timeline.selectedTrackId,
448	        nextSelection.size > 1,
449	      );
450	
451	      if (
452	        areSetsEqual(state.timeline.selectedClipIds, nextTimeline.selectedClipIds)
453	        && state.timeline.primaryClipId === nextTimeline.primaryClipId
454	        && state.timeline.selectedClipId === nextTimeline.selectedClipId
455	        && state.timeline.additiveSelection === nextTimeline.additiveSelection
456	      ) {
457	        return state;
458	      }
459	
460	      return {
461	        timeline: nextTimeline,
462	        gallery: syncOptions?.clearGallery === false ? state.gallery : initialGalleryState(),
463	      };
464	    });
465	  },
466	
467	  selectTimelineClips: (clipIds, syncOptions) => {
468	    const nextSelection = new Set<string>();
469	    for (const clipId of clipIds) {
470	      nextSelection.add(clipId);
471	    }
472	
473	    set((state) => {
474	      const nextTimeline = buildTimelineState(
475	        nextSelection,
476	        null,
477	        state.timeline.selectedTrackId,
478	        nextSelection.size > 1,
479	      );
480	
481	      if (
482	        areSetsEqual(state.timeline.selectedClipIds, nextTimeline.selectedClipIds)
483	        && state.timeline.primaryClipId === nextTimeline.primaryClipId
484	        && state.timeline.selectedClipId === nextTimeline.selectedClipId
485	        && state.timeline.additiveSelection === nextTimeline.additiveSelection
486	      ) {
487	        return state;
488	      }
489	
490	      return {
491	        timeline: nextTimeline,
492	        gallery: syncOptions?.clearGallery === false ? state.gallery : initialGalleryState(),
493	      };
494	    });
495	  },
496	
497	  addTimelineClips: (clipIds) => {
498	    const nextClipIds = new Set<string>();

500	      nextClipIds.add(clipId);
501	    }
502	
503	    set((state) => {
504	      const mergedSelection = new Set(state.timeline.selectedClipIds);

506	      const nextPrimary = getPrimaryClipId(mergedSelection, state.timeline.primaryClipId);
507	
508	      if (
509	        areSetsEqual(state.timeline.selectedClipIds, mergedSelection)
510	        && state.timeline.primaryClipId === nextPrimary
511	      ) {
512	        return state;
513	      }
514	
515	      return {
516	        timeline: buildTimelineState(
517	          mergedSelection,
518	          nextPrimary,
519	          state.timeline.selectedTrackId,
520	          mergedSelection.size > 1,
521	        ),
522	      };
523	    });
524	  },

739	  selectedClipIds: ReadonlySet<string>;
740	  selectedClipIdsRef: React.MutableRefObject<Set<string>>;
741	  additiveSelectionRef: React.MutableRefObject<boolean>;
742	  primaryClipId: string | null;
743	  selectClip: (clipId: string, opts?: SelectClipOptions) => void;
744	  selectClips: (clipIds: Iterable<string>) => void;
745	  addToSelection: (clipIds: Iterable<string>) => void;

751	export function useTimelineMultiSelect(): UseTimelineMultiSelectResult {
752	  const {
753	    selectedClipIds,
754	    primaryClipId,
755	    additiveSelection,
756	    clearSelection,
757	    selectClip,
758	    selectClips,
759	    addToSelection,
760	    pruneSelection,
761	  } = useTimelineSelectionStore();
762	
763	  const selectedClipIdsRef = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set(selectedClipIds));
764	  const additiveSelectionRef = useRef(additiveSelection);
765	
766	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
767	    selectedClipIdsRef.current = new Set(selectedClipIds);
768	    additiveSelectionRef.current = additiveSelection;
769	  }, [additiveSelection, selectedClipIds]);
770	
771	  const isClipSelected = useCallback((clipId: string) => {
772	    return selectedClipIdsRef.current.has(clipId);
773	  }, []);
774	
775	  return useMemo(() => ({
776	    selectedClipIds,
777	    selectedClipIdsRef,
778	    additiveSelectionRef,
779	    primaryClipId,
780	    selectClip: (clipId: string, opts?: SelectClipOptions) => {
781	      selectionStore.getState().selectTimelineClip(clipId, opts, { clearGallery: false });
782	    },
783	    selectClips: (clipIds: Iterable<string>) => {
784	      selectionStore.getState().selectTimelineClips(clipIds, { clearGallery: false });
785	    },
786	    addToSelection,
787	    clearSelection: () => {
788	      selectionStore.getState().clearTimelineSelection({ clearGallery: false });
789	    },
790	    isClipSelected,
791	    pruneSelection,
792	  }), [
793	    addToSelection,
794	    additiveSelection,
795	    clearSelection,
796	    isClipSelected,
797	    primaryClipId,
798	    pruneSelection,
799	    selectClip,
800	    selectClips,
801	    selectedClipIds,
802	  ]);
803	}

809	    shot: initialShotState(),
810	  });
811	}
```

```
84	function cloneValue<T>(value: T): T {
85	  return structuredClone(value);

88	function asError(error: unknown): Error {

92	function uniqueKeys<T>(keys: Iterable<keyof T>): Array<keyof T> {
93	  return Array.from(new Set(keys));

95	
96	function getChangedKeys<T extends object>(current: T, baseline: T): Array<keyof T> {
97	  const keys = new Set<keyof T>([
98	    ...(Object.keys(current) as Array<keyof T>),
99	    ...(Object.keys(baseline) as Array<keyof T>),
100	  ]);
101	
102	  return Array.from(keys).filter((key) => !deepEqual(current[key], baseline[key]));
103	}

105	export function createEntityStore<T extends object>(
106	  config: EntityStoreConfig<T>
107	): EntityStoreApi<T> {
108	  const debounceMs = config.persistenceDebounceMs ?? 300;
109	  const textFieldKeySet = new Set<keyof T>(config.textFieldKeys ?? []);
110	  const pendingTimers = new Map<string, TimeoutHandle>();
111	  const loadPromises = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();
112	  const placeholderEntities = new Map<string, EntityRecord<T>>();
113	
114	  const createRecord = (seed: T, overrides?: Partial<EntityRecord<T>>): EntityRecord<T> => ({
115	    settings: cloneValue(seed),
116	    status: 'idle',
117	    error: null,
118	    hasPersistedData: false,
119	    cleanSnapshot: cloneValue(seed),
120	    savedSettings: cloneValue(seed),
121	    pendingTextFieldKeys: [],
122	    loaded: false,
123	    ...overrides,
124	  });
125	
126	  const getPlaceholder = (entityId: string): EntityRecord<T> => {
127	    const existing = placeholderEntities.get(entityId);
128	    if (existing) {
129	      return existing;
130	    }
131	
132	    const placeholder = createRecord(config.defaults);
133	    placeholderEntities.set(entityId, placeholder);
134	    return placeholder;
135	  };
136	
137	  const getSeed = ({ db, lastUsed }: Omit<EntityBootstrapInput<T>, 'entityId'>): T => {
138	    const source = db ?? lastUsed;
139	    if (!source) return cloneValue(config.defaults);
140	    // Backfill any missing keys from defaults so partial DB rows (saved before a field was added)
141	    // don't surface `undefined` to consumers that read the field directly.
142	    return { ...cloneValue(config.defaults), ...source } as T;
143	  };
144	
145	  const store = createStore<EntityStoreState<T>>((set, get) => {
146	    const clearPendingTimer = (entityId: string): void => {
147	      const timer = pendingTimers.get(entityId);
148	      if (!timer) {
149	        return;
150	      }
151	      clearTimeout(timer);
152	      pendingTimers.delete(entityId);
153	    };
154	
155	    const ensureStoredEntity = (entityId: string): EntityRecord<T> => {
156	      const existing = get().entities[entityId];
157	      if (existing) {
158	        return existing;
159	      }
160	
161	      const nextRecord = createRecord(config.defaults);
162	      set((state) => ({
163	        entities: {
164	          ...state.entities,
165	          [entityId]: nextRecord,
166	        },
167	      }));
168	      return get().entities[entityId];
169	    };
170	
171	    const isDirtyRecord = (record: EntityRecord<T> | undefined): boolean => {
172	      if (!record) {
173	        return false;
174	      }
175	      return !deepEqual(record.settings, record.cleanSnapshot);
176	    };
177	
178	    const hasPendingPersistenceRecord = (
179	      entityId: string,
180	      record: EntityRecord<T> | undefined
181	    ): boolean => {
182	      if (!record) {
183	        return false;
184	      }
185	
186	      return (
187	        record.status === 'saving' ||
188	        pendingTimers.has(entityId) ||
189	        record.pendingTextFieldKeys.length > 0
190	      );
191	    };
192	
193	    const persistEntity = async (entityId: string): Promise<void> => {
194	      clearPendingTimer(entityId);
195	      const current = get().entities[entityId] ?? ensureStoredEntity(entityId);
196	
197	      if (!isDirtyRecord(current)) {
198	        if (current.pendingTextFieldKeys.length > 0) {
199	          set((state) => ({
200	            entities: {
201	              ...state.entities,
202	              [entityId]: {
203	                ...state.entities[entityId],
204	                pendingTextFieldKeys: [],
205	              },
206	            },
207	          }));
208	        }
209	        return;
210	      }
211	
212	      const snapshot = cloneValue(current.settings);
213	      set((state) => {
214	        const entity = state.entities[entityId];
215	        if (!entity) {
216	          return state;
217	        }
218	
219	        return {
220	          entities: {
221	            ...state.entities,
222	            [entityId]: {
223	              ...entity,
224	              status: 'saving',
225	              error: null,
226	            },
227	          },
228	        };
229	      });
230	
231	      try {
232	        await config.save(entityId, cloneValue(snapshot));
233	
234	        set((state) => {
235	          const entity = state.entities[entityId];
236	          if (!entity) {
237	            return state;
238	          }
239	
240	          const stillDirty = !deepEqual(entity.settings, snapshot);
241	
242	          return {
243	            entities: {
244	              ...state.entities,
245	              [entityId]: {
246	                ...entity,
247	                status: 'ready',
248	                error: null,
249	                hasPersistedData: true,
250	                cleanSnapshot: snapshot,
251	                savedSettings: snapshot,
252	                pendingTextFieldKeys: stillDirty ? entity.pendingTextFieldKeys : [],
253	              },
254	            },
255	          };
256	        });
257	      } catch (error) {
258	        set((state) => {
259	          const entity = state.entities[entityId];
260	          if (!entity) {
261	            return state;
262	          }
263	
264	          return {
265	            entities: {
266	              ...state.entities,
267	              [entityId]: {
268	                ...entity,
269	                status: 'error',
270	                error: asError(error),
271	              },
272	            },
273	          };
274	        });
275	        throw error;
276	      }
277	    };
278	
279	    const scheduleSave = (entityId: string): void => {
280	      clearPendingTimer(entityId);
281	      pendingTimers.set(
282	        entityId,
283	        setTimeout(() => {
284	          pendingTimers.delete(entityId);
285	          void persistEntity(entityId);
286	        }, debounceMs)
287	      );
288	    };
289	
290	    const applyPatch = (
291	      entityId: string,
292	      patch: Partial<T>,
293	      options?: { deferKeys?: readonly (keyof T)[] }
294	    ): void => {
295	      const current = get().entities[entityId] ?? ensureStoredEntity(entityId);
296	      const patchEntries = Object.entries(patch) as Array<[keyof T, T[keyof T]]>;
297	      const changedEntries = patchEntries.filter(([key, value]) => !deepEqual(current.settings[key], value));
298	
299	      if (changedEntries.length === 0) {
300	        return;
301	      }
302	
303	      const nextSettings = {
304	        ...current.settings,
305	        ...Object.fromEntries(changedEntries),
306	      } as T;
307	      const explicitDeferredKeys = new Set<keyof T>(options?.deferKeys ?? []);
308	      const deferredKeys = uniqueKeys(
309	        changedEntries
310	          .map(([key]) => key)
311	          .filter((key) => textFieldKeySet.has(key) || explicitDeferredKeys.has(key))
312	      );
313	      const shouldScheduleSave =
314	        current.status !== 'loading'
315	        && changedEntries.some(([key]) => !deferredKeys.includes(key));
316	
317	      set((state) => {
318	        const entity = state.entities[entityId] ?? current;
319	        return {
320	          entities: {
321	            ...state.entities,
322	            [entityId]: {
323	              ...entity,
324	              settings: nextSettings,
325	              status:
326	                entity.status === 'loading'
327	                  ? 'loading'
328	                  : entity.status === 'saving'
329	                    ? 'saving'
330	                    : 'ready',
331	              error: null,
332	              pendingTextFieldKeys: uniqueKeys([
333	                ...entity.pendingTextFieldKeys,
334	                ...deferredKeys,
335	              ]),
336	            },
337	          },
338	        };
339	      });
340	
341	      if (shouldScheduleSave) {
342	        scheduleSave(entityId);
343	      }
344	    };
345	
346	    return {
347	      entities: {},
348	
349	      bootstrapEntity: ({ entityId, db, lastUsed }) => {
350	        clearPendingTimer(entityId);
351	        const seed = getSeed({ db, lastUsed });
352	        const current = get().entities[entityId];
353	        const currentHasPendingPersistence = hasPendingPersistenceRecord(entityId, current);
354	        const currentIsDirty = isDirtyRecord(current);
355	        const shouldPreserveLocalState = !!current && (currentHasPendingPersistence || currentIsDirty);
356	        const changedKeys = shouldPreserveLocalState
357	          ? getChangedKeys(current.settings, seed)
358	          : [];
359	        const pendingTextFieldKeys = shouldPreserveLocalState
360	          ? uniqueKeys([
361	              ...current.pendingTextFieldKeys,
362	              ...changedKeys.filter((key) => textFieldKeySet.has(key)),
363	            ])
364	          : [];
365	        const shouldScheduleSave = shouldPreserveLocalState
366	          && changedKeys.some((key) => !pendingTextFieldKeys.includes(key));
367	
368	        set((state) => ({
369	          entities: {
370	            ...state.entities,
371	            [entityId]: createRecord(seed, shouldPreserveLocalState
372	              ? {
373	                  settings: cloneValue(current.settings),
374	                  status: 'ready',
375	                  error: null,
376	                  hasPersistedData: db !== null,
377	                  cleanSnapshot: cloneValue(seed),
378	                  savedSettings: cloneValue(seed),
379	                  pendingTextFieldKeys,
380	                  loaded: true,
381	                }
382	              : {
383	                  status: 'ready',
384	                  error: null,
385	                  hasPersistedData: db !== null,
386	                  savedSettings: cloneValue(seed),
387	                  pendingTextFieldKeys: [],
388	                  loaded: true,
389	                }),
390	          },
391	        }));
392	
393	        if (shouldScheduleSave) {
394	          scheduleSave(entityId);
395	        }
396	      },
397	
398	      ensureEntity: async (entityId) => {
399	        const current = get().entities[entityId];
400	        if (current?.loaded) {
401	          return;
402	        }
403	
404	        const inFlight = loadPromises.get(entityId);
405	        if (inFlight) {
406	          return inFlight;
407	        }
408	
409	        set((state) => {
410	          const existing = state.entities[entityId] ?? createRecord(config.defaults);
411	          return {
412	            entities: {
413	              ...state.entities,
414	              [entityId]: {
415	                ...existing,
416	                status: 'loading',
417	                error: null,
418	              },
419	            },
420	          };
421	        });
422	
423	        const loadPromise = config
424	          .load(entityId)
425	          .then((result) => {
426	            get().bootstrapEntity({
427	              entityId,
428	              db: result.db,
429	              lastUsed: result.lastUsed,
430	            });
431	          })
432	          .catch((error) => {
433	            set((state) => {
434	              const existing = state.entities[entityId] ?? createRecord(config.defaults);
435	              return {
436	                entities: {
437	                  ...state.entities,
438	                  [entityId]: {
439	                    ...existing,
440	                    status: 'error',
441	                    error: asError(error),
442	                  },
443	                },
444	              };
445	            });
446	            throw error;
447	          })
448	          .finally(() => {
449	            loadPromises.delete(entityId);
450	          });
451	
452	        loadPromises.set(entityId, loadPromise);
453	        return loadPromise;
454	      },
455	
456	      reloadEntity: async (entityId) => {
457	        clearPendingTimer(entityId);
458	        loadPromises.delete(entityId);
459	        set((state) => {
460	          const existing = state.entities[entityId] ?? createRecord(config.defaults);
461	          return {
462	            entities: {
463	              ...state.entities,
464	              [entityId]: {
465	                ...existing,
466	                status: 'loading',
467	                error: null,
468	                loaded: false,
469	              },
470	            },
471	          };
472	        });
473	
474	        await get().ensureEntity(entityId);
475	      },
476	
477	      updateField: (entityId, key, value, options) => {
478	        get().updateFields(
479	          entityId,
480	          { [key]: value } as Partial<T>,
481	          options?.deferPersistence ? { deferKeys: [key] } : undefined
482	        );
483	      },
484	
485	      updateFields: (entityId, patch, options) => {
486	        applyPatch(entityId, patch, options);
487	      },
488	
489	      updateTextField: (entityId, key, value) => {
490	        get().updateFields(entityId, { [key]: value } as Partial<T>, { deferKeys: [key] });
491	      },
492	
493	      flushTextFields: async (entityId) => {
494	        const entity = get().entities[entityId];
495	        if (!entity) {
496	          return;
497	        }
498	
499	        if (!hasPendingPersistenceRecord(entityId, entity) && !isDirtyRecord(entity)) {
500	          return;
501	        }
502	
503	        await persistEntity(entityId);
504	      },
505	
506	      save: async (entityId) => {
507	        await persistEntity(entityId);
508	      },
509	
510	      saveImmediate: async (entityId) => {
511	        await persistEntity(entityId);
512	      },
513	
514	      revert: (entityId) => {
515	        clearPendingTimer(entityId);
516	        const current = get().entities[entityId] ?? ensureStoredEntity(entityId);
517	        set((state) => ({
518	          entities: {
519	            ...state.entities,
520	            [entityId]: {
521	              ...state.entities[entityId],
522	              settings: cloneValue(current.cleanSnapshot),
523	              status: 'ready',
524	              error: null,
525	              pendingTextFieldKeys: [],
526	            },
527	          },
528	        }));
529	      },
530	
531	      reset: (entityId, nextSettings) => {
532	        clearPendingTimer(entityId);
533	        const current = get().entities[entityId] ?? ensureStoredEntity(entityId);
534	        const resetSnapshot = cloneValue(nextSettings ?? config.defaults);
535	        set((state) => ({
536	          entities: {
537	            ...state.entities,
538	            [entityId]: {
539	              ...state.entities[entityId],
540	              settings: resetSnapshot,
541	              cleanSnapshot: cloneValue(resetSnapshot),
542	              savedSettings: cloneValue(resetSnapshot),
543	              status: 'ready',
544	              error: null,
545	              hasPersistedData: current.hasPersistedData,
546	              pendingTextFieldKeys: [],
547	            },
548	          },
549	        }));
550	      },
551	
552	      isDirty: (entityId) => {
553	        return isDirtyRecord(get().entities[entityId]);
554	      },
555	
556	      hasPendingPersistence: (entityId) => {
557	        return hasPendingPersistenceRecord(entityId, get().entities[entityId]);
558	      },
559	
560	      syncExternalEntity: ({ entityId, db, lastUsed }) => {
561	        const current = get().entities[entityId];
562	        if (!current) {
563	          get().bootstrapEntity({ entityId, db, lastUsed });
564	          return true;
565	        }
566	
567	        if (isDirtyRecord(current) || hasPendingPersistenceRecord(entityId, current)) {
568	          return false;
569	        }
570	
571	        clearPendingTimer(entityId);
572	        get().bootstrapEntity({ entityId, db, lastUsed });
573	        return true;
574	      },
575	    };
576	  });
577	
578	  function useEntity(entityId: string): EntityView<T> {
579	    const entitySlice = useStoreWithEqualityFn(
580	      store,
581	      (state) => {
582	        const entity = state.entities[entityId] ?? getPlaceholder(entityId);
583	        return {
584	          settings: entity.settings,
585	          status: entity.status,
586	          error: entity.error,
587	          hasPersistedData: entity.hasPersistedData,
588	        };
589	      },
590	      shallow
591	    );
592	
593	    useEffect(() => {
594	      void store.getState().ensureEntity(entityId).catch(() => {});
595	    }, [entityId]);
596	
597	    const actions = useMemo(
598	      () => ({
599	        updateField: <K extends keyof T>(key: K, value: T[K]) => {
600	          store.getState().updateField(entityId, key, value);
601	        },
602	        updateFields: (patch: Partial<T>) => {
603	          store.getState().updateFields(entityId, patch);
604	        },
605	        updateTextField: <K extends keyof T>(key: K, value: T[K]) => {
606	          store.getState().updateTextField(entityId, key, value);
607	        },
608	        flushTextFields: () => store.getState().flushTextFields(entityId),
609	        save: () => store.getState().save(entityId),
610	        saveImmediate: () => store.getState().saveImmediate(entityId),
611	        revert: () => {
612	          store.getState().revert(entityId);
613	        },
614	        reset: (nextSettings?: T) => {
615	          store.getState().reset(entityId, nextSettings);
616	        },
617	      }),
618	      [entityId]
619	    );
620	
621	    return useMemo(
622	      () => ({
623	        ...entitySlice,
624	        ...actions,
625	      }),
626	      [actions, entitySlice]
627	    );
628	  }
629	
630	  return {
631	    useEntity,
632	    getState: store.getState,
633	  };
634	}
635
```

```
302	export function createTimelineStore(bootstrap?: Partial<TimelineStoreBootstrap>): TimelineStoreApi {
303	  const initialSlices = createInitialSlices();
304	  const seededSlices = {
305	    data: bootstrap?.data ?? initialSlices.data,
306	    ops: bootstrap?.ops ?? initialSlices.ops,
307	    chrome: bootstrap?.chrome ?? initialSlices.chrome,
308	    playback: bootstrap?.playback ?? initialSlices.playback,
309	  };
310	  const initialMounted = bootstrap !== undefined;
311	
312	  return createStore<TimelineStoreState>((set) => ({
313	    availability: { mounted: initialMounted },
314	    ...seededSlices,
315	    setMounted: (mounted) => {
316	      set((state) => (
317	        state.availability.mounted === mounted
318	          ? state
319	          : { availability: { mounted } }
320	      ));
321	    },
322	    syncDataSlice: (data) => {
323	      set((state) => (
324	        state.data === data && state.availability.mounted
325	          ? state
326	          : {
327	              availability: state.availability.mounted ? state.availability : { mounted: true },
328	              data,
329	            }
330	      ));
331	    },
332	    syncOpsSlice: (ops) => {
333	      set((state) => (
334	        state.ops === ops && state.availability.mounted
335	          ? state
336	          : {
337	              availability: state.availability.mounted ? state.availability : { mounted: true },
338	              ops,
339	            }
340	      ));
341	    },
342	    syncChromeSlice: (chrome) => {
343	      set((state) => (
344	        state.chrome === chrome && state.availability.mounted
345	          ? state
346	          : {
347	              availability: state.availability.mounted ? state.availability : { mounted: true },
348	              chrome,
349	            }
350	      ));
351	    },
352	    syncPlaybackSlice: (playback) => {
353	      set((state) => (
354	        state.playback === playback && state.availability.mounted
355	          ? state
356	          : {
357	              availability: state.availability.mounted ? state.availability : { mounted: true },
358	              playback,
359	            }
360	      ));
361	    },
362	    syncSlices: (bootstrap) => {
363	      set((state) => {
364	        const nextData = bootstrap.data ?? state.data;
365	        const nextOps = bootstrap.ops ?? state.ops;
366	        const nextChrome = bootstrap.chrome ?? state.chrome;
367	        const nextPlayback = bootstrap.playback ?? state.playback;
368	        const nextMounted = true;
369	
370	        if (
371	          state.data === nextData
372	          && state.ops === nextOps
373	          && state.chrome === nextChrome
374	          && state.playback === nextPlayback
375	          && state.availability.mounted === nextMounted
376	        ) {
377	          return state;
378	        }
379	
380	        return {
381	          availability: { mounted: nextMounted },
382	          data: nextData,
383	          ops: nextOps,
384	          chrome: nextChrome,
385	          playback: nextPlayback,
386	        };
387	      });
388	    },
389	    resetSlices: () => {
390	      set(() => ({
391	        availability: { mounted: false },
392	        ...createInitialSlices(),
393	      }));
394	    },
395	  }));
396	}

398	const TimelineStoreContext = createContext<TimelineStoreApi | null>(null);
399	const fallbackTimelineStore = createTimelineStore();
```
