```
401	export function TimelineStoreProvider({
402	  store,
403	  children,
404	}: PropsWithChildren<{ store: TimelineStoreApi }>) {
405	  return createElement(TimelineStoreContext.Provider, { value: store }, children);
406	}
407	
408	export function useTimelineStoreApi(): TimelineStoreApi {
409	  const store = useContext(TimelineStoreContext);
410	  if (!store) {
411	    throw new Error('useTimelineStoreApi must be used within TimelineStoreProvider');
412	  }
413	
414	  return store;
415	}

417	export function useTimelineStoreApiSafe(): TimelineStoreApi | null {
418	  return useContext(TimelineStoreContext);

420	
421	function useBoundTimelineStore<T>(
422	  selector: (state: TimelineStoreState) => T,
423	  equalityFn?: (left: T, right: T) => boolean,
424	): T {
425	  return useStoreWithEqualityFn(useTimelineStoreApi(), selector, equalityFn);
426	}
427	
428	function useSafeTimelineStoreValue<T>(
429	  selector: (state: TimelineStoreState) => T,
430	  equalityFn?: (left: T, right: T) => boolean,
431	): T | null {
432	  const providedStore = useTimelineStoreApiSafe();
433	  const store = providedStore ?? fallbackTimelineStore;
434	  const mounted = useStoreWithEqualityFn(store, (state) => state.availability.mounted);
435	  const value = useStoreWithEqualityFn(store, selector, equalityFn);
436	  return providedStore && mounted ? value : null;
437	}
438	
439	export function useTimelineAvailabilityState() {
440	  const providedStore = useTimelineStoreApiSafe();
441	  const store = providedStore ?? fallbackTimelineStore;
442	  const mounted = useStoreWithEqualityFn(store, (state) => state.availability.mounted);
443	
444	  return useMemo(() => ({
445	    hasProvider: providedStore !== null,
446	    mounted: providedStore !== null && mounted,
447	  }), [mounted, providedStore]);
448	}
449

459	    resetSlices: state.resetSlices,
460	  }), shallow);
461	}
```

```
44	function mergeSelectedClips(
45	  timelineClips: SelectedMediaClip[],
46	  galleryClips: SelectedMediaClip[],
47	): SelectedMediaClip[] {
48	  const clipsByUrl = new Map<string, SelectedMediaClip>();
49	
50	  for (const clip of [...timelineClips, ...galleryClips]) {
51	    const existing = clipsByUrl.get(clip.url);
52	    if (existing) {
53	      const preferIncoming = !existing.generationId && Boolean(clip.generationId);
54	      const preferred = preferIncoming ? clip : existing;
55	      const secondary = preferIncoming ? existing : clip;
56	
57	      clipsByUrl.set(clip.url, {
58	        ...preferred,
59	        generationId: preferred.generationId ?? secondary.generationId,
60	        variantId: preferred.variantId ?? secondary.variantId,
61	        isTimelineBacked: preferred.isTimelineBacked || secondary.isTimelineBacked,
62	        shotId: preferred.shotId ?? secondary.shotId,
63	        shotName: preferred.shotName ?? secondary.shotName,
64	        shotSelectionClipCount: preferred.shotSelectionClipCount ?? secondary.shotSelectionClipCount,
65	        trackId: preferred.trackId ?? secondary.trackId,
66	        at: preferred.at ?? secondary.at,
67	        duration: preferred.duration ?? secondary.duration,
68	        [REDACTED] || secondary.assetKey,
69	      });
70	      continue;
71	    }
72	
73	    // Prefer gallery entries when the same URL exists in both panes because they retain
74	    // generationId metadata that timeline clips for that asset may not carry.
75	    clipsByUrl.set(clip.url, clip);
76	  }
77	
78	  return Array.from(clipsByUrl.values());
79	}
```

In /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src, find state that SHOULD probably be in zustand but isn't. Focus on two patterns:

**Pattern 1: React Context holding mutable state that's read by many consumers.**
Grep for `createContext` and for each result, determine: does the context value include `set*` functions or a `dispatch`? Is the value updated frequently (not just once at mount)? Is it consumed by many components (grep for the hook name)? If yes to all three, it's a zustand candidate.

Call out specifically:
- Contexts where `value={{ state, setState }}` pattern appears (classic context-as-store anti-pattern — re-renders all consumers on any change)
- Contexts with >5 consumers that hold mutable state
- "Provider" components more than 100 lines long (usually means embedded state logic worth extracting)

**Pattern 2: Shared state living in useState hoisted to a high component + prop-drilled.**
Grep for components that:
- Have many `useState` calls (>5)
- Pass state + setter as props to multiple child levels
- Have names matching `*Layout.tsx`, `*Shell.tsx`, `*Orchestrator.tsx`, `*Manager.tsx`, `App.tsx`

Also find any module-level `let` variables that act as singletons for app state (e.g. `let currentThing: X | null = null` — usually a lifecycle bug waiting to happen, sometimes a zustand candidate).

For each candidate, report:
- File path + line number + (context name or component name)
- What the state holds (one line)
- Why zustand would be better: "N consumers re-render on any change" / "prop-drilled through M levels" / "module singleton, no React lifecycle"
- Risk/size of migration: `trivial` (<50 lines changed), `moderate` (50-200), `large` (>200 or crosses tool boundaries)

Background: the repo uses zustand for overlayStack, panesStore, and is migrating more under M1a/M1b/M1c commits. Conventions: React Query owns server state; React Context is for DI and provider-scoped features; useState is local UI; zustand is shared client state.

Skip: anything in tests (`*.test.*`), anything in `docs/`, anything in `node_modules/`.

Rank findings by value: high value = many consumers + frequent updates + reasonable migration cost. Limit to top 10 candidates. Under 800 words total.

```
34	// Scroll to top component
35	function ScrollToTop() {
36	  const { pathname } = useLocation();
37	
38	  useEffect(() => {
39	    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
40	    // Also dispatch event for custom scroll containers
41	    dispatchAppEvent('app:scrollToTop', { behavior: 'auto' });
42	  }, [pathname]);
43	
44	  return null;
45	}
46	
47	export const Layout: React.FC = () => {
48	  const { isVideoEditorShellActive } = useVideoEditorRouteState();
49	  const isTasksPaneLocked = usePanesStore((state) => state.isTasksPaneLocked);
50	  const tasksPaneWidth = usePanesStore((state) => state.tasksPaneWidth);
51	  const isShotsPaneLocked = usePanesStore((state) => state.isShotsPaneLocked);
52	  const shotsPaneWidth = usePanesStore((state) => state.shotsPaneWidth);
53	  const isGenerationsPaneLocked = usePanesStore((state) => state.isGenerationsPaneLocked);
54	  const generationsPaneHeight = usePanesStore((state) => state.generationsPaneHeight);
55	
56	  // Mobile detection for split-view scroll handling
57	  const isMobile = useIsMobile();
58	  const isTablet = useIsTablet();
59	  const isSmallMobile = isMobile && !isTablet;
60	
61	  // On small mobile with locked generations pane, create split-view scroll behavior
62	  const isMobileSplitView = isSmallMobile && isGenerationsPaneLocked && !isVideoEditorShellActive;
63	
64	  // Extracted hooks
65	  const { splitViewWrapperRef } = useSplitViewScroll(isMobileSplitView);
66	  const { isAuthenticated, isLoading } = useAuth();
67	  const { isSettingsModalOpen, setIsSettingsModalOpen, settingsInitialTab, settingsCreditsTab, handleOpenSettings } = useSettingsModal();
68	  const { showOnboardingModal, handleOnboardingClose } = useOnboardingFlow();
69	  useResetCurrentShotOnRouteChange();
70	  useGlobalPaneShortcuts();
71	
72	  // Show loading spinner while determining auth state
73	  // (isLoading is always false here because AuthGate gates on it, but kept for safety)
74	  if (isLoading) {
75	    return (
76	      <ReighLoading />
77	    );
78	  }
79	
80	  // Redirect unauthenticated users to home page
81	  // Use /home instead of / to avoid redirect loops in non-WEB environments
82	  // where / is inside Layout
83	  if (!isAuthenticated) {
84	    return <Navigate to="/home" replace state={{ fromProtected: true }} />;
85	  }
86	
87	  // Footer style matches main content margins for side panes
88	  const footerStyle = {
89	    marginRight: isTasksPaneLocked ? `${tasksPaneWidth}px` : '0px',
90	    marginLeft: isShotsPaneLocked ? `${shotsPaneWidth}px` : '0px',
91	    willChange: 'margin',
92	  } as React.CSSProperties;
93	
94	  // Style for the scroll wrapper when in mobile split view
95	  // This wraps both header and content so they scroll together
96	  const splitViewWrapperStyle: React.CSSProperties = isMobileSplitView ? {
97	    height: `calc(100dvh - ${generationsPaneHeight}px)`,
98	    overflowY: 'auto',
99	    overscrollBehavior: 'contain',
100	    WebkitOverflowScrolling: 'touch',
101	  } : {};
102	
103	  const mainContent = (
104	    <LayoutMainContent
105	      isMobileSplitView={isMobileSplitView}
106	      onOpenSettings={handleOpenSettings}
107	    />
108	  );
109	
110	  return (
111	    <AIInputModeProvider>
112	      <div className={cn('flex flex-col', isVideoEditorShellActive && 'h-screen overflow-hidden')}>
113	        <ScrollToTop />
114	        {/* Theme-adaptive background gradient - subtle in dark mode */}
115	        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-gradient-to-br from-background via-secondary/10 to-accent/5 opacity-40 dark:opacity-0 pointer-events-none"></div>
116	
117	        {/* When in mobile split view, wrap header + content in a scroll container */}
118	        {isMobileSplitView ? (
119	          <div ref={splitViewWrapperRef} style={splitViewWrapperStyle}>
120	            {mainContent}
121	          </div>
122	        ) : (
123	          mainContent
124	        )}
125	
126	        <EditorPane />
127	        <TasksPane onOpenSettings={handleOpenSettings} />
128	        <ToolsPane />
129	        <GenerationsPane />
130	
131	        {/* Social Icons Footer */}
132	        {!isVideoEditorShellActive && (
133	          <div
134	            className="relative transition-[margin] duration-300 ease-smooth"
135	            style={footerStyle}
136	          >
137	            <SocialIcons />
138	          </div>
139	        )}
140	
141	        <SettingsModal
142	          isOpen={isSettingsModalOpen}
143	          onOpenChange={setIsSettingsModalOpen}
144	          initialTab={settingsInitialTab}
145	          creditsTab={settingsCreditsTab}
146	        />
147	
148	        {/* Onboarding Modal */}
149	        <OnboardingModal
150	          isOpen={showOnboardingModal}
151	          onClose={handleOnboardingClose}
152	        />
153	
154	        {/* Product Tour - lazy loaded since only needed during onboarding */}
155	        <ChunkLoadErrorBoundary>
156	          <React.Suspense fallback={null}>
157	            <LazyProductTour />
158	          </React.Suspense>
159	        </ChunkLoadErrorBoundary>
160	      </div>
161	    </AIInputModeProvider>
162	  );
163	};
164
```

```
73	function getInspectorTargetForSelection(
74	  selectedClipIds: string[],
75	  selectedTrackId: string | null,
76	): TimelineInspectorTarget {
77	  if (selectedClipIds.length > 1) {
78	    return { kind: 'selection', clipIds: selectedClipIds };
79	  }
80	
81	  if (selectedClipIds.length === 1) {
82	    return { kind: 'clip', clipId: selectedClipIds[0] };
83	  }
84

87	  }
88	
89	  return { kind: 'timeline' };

92	function FullEditorLayout({ timelineId, forceCondensed = false }: { timelineId: string; forceCondensed?: boolean }) {

107	  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

110	  const [isTimelineMaximized, setIsTimelineMaximized] = useState(false);
111	  /** In condensed mode: 'preview' (default) or 'properties' for the right panel. */
112	  const [condensedRightPanel, setCondensedRightPanel] = useState<'preview' | 'properties'>('preview');
113	  const [isMobilePropertiesOpen, setIsMobilePropertiesOpen] = useState(false);
114	  const timelineFps = Math.max(1, editorData.resolvedConfig?.output?.fps ?? 30);
115	  const conflict = useTimelineRealtime({
116	    timelineId,
117	    conflictExhausted: chrome.isConflictExhausted,
118	    onKeepLocalChanges: chrome.retrySaveAfterConflict,
119	    onDiscardRemoteChanges: chrome.reloadFromServer,
120	  });
121	
122	  useEffect(() => {
123	    bootDiagnostics();
124	    MemoryPressureDetector.start();
125	    return MemoryPressureDetector.stop;
126	  }, []);

128	  const handleKeyboardDelete = useCallback(() => {
129	    const mutation = buildKeyboardDeleteMutation(editorData.dataRef.current, editorData.selectedClipIds);
130	    if (mutation) {
131	      editorOps.applyEdit(mutation, { semantic: true });
132	      return;
133	    }
134	
135	    editorOps.handleDeleteClips([...editorData.selectedClipIds]);
136	  }, [editorData.dataRef, editorData.selectedClipIds, editorOps]);

140	    canMoveSelectedClipToTrack: editorData.selectedClipIds.size >= 1,

156	  const onDividerMouseDown = useCallback((event: ReactMouseEvent) => {
157	    event.preventDefault();

159	    const container = containerRef.current;
160	    const divider = dividerRef.current;
161	    if (!container || !divider) {
162	      return;
163	    }
164	
165	    divider.classList.add('is-dragging');
166	    document.body.style.cursor = 'row-resize';
167	    document.body.style.userSelect = 'none';
168	
169	    const onMouseMove = (moveEvent: MouseEvent) => {
170	      const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();
171	      const nextHeight = Math.max(MIN_TIMELINE_HEIGHT, rect.bottom - moveEvent.clientY);
172	      if (rect.height - nextHeight < MIN_PREVIEW_HEIGHT) {
173	        return;
174	      }
175	      container.style.gridTemplateRows = `minmax(0,1fr) auto ${nextHeight}px`;
176	    };
177	
178	    const onMouseUp = () => {
179	      window.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);
180	      window.removeEventListener('mouseup', onMouseUp);
181	      divider.classList.remove('is-dragging');
182	      document.body.style.cursor = '';
183	      document.body.style.userSelect = '';
184	      const match = container.style.gridTemplateRows.match(/(\d+)px$/);
185	      container.style.gridTemplateRows = '';
186	      if (match) {
187	        setTimelineHeight(Number.parseInt(match[1], 10));
188	      }
189	    };
190	
191	    window.addEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);
192	    window.addEventListener('mouseup', onMouseUp);
193	  }, []);

195	  const outputResolution = editorData.resolvedConfig?.output?.resolution;
196	  const aspectRatio = useMemo(() => {
197	    if (!outputResolution) {
198	      return 16 / 9;
199	    }
200	
201	    const { width, height } = parseResolution(outputResolution);
202	    if (!Number.isFinite(width) || !Number.isFinite(height) || width <= 0 || height <= 0) {
203	      return 16 / 9;
204	    }
205	
206	    return width / height;
207	  }, [outputResolution]);
208	  const [tooSmall, setTooSmall] = useState(false);

210	  const condensedSlotRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

219	      return null;
220	    }
221	
222	    const host = document.createElement('div');
223	    host.style.display = 'contents';
224	    return host;
225	  });
226

228	    const el = outerRef.current;

231	    const observer = new ResizeObserver(([entry]) => {

235	      setTooSmall(entry.contentRect.height < minPreviewHeight + CHROME_OVERHEAD);

239	  }, [forceCondensed, aspectRatio, isTimelineMaximized]);

797	function VideoEditorShellComponent({ mode, timelineId, onCreateTimeline }: VideoEditorShellProps) {
798	  if (!timelineId) {

801	    }
802	    return null;
803	  }
804	
805	  return <FullEditorLayout timelineId={timelineId} forceCondensed={mode === 'compact'} />;
806	}
```

> DEVELOPER

In /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src, find zustand misuse and anti-patterns. For each file importing from 'zustand', check for these specific problems:

**A. Duplicating server state.** If a store holds arrays/objects fetched from Supabase (anything with fields like `id`, `created_at`, `*_id`, etc. that look like rows), it's probably fighting React Query. Flag it. Hint: grep for usages of `supabase` or `queryClient` inside the same file as a zustand store creator.

**B. Missing selectors / over-subscription.** Stores consumed via `useStore((s) => s)` (returning the whole state) or `useStore()` with no selector force re-renders on any field change. Grep for both patterns near zustand hook calls.

**C. Missing `shallow` / equality function on composite selectors.** If a selector returns an object/array (e.g. `(s) => ({ a: s.a, b: s.b })`) without `shallow` or a custom equality fn, every render produces a new reference and the consumer re-renders every store change. Look for selector functions returning `{` or `[` literals and check whether the hook call passes `shallow` (from `zustand/shallow`) or an equality function.

**D. Global state that should be per-instance.** Any store that holds state that depends on route params, IDs, or feature context (e.g. "current project ID", "current shot ID") at the module level. If two of those things can be open at once, this is a bug. Look for stores whose fields include `currentXId` / `activeXId` / `selectedXId` where X is a domain entity.

**E. `subscribe` with side effects.** Stores that call `.subscribe()` at module scope or inside a Bootstrap provider to trigger side effects (toasts, fetches, DOM manipulation). These couple the store to the app lifecycle in ways that break under HMR and SSR. Flag them.

**F. Zombie state / missing lifecycle.** State that should reset on navigation/logout/user-switch but lives module-level in zustand with no reset hook. Hint: grep for stores that have no `reset` or `clear` method but hold user-specific data.

**G. Redundant stores.** Two stores holding overlapping or duplicated data (e.g. both have a `currentProject` field).

For each finding report:
- File path + line number
- Which pattern (A–G)
- Concrete evidence (a 2-4 line code excerpt)
- Severity: `bug` (causes visible incorrect behavior), `perf` (unnecessary re-renders), `smell` (maintenance risk, no immediate harm)
- Suggested fix (one line)

Rank by severity. If you find nothing for a pattern, say so — negative findings are useful.

Background: repo uses React Query for server state, Context for DI, useState for local UI. Branch name is `megaplan/m1b-m1c-stores` — they're mid-migration to zustand, so new stores in progress are allowed. Skip tests, docs, node_modules.

Under 900 words.

```
1	import React, { createContext, useContext, ReactNode, useMemo, useState, useEffect } from 'react';
2	import { useListShots, useProjectImageStats } from '@/shared/hooks/shots';
3	import { useProjectSelectionContext } from '@/shared/contexts/ProjectContext';
4	import { Shot } from '@/domains/generation/types';
5	
6	interface ShotsContextType {
7	  shots: Shot[] | undefined;
8	  isLoading: boolean;
9	  error: Error | null;
10	  refetchShots: () => void;
11	  // Stats for SHOT_FILTER.ALL and SHOT_FILTER.NO_SHOT filters
12	  allImagesCount?: number;
13	  noShotImagesCount?: number;
14	}
15	
16	const ShotsContext = createContext<ShotsContextType | undefined>(undefined);
17	
18	interface ShotsProviderProps {
19	  children: ReactNode;
20	}
21	
22	export const ShotsProvider: React.FC<ShotsProviderProps> = ({ children }) => {
23	  const { selectedProjectId } = useProjectSelectionContext();
24	
25	  const [isProjectTransitioning, setIsProjectTransitioning] = useState(false);
26	  const prevProjectIdRef = React.useRef<string | null>(null);
27	
28	  // Load all images per shot (0 = unlimited)
29	  // Previously limited to 2 on mobile for performance, but this broke expand/collapse UI
30	  const maxImagesPerShot = 0;
31	
32	  const { data: shots, isLoading: isShotsLoading, isFetching: isShotsFetching, error: shotsError, refetch } = useListShots(selectedProjectId, { maxImagesPerShot });
33	
34	  // Load project-wide image stats
35	  const { data: projectStats, isLoading: isStatsLoading } = useProjectImageStats(selectedProjectId);
36	
37	  // Set transitioning flag when project changes
38	  useEffect(() => {
39	    if (prevProjectIdRef.current !== null && prevProjectIdRef.current !== selectedProjectId) {
40	      setIsProjectTransitioning(true);
41	    }
42	    prevProjectIdRef.current = selectedProjectId;
43	  }, [selectedProjectId]);
44	
45	  // Clear transitioning flag when new data arrives
46	  useEffect(() => {
47	    if (isProjectTransitioning && !isShotsFetching && shots !== undefined) {
48	      setIsProjectTransitioning(false);
49	    }
50	  }, [isProjectTransitioning, isShotsFetching, shots]);
51	
52	  // Show loading during transition or actual loading
53	  const isLoading = isShotsLoading || isStatsLoading || isProjectTransitioning;
54	  const error = shotsError;
55	
56	  // Return undefined for shots during transition to force skeleton display
57	  // This prevents showing stale data from a previously cached project
58	  const effectiveShots = isProjectTransitioning ? undefined : shots;
59	
60	  // Memoize context value to prevent unnecessary re-renders of consumers
61	  const value = useMemo<ShotsContextType>(() => ({
62	    shots: effectiveShots,
63	    isLoading,
64	    error,
65	    refetchShots: refetch,
66	    allImagesCount: projectStats?.allCount,
67	    noShotImagesCount: projectStats?.noShotCount,
68	  }), [effectiveShots, isLoading, error, refetch, projectStats]);
69	
70	  return (
71	    <ShotsContext.Provider value={value}>
72	      {children}
73	    </ShotsContext.Provider>
74	  );
75	};
76	
77	export const useShots = (): ShotsContextType => {
78	  const context = useContext(ShotsContext);
79	  if (context === undefined) {
80	    throw new Error('useShots must be used within a ShotsProvider');
81	  }
82	  return context;
83	}; 
84
```

```
122	}) => {

124	    <LightboxCoreContext.Provider value={value.core}>
125	      <LightboxMediaContext.Provider value={value.media}>
126	        <LightboxVariantsContext.Provider value={value.variants}>
127	          <LightboxNavigationContext.Provider value={value.navigation}>
128	            {children}
129	          </LightboxNavigationContext.Provider>
130	        </LightboxVariantsContext.Provider>
131	      </LightboxMediaContext.Provider>
132	    </LightboxCoreContext.Provider>
133	  );
134	};
135	
136	// ============================================================================
137	// Consumer Hooks - Safe versions only (return defaults outside provider)
138	// ============================================================================
139	
140	// Note: Non-safe versions (useLightboxState, useLightboxCore, etc.) are not
141	// exported as they are not used. All internal consumers use the Safe versions
142	// for safe operation outside the provider.
143	
144	// ============================================================================
145	// Safe Hooks (for use outside provider - returns defaults)
146	// ============================================================================
147	
148	const EMPTY_CORE: LightboxCoreState = {
149	  onClose: () => {},
150	  readOnly: true,
151	  isMobile: false,
152	  isTabletOrLarger: true,
153	  selectedProjectId: null,
154	  actualGenerationId: null,
155	};
156

166

202	
203	/**
204	 * Safe version of useLightboxCore that returns defaults when used outside provider.
205	 * Use this in components that may render outside the lightbox context.
206	 */
207	export function useLightboxCoreSafe(): LightboxCoreState {
208	  const context = useContext(LightboxCoreContext);
209	  return context ?? EMPTY_CORE;
210	}
211	
212	/**
213	 * Safe version of useLightboxMedia that returns defaults when used outside provider.
214	 */
215	export function useLightboxMediaSafe(): LightboxMediaState {
216	  const context = useContext(LightboxMediaContext);

222	 */

227	
228	/**

230	 */
231	export function useLightboxNavigationSafe(): LightboxNavigationState {
232	  const context = useContext(LightboxNavigationContext);
233	  return context ?? EMPTY_NAVIGATION;
234	}
```

```
1	import { createContext, useContext } from 'react';
2	import type { DataProvider } from '@/tools/video-editor/data/DataProvider';
3	
4	export interface VideoEditorRuntimeContextValue {
5	  provider: DataProvider;
6	  timelineId: string;
7	  userId: string;
8	  timelineName?: string | null;
9	}
10	
11	const DataProviderContext = createContext<VideoEditorRuntimeContextValue | null>(null);
12	
13	export function DataProviderWrapper({
14	  value,
15	  children,
16	}: {
17	  value: VideoEditorRuntimeContextValue;
18	  children: React.ReactNode;
19	}) {
20	  return (
21	    <DataProviderContext.Provider value={value}>
22	      {children}
23	    </DataProviderContext.Provider>
24	  );
25	}
26	
27	export function useVideoEditorRuntime(): VideoEditorRuntimeContextValue {
28	  const context = useContext(DataProviderContext);
29	  if (!context) {
30	    throw new Error('useVideoEditorRuntime must be used within DataProviderWrapper');
31	  }
32	
33	  return context;
34	}
```

```
1	/**
2	 * LightboxShell - Dialog/Overlay container for MediaLightbox
3	 *
4	 * Encapsulates all the complex event handling for the lightbox modal:
5	 * - Dialog root/portal/overlay
6	 * - Pointer/touch/click event handling with z-index awareness
7	 * - Body scroll locking
8	 * - Accessibility elements
9	 *
10	 * Note: Tasks pane controls are handled by the existing PaneControlTab from TasksPane,
11	 * which is visible above the lightbox at z-[100001]. The overlay adjusts its size
12	 * to account for the pane when it's open or locked.
13	 *
14	 * This allows the main MediaLightbox to focus on content orchestration.
15	 */
16	
17	import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
18	import { cn } from '@/shared/components/ui/contracts/cn';
19	import {
20	  LightboxDialog,
21	  LightboxDialogBackdrop,
22	  LightboxDialogDescription,
23	  LightboxDialogPopup,
24	  LightboxDialogPortal,
25	  LightboxDialogTitle,
26	} from '@/shared/components/ui/overlay';
27	import { useLightboxShellInteractionHandlers } from '@/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useLightboxShellInteractionHandlers';
28	import { useLightboxViewportLock } from '@/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useLightboxViewportLock';
29	import { useLightboxPaneLayout } from '@/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useLightboxPaneLayout';
30	import type { OverlayViewportConstraints } from '@/shared/lib/layout/overlayViewportConstraints';
31	import { useRenderBudget } from '@/shared/dev/useRenderBudget';
32	
33	interface LightboxShellProps {
34	  children: React.ReactNode;
35	  onClose: () => void;
36	
37	  // Edit mode
38	  /** Canvas/brush edit overlay is active (inpaint, annotate, reposition, text).
39	   *  Used to allow canvas touch events to pass through. */
40	  hasCanvasOverlay: boolean;
41	  /** Reposition (move) mode — the only edit mode that blocks background click-to-close
42	   *  (because dragging could cause accidental closes). */
43	  isRepositionMode: boolean;
44	
45	  // Responsive
46	  isMobile: boolean;
47	  isTabletOrLarger: boolean;
48	
49	  // Layout constraints from the layout subsystem.
50	  overlayViewport: OverlayViewportConstraints;
51	
52	  // Ref for content
53	  contentRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>;
54	
55	  // Accessibility
56	  accessibilityTitle: string;
57	  accessibilityDescription: string;
58	}
59	
60	export const LightboxShell: React.FC<LightboxShellProps> = ({
61	  children,
62	  onClose,
63	  hasCanvasOverlay,
64	  isRepositionMode,
65	  isMobile,
66	  isTabletOrLarger,
67	  overlayViewport,
68	  contentRef,
69	  accessibilityTitle,
70	  accessibilityDescription,
71	}) => {
72	  useRenderBudget('LightboxShell', 5);
73	  const isActuallyModal = isMobile && !isTabletOrLarger;
74	  useLightboxViewportLock({ isActuallyModal });
75	
76	  const {
77	    handleOverlayPointerDown,
78	    handleOverlayPointerUp,
79	    handleBgPointerDownCapture,
80	    handleBgClickCapture,
81	    handleContentPointerDown,
82	    handleContentClick,
83	    handleTouchEvent,
84	    handleTouchCancel,
85	  } = useLightboxShellInteractionHandlers({
86	    hasCanvasOverlay,
87	    isRepositionMode,
88	    isMobile,
89	    onClose,
90	    popupRef: contentRef,
91	  });
92	
93	  // Focus the content element on mount
94	  useEffect(() => {
95	    contentRef.current?.focus();
96	  }, [contentRef]);
97	
98	  const { overlayStyle, contentStyle } = useLightboxPaneLayout({
99	    overlayViewport,
100	  });
101	
102	  return (
103	    <LightboxDialog
104	      open={true}
105	      modal={isActuallyModal}
106	      disablePointerDismissal
107	      onOpenChange={(_open, eventDetails) => {
108	        // Prevent Base UI from updating internal state (which corrupts controlled open={true})
109	        // and from calling stopPropagation on the native event (which blocks our custom handlers).
110	        // Our own handlers in useLightboxNavigation (Escape) and our overlay dismissal handlers
111	        // manage all closing logic.
112	        eventDetails.cancel();
113	        eventDetails.allowPropagation();
114	      }}
115	    >
116	      <LightboxDialogPortal>
117	        <LightboxDialogBackdrop
118	          data-dialog-backdrop
119	          className={cn(
120	            "fixed bg-black/80 p-0 border-none shadow-none",
121	            "data-[open]:animate-in data-[ending-style]:animate-out data-[ending-style]:fade-out-0 data-[open]:fade-in-0",
122	            !isMobile && "duration-200"
123	          )}
124	          onPointerDown={handleOverlayPointerDown}
125	          onPointerUp={handleOverlayPointerUp}
126	          onTouchStart={handleTouchEvent}
127	          onTouchMove={handleTouchEvent}
128	          onTouchEnd={handleTouchEvent}
129	          onTouchCancel={handleTouchCancel}
130	          style={{
131	            ...overlayStyle,
132	          }}
133	        />
134	
135	        {/* Task pane handle removed - the existing PaneControlTab from TasksPane
136	            is now visible above the lightbox and handles all pane controls.
137	            The overlay correctly accounts for the pane via shouldAccountForTasksPane. */}
138	
139	        <LightboxDialogPopup
140	          ref={contentRef}
141	          data-lightbox-popup
142	          tabIndex={-1}
143	          onPointerDownCapture={handleBgPointerDownCapture}
144	          onClickCapture={handleBgClickCapture}
145	          onPointerDown={handleContentPointerDown}
146	          onClick={handleContentClick}
147	          onTouchStart={handleTouchEvent}
148	          onTouchMove={handleTouchEvent}
149	          onTouchEnd={handleTouchEvent}
150	          onTouchCancel={handleTouchCancel}
151	          className={cn(
152	            "fixed",
153	            isMobile
154	              ? ""
155	              : "duration-200 data-[open]:animate-in data-[ending-style]:animate-out data-[ending-style]:fade-out-0 data-[open]:fade-in-0 data-[ending-style]:zoom-out-95 data-[open]:zoom-in-95",
156	            "p-0 border-none bg-transparent shadow-none",
157	            overlayViewport.needsFullscreenLayout
158	              ? "inset-0 w-full h-full"
159	              : "left-[50%] top-[50%] translate-x-[-50%] translate-y-[-50%] w-auto h-auto data-[ending-style]:slide-out-to-left-1/2 data-[ending-style]:slide-out-to-top-[48%] data-[open]:slide-in-from-left-1/2 data-[open]:slide-in-from-top-[48%]"
160	          )}
161	          style={{
162	            ...contentStyle,
163	          }}
164	        >
165	          {/* Accessibility: Hidden dialog title for screen readers */}
166	          <LightboxDialogTitle className="sr-only">
167	            {accessibilityTitle}
168	          </LightboxDialogTitle>
169	          <LightboxDialogDescription className="sr-only">
170	            {accessibilityDescription}
171	          </LightboxDialogDescription>
172	
173	          {children}
174	        </LightboxDialogPopup>
175	      </LightboxDialogPortal>
176	    </LightboxDialog>
177	  );
178	};
179
```

```
1	import {
2	  createContext,
3	  useState,
4	  useContext,
5	  ReactNode,
6	  useEffect,
7	  useRef,
8	  useCallback,
9	  useMemo
10	} from 'react';
11	import { getSupabaseClient as supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
12	import { UserPreferences } from '@/shared/settings/userPreferences';
13	import { updateToolSettingsSupabase } from '@/shared/hooks/settings/useToolSettings';
14	import { useMobileTimeoutFallback } from '@/shared/hooks/useMobileTimeoutFallback';
15	import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
16	import { SETTINGS_IDS } from '@/shared/lib/settingsIds';
17	import { ToolSettingsError } from '@/shared/settings';
18	import { useAuth } from './AuthContext';
19	import { requireContextValue } from './contextGuard';
20	
21	interface UserSettingsContextType {
22	  /** User settings from the server (cross-device, account-level) */
23	  userSettings: UserPreferences | undefined;
24	  /** Whether settings are currently loading */
25	  isLoadingSettings: boolean;
26	  /** Fetch user settings from the server */
27	  fetchUserSettings: () => Promise<void>;
28	  /** Update user settings (persisted to server, syncs across devices) */
29	  updateUserSettings: (scope: 'user', patch: Partial<UserPreferences>) => Promise<void>;
30	}
31	
32	const UserSettingsContext = createContext<UserSettingsContextType | undefined>(undefined);
33	
34	/**
35	 * UserSettingsProvider manages server-persisted user settings.
36	 * These settings sync across devices (stored in users.settings['user-preferences']).
37	 *
38	 * Features:
39	 * - [MobileStallFix] Timeout and recovery handling for settings loading
40	 * - Optimistic updates for better UX
41	 * - Depends on AuthContext for userId
42	 */
43	export const UserSettingsProvider = ({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) => {
44	  const { userId } = useAuth();
45	
46	  const [userSettings, setUserSettings] = useState<UserPreferences | undefined>(undefined);
47	  const [isLoadingSettings, setIsLoadingSettings] = useState(false);
48	  const userSettingsRef = useRef<UserPreferences | undefined>(undefined);
49	
50	  // Settings fetching
51	  const fetchUserSettings = useCallback(async () => {
52	    if (!userId) return;
53	
54	    setIsLoadingSettings(true);
55	
56	    try {
57	      // Read the settings JSON for the current user
58	      const { data, error } = await supabase().from('users')
59	        .select('settings')
60	        .eq('id', userId)
61	        .single();
62	
63	      if (error) throw error;
64	
65	      const settings = (data?.settings as Record<string, unknown> | null)?.[SETTINGS_IDS.USER_PREFERENCES] as UserPreferences ?? {};
66	      setUserSettings(settings);
67	      userSettingsRef.current = settings;
68	    } catch (error) {
69	      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'UserSettingsContext', showToast: false });
70	      // Set empty settings on error instead of leaving undefined
71	      setUserSettings({});
72	      userSettingsRef.current = {};
73	    } finally {
74	      setIsLoadingSettings(false);
75	    }
76	  }, [userId]);
77	
78	  // Update user settings directly using the global write queue
79	  const updateUserSettings = useCallback(async (_scope: 'user', patch: Partial<UserPreferences>) => {
80	    if (!userId) {
81	      const authError = new ToolSettingsError(
82	        'auth_required',
83	        'Authentication required for user settings update',
84	      );
85	      normalizeAndPresentError(authError, {
86	        context: 'UserSettingsContext.updateUserSettings',
87	        showToast: false,
88	      });
89	      throw authError;
90	    }
91	
92	    try {
93	      // Use the global queue - it handles read-modify-write internally
94	      await updateToolSettingsSupabase({
95	        scope: 'user',
96	        id: userId,
97	        toolId: SETTINGS_IDS.USER_PREFERENCES,
98	        patch,
99	      });
100	
101	      // Update local state optimistically
102	      setUserSettings(prev => {
103	        const merged = { ...prev, ...patch };
104	        userSettingsRef.current = merged;
105	        return merged;
106	      });
107	    } catch (error) {
108	      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'UserSettingsContext', showToast: false });
109	      throw error;
110	    }
111	  }, [userId]);
112	
113	  // Fetch settings when user changes
114	  useEffect(() => {
115	    if (userId) {
116	      fetchUserSettings();
117	    } else {
118	      setUserSettings(undefined);
119	      userSettingsRef.current = undefined;
120	      setIsLoadingSettings(false);
121	    }
122	  }, [userId, fetchUserSettings]);
123	
124	  // [MobileStallFix] Fallback recovery: set empty defaults if settings loading stalls
125	  const handleSettingsTimeout = useCallback(() => {
126	    setIsLoadingSettings(false);
127	    setUserSettings({});
128	    userSettingsRef.current = {};
129	  }, []);
130	
131	  useMobileTimeoutFallback({
132	    isLoading: isLoadingSettings,
133	    onTimeout: handleSettingsTimeout,
134	    mobileTimeoutMs: 10000,
135	    desktopTimeoutMs: 5000,
136	    enabled: !!userId,
137	  });
138	
139	  const contextValue = useMemo(
140	    () => ({
141	      userSettings,
142	      isLoadingSettings,
143	      fetchUserSettings,
144	      updateUserSettings,
145	    }),
146	    [userSettings, isLoadingSettings, fetchUserSettings, updateUserSettings]
147	  );
148	
149	  return (
150	    <UserSettingsContext.Provider value={contextValue}>
151	      {children}
152	    </UserSettingsContext.Provider>
153	  );
154	};
155	
156	/**
157	 * Hook to access user settings (server-persisted, cross-device).
158	 *
159	 * For device-local preferences (like sound on/off), use useLocalPreferences instead.
160	 *
161	 * @returns { userSettings, isLoadingSettings, fetchUserSettings, updateUserSettings }
162	 */
163	export const useUserSettings = () => {
164	  return requireContextValue(
165	    useContext(UserSettingsContext),
166	    'useUserSettings',
167	    'UserSettingsProvider',
168	  );
169	};
170
```

```
1	import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useCallback, useMemo, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
2	
3	// Auto-cleanup stale incoming tasks after this many seconds
4	const STALE_TASK_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 90;
5	
6	/**
7	 * Generic incoming task - represents a task that is being prepared/created
8	 * but hasn't yet appeared in the database. This allows for immediate UI feedback
9	 * while background operations (like AI prompt generation) complete.
10	 *
11	 * Designed to work with any task type, not just image generation.
12	 */
13	export interface IncomingTask {
14	  id: string;
15	  startedAt: Date;
16	  taskType: string;       // e.g., 'image_generation', 'travel_video', 'upscale', etc.
17	  label: string;          // Display text (e.g., "cinematic shot of...", "Travel video")
18	  expectedCount?: number; // Optional: expected number of tasks to create
19	  taskIds?: string[];     // Real task IDs, set after creation completes (for exact matching)
20	}
21	
22	interface IncomingTasksContextValue {
23	  /** List of all incoming tasks currently being prepared */
24	  incomingTasks: IncomingTask[];
25	
26	  /** Add a new incoming task. Returns the generated ID for later removal. */
27	  addIncomingTask: (task: Omit<IncomingTask, 'id' | 'startedAt'>) => string;
28	
29	  /** Remove an incoming task by ID (call when real tasks appear or on error) */
30	  removeIncomingTask: (id: string) => void;
31	
32	  /**
33	   * Resolve an incoming task with real task IDs from the database.
34	   * The placeholder will be hidden once any of these IDs appear in the task list.
35	   */
36	  resolveTaskIds: (id: string, taskIds: string[]) => void;
37	
38	  /** Quick check if there are any incoming tasks */
39	  hasIncomingTasks: boolean;
40	
41	  /**
42	   * Cancel a single incoming task. Removes it from the UI immediately and
43	   * marks its ID so the in-flight create() call can detect the cancellation.
44	   */
45	  cancelIncoming: (id: string) => void;
46	
47	  /**
48	   * Cancel all incoming tasks. Removes them from the UI immediately and
49	   * marks their IDs so in-flight create() calls can detect the cancellation.
50	   */
51	  cancelAllIncoming: () => void;
52	
53	  /** Check if an incoming task was cancelled (for in-flight create() calls). */
54	  wasCancelled: (id: string) => boolean;
55	
56	  /** Clear the cancelled flag for a task (after handling the cancellation). */
57	  acknowledgeCancellation: (id: string) => void;
58	}
59	
60	const IncomingTasksContext = createContext<IncomingTasksContextValue | null>(null);
61	
62	let idCounter = 0;
63	const generateId = () => `incoming-${Date.now()}-${++idCounter}`;
64	
65	export const IncomingTasksProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
66	  const [incomingTasks, setIncomingTasks] = useState<IncomingTask[]>([]);
67	
68	  // Track cancelled incoming task IDs so in-flight create() calls can detect cancellation.
69	  // Uses a ref because the check happens inside async closures that need the latest value.
70	  const cancelledIdsRef = useRef(new Set<string>());
71	
72	  const addIncomingTask = useCallback((task: Omit<IncomingTask, 'id' | 'startedAt'>): string => {
73	    const id = generateId();
74	    const newTask: IncomingTask = {
75	      ...task,
76	      id,
77	      startedAt: new Date(),
78	    };
79	
80	    setIncomingTasks(prev => [newTask, ...prev]);
81	    return id;
82	  }, []);
83	
84	  const removeIncomingTask = useCallback((id: string) => {
85	    setIncomingTasks(prev => prev.filter(task => task.id !== id));
86	  }, []);
87	
88	  const resolveTaskIds = useCallback((id: string, taskIds: string[]) => {
89	    setIncomingTasks(prev =>
90	      prev.map(task => task.id === id ? { ...task, taskIds } : task)
91	    );
92	  }, []);
93	
94	  const cancelIncoming = useCallback((id: string) => {
95	    cancelledIdsRef.current.add(id);
96	    setIncomingTasks(prev => prev.filter(task => task.id !== id));
97	  }, []);
98	
99	  const cancelAllIncoming = useCallback(() => {
100	    setIncomingTasks(prev => {
101	      prev.forEach(t => cancelledIdsRef.current.add(t.id));
102	      return [];
103	    });
104	  }, []);
105	
106	  const wasCancelled = useCallback((id: string): boolean => {
107	    return cancelledIdsRef.current.has(id);
108	  }, []);
109	
110	  const acknowledgeCancellation = useCallback((id: string) => {
111	    cancelledIdsRef.current.delete(id);
112	  }, []);
113	
114	  const hasIncomingTasks = incomingTasks.length > 0;
115	
116	  // Auto-cleanup stale incoming tasks that have been sitting too long
117	  // This prevents stuck placeholders if removeIncomingTask is never called (e.g., due to errors)
118	  useEffect(() => {
119	    if (incomingTasks.length === 0) return;
120	
121	    const interval = setInterval(() => {
122	      const now = new Date();
123	      setIncomingTasks(prev => {
124	        const staleTasks = prev.filter(task => {
125	          const ageSeconds = (now.getTime() - task.startedAt.getTime()) / 1000;
126	          return ageSeconds > STALE_TASK_TIMEOUT_SECONDS;
127	        });
128	
129	        if (staleTasks.length > 0) {
130	          return prev.filter(task => {
131	            const ageSeconds = (now.getTime() - task.startedAt.getTime()) / 1000;
132	            return ageSeconds <= STALE_TASK_TIMEOUT_SECONDS;
133	          });
134	        }
135	        return prev;
136	      });
137	    }, 5000); // Check every 5 seconds
138	
139	    return () => clearInterval(interval);
140	  }, [incomingTasks.length]);
141	
142	  const value = useMemo(() => ({
143	    incomingTasks,
144	    addIncomingTask,
145	    removeIncomingTask,
146	    resolveTaskIds,
147	    hasIncomingTasks,
148	    cancelIncoming,
149	    cancelAllIncoming,
150	    wasCancelled,
151	    acknowledgeCancellation,
152	  }), [incomingTasks, addIncomingTask, removeIncomingTask, resolveTaskIds, hasIncomingTasks, cancelIncoming, cancelAllIncoming, wasCancelled, acknowledgeCancellation]);
153	
154	  return (
155	    <IncomingTasksContext.Provider value={value}>
156	      {children}
157	    </IncomingTasksContext.Provider>
158	  );
159	};
160	
161	export const useIncomingTasks = (): IncomingTasksContextValue => {
162	  const context = useContext(IncomingTasksContext);
163	  if (!context) {
164	    throw new Error('useIncomingTasks must be used within an IncomingTasksProvider');
165	  }
166	  return context;
167	};
168
```

```
302	export function createTimelineStore(bootstrap?: Partial<TimelineStoreBootstrap>): TimelineStoreApi {
303	  const initialSlices = createInitialSlices();
304	  const seededSlices = {
305	    data: bootstrap?.data ?? initialSlices.data,
306	    ops: bootstrap?.ops ?? initialSlices.ops,
307	    chrome: bootstrap?.chrome ?? initialSlices.chrome,
308	    playback: bootstrap?.playback ?? initialSlices.playback,
309	  };
310	  const initialMounted = bootstrap !== undefined;
311	
312	  return createStore<TimelineStoreState>((set) => ({
313	    availability: { mounted: initialMounted },
314	    ...seededSlices,
315	    setMounted: (mounted) => {
316	      set((state) => (
317	        state.availability.mounted === mounted
318	          ? state
319	          : { availability: { mounted } }
320	      ));
321	    },
322	    syncDataSlice: (data) => {
323	      set((state) => (
324	        state.data === data && state.availability.mounted
325	          ? state
326	          : {
327	              availability: state.availability.mounted ? state.availability : { mounted: true },
328	              data,
329	            }
330	      ));
331	    },
332	    syncOpsSlice: (ops) => {
333	      set((state) => (
334	        state.ops === ops && state.availability.mounted
335	          ? state
336	          : {
337	              availability: state.availability.mounted ? state.availability : { mounted: true },
338	              ops,
339	            }
340	      ));
341	    },
342	    syncChromeSlice: (chrome) => {
343	      set((state) => (
344	        state.chrome === chrome && state.availability.mounted
345	          ? state
346	          : {
347	              availability: state.availability.mounted ? state.availability : { mounted: true },
348	              chrome,
349	            }
350	      ));
351	    },
352	    syncPlaybackSlice: (playback) => {
353	      set((state) => (
354	        state.playback === playback && state.availability.mounted
355	          ? state
356	          : {
357	              availability: state.availability.mounted ? state.availability : { mounted: true },
358	              playback,
359	            }
360	      ));
361	    },
362	    syncSlices: (bootstrap) => {
363	      set((state) => {
364	        const nextData = bootstrap.data ?? state.data;
365	        const nextOps = bootstrap.ops ?? state.ops;
366	        const nextChrome = bootstrap.chrome ?? state.chrome;
367	        const nextPlayback = bootstrap.playback ?? state.playback;
368	        const nextMounted = true;
369	
370	        if (
371	          state.data === nextData
372	          && state.ops === nextOps
373	          && state.chrome === nextChrome
374	          && state.playback === nextPlayback
375	          && state.availability.mounted === nextMounted
376	        ) {
377	          return state;
378	        }
379	
380	        return {
381	          availability: { mounted: nextMounted },
382	          data: nextData,
383	          ops: nextOps,
384	          chrome: nextChrome,
385	          playback: nextPlayback,
386	        };
387	      });
388	    },
389	    resetSlices: () => {
390	      set(() => ({
391	        availability: { mounted: false },
392	        ...createInitialSlices(),
393	      }));
394	    },
395	  }));
396	}

398	const TimelineStoreContext = createContext<TimelineStoreApi | null>(null);
399	const fallbackTimelineStore = createTimelineStore();
400	
401	export function TimelineStoreProvider({
402	  store,
403	  children,
404	}: PropsWithChildren<{ store: TimelineStoreApi }>) {
405	  return createElement(TimelineStoreContext.Provider, { value: store }, children);
406	}

408	export function useTimelineStoreApi(): TimelineStoreApi {
409	  const store = useContext(TimelineStoreContext);
410	  if (!store) {
411	    throw new Error('useTimelineStoreApi must be used within TimelineStoreProvider');
412	  }
413	
414	  return store;
415	}

417	export function useTimelineStoreApiSafe(): TimelineStoreApi | null {
418	  return useContext(TimelineStoreContext);
419	}
420	
421	function useBoundTimelineStore<T>(
422	  selector: (state: TimelineStoreState) => T,
423	  equalityFn?: (left: T, right: T) => boolean,
424	): T {
425	  return useStoreWithEqualityFn(useTimelineStoreApi(), selector, equalityFn);
426	}
427	
428	function useSafeTimelineStoreValue<T>(
429	  selector: (state: TimelineStoreState) => T,
430	  equalityFn?: (left: T, right: T) => boolean,
431	): T | null {
432	  const providedStore = useTimelineStoreApiSafe();
433	  const store = providedStore ?? fallbackTimelineStore;
434	  const mounted = useStoreWithEqualityFn(store, (state) => state.availability.mounted);
435	  const value = useStoreWithEqualityFn(store, selector, equalityFn);
436	  return providedStore && mounted ? value : null;
437	}
438	
439	export function useTimelineAvailabilityState() {
440	  const providedStore = useTimelineStoreApiSafe();
441	  const store = providedStore ?? fallbackTimelineStore;
442	  const mounted = useStoreWithEqualityFn(store, (state) => state.availability.mounted);
443	
444	  return useMemo(() => ({
445	    hasProvider: providedStore !== null,
446	    mounted: providedStore !== null && mounted,
447	  }), [mounted, providedStore]);
448	}
449	
450	export function useTimelineStoreLifecycle() {
451	  return useBoundTimelineStore((state) => ({
452	    mounted: state.availability.mounted,
453	    setMounted: state.setMounted,
454	    syncDataSlice: state.syncDataSlice,
455	    syncOpsSlice: state.syncOpsSlice,
456	    syncChromeSlice: state.syncChromeSlice,
457	    syncPlaybackSlice: state.syncPlaybackSlice,
458	    syncSlices: state.syncSlices,
459	    resetSlices: state.resetSlices,
460	  }), shallow);
461	}
```

```
88	function asError(error: unknown): Error {
89	  return error instanceof Error ? error : new Error(String(error));
90	}
91	
92	function uniqueKeys<T>(keys: Iterable<keyof T>): Array<keyof T> {
93	  return Array.from(new Set(keys));
94	}
95	
96	function getChangedKeys<T extends object>(current: T, baseline: T): Array<keyof T> {
97	  const keys = new Set<keyof T>([
98	    ...(Object.keys(current) as Array<keyof T>),
99	    ...(Object.keys(baseline) as Array<keyof T>),
100	  ]);
101	
102	  return Array.from(keys).filter((key) => !deepEqual(current[key], baseline[key]));
103	}
104	
105	export function createEntityStore<T extends object>(
106	  config: EntityStoreConfig<T>
107	): EntityStoreApi<T> {
108	  const debounceMs = config.persistenceDebounceMs ?? 300;
109	  const textFieldKeySet = new Set<keyof T>(config.textFieldKeys ?? []);
110	  const pendingTimers = new Map<string, TimeoutHandle>();
111	  const loadPromises = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();
112	  const placeholderEntities = new Map<string, EntityRecord<T>>();
113	
114	  const createRecord = (seed: T, overrides?: Partial<EntityRecord<T>>): EntityRecord<T> => ({
115	    settings: cloneValue(seed),
116	    status: 'idle',
117	    error: null,
118	    hasPersistedData: false,
119	    cleanSnapshot: cloneValue(seed),
120	    savedSettings: cloneValue(seed),
121	    pendingTextFieldKeys: [],
122	    loaded: false,
123	    ...overrides,
124	  });
125	
126	  const getPlaceholder = (entityId: string): EntityRecord<T> => {
127	    const existing = placeholderEntities.get(entityId);
128	    if (existing) {
129	      return existing;
130	    }
131	
132	    const placeholder = createRecord(config.defaults);
133	    placeholderEntities.set(entityId, placeholder);
134	    return placeholder;
135	  };
136	
137	  const getSeed = ({ db, lastUsed }: Omit<EntityBootstrapInput<T>, 'entityId'>): T => {
138	    const source = db ?? lastUsed;
139	    if (!source) return cloneValue(config.defaults);
140	    // Backfill any missing keys from defaults so partial DB rows (saved before a field was added)
141	    // don't surface `undefined` to consumers that read the field directly.
142	    return { ...cloneValue(config.defaults), ...source } as T;
143	  };
144	
145	  const store = createStore<EntityStoreState<T>>((set, get) => {
146	    const clearPendingTimer = (entityId: string): void => {
147	      const timer = pendingTimers.get(entityId);
148	      if (!timer) {
149	        return;
150	      }
151	      clearTimeout(timer);
152	      pendingTimers.delete(entityId);
153	    };
154	
155	    const ensureStoredEntity = (entityId: string): EntityRecord<T> => {
156	      const existing = get().entities[entityId];
157	      if (existing) {
158	        return existing;
159	      }
160	
161	      const nextRecord = createRecord(config.defaults);
162	      set((state) => ({
163	        entities: {
164	          ...state.entities,
165	          [entityId]: nextRecord,
166	        },
167	      }));
168	      return get().entities[entityId];
169	    };
170	
171	    const isDirtyRecord = (record: EntityRecord<T> | undefined): boolean => {
172	      if (!record) {
173	        return false;
174	      }
175	      return !deepEqual(record.settings, record.cleanSnapshot);
176	    };
177	
178	    const hasPendingPersistenceRecord = (
179	      entityId: string,
180	      record: EntityRecord<T> | undefined
181	    ): boolean => {
182	      if (!record) {
183	        return false;
184	      }
185	
186	      return (
187	        record.status === 'saving' ||
188	        pendingTimers.has(entityId) ||
189	        record.pendingTextFieldKeys.length > 0
190	      );
191	    };
192	
193	    const persistEntity = async (entityId: string): Promise<void> => {
194	      clearPendingTimer(entityId);
195	      const current = get().entities[entityId] ?? ensureStoredEntity(entityId);
196	
197	      if (!isDirtyRecord(current)) {
198	        if (current.pendingTextFieldKeys.length > 0) {
199	          set((state) => ({
200	            entities: {
201	              ...state.entities,
202	              [entityId]: {
203	                ...state.entities[entityId],
204	                pendingTextFieldKeys: [],
205	              },
206	            },
207	          }));
208	        }
209	        return;
210	      }
211	
212	      const snapshot = cloneValue(current.settings);
213	      set((state) => {
214	        const entity = state.entities[entityId];
215	        if (!entity) {
216	          return state;
217	        }
218	
219	        return {
220	          entities: {
221	            ...state.entities,
222	            [entityId]: {
223	              ...entity,
224	              status: 'saving',
225	              error: null,
226	            },
227	          },
228	        };
229	      });
230	
231	      try {
232	        await config.save(entityId, cloneValue(snapshot));
233	
234	        set((state) => {
235	          const entity = state.entities[entityId];
236	          if (!entity) {
237	            return state;
238	          }
239	
240	          const stillDirty = !deepEqual(entity.settings, snapshot);
241	
242	          return {
243	            entities: {
244	              ...state.entities,
245	              [entityId]: {
246	                ...entity,
247	                status: 'ready',
248	                error: null,
249	                hasPersistedData: true,
250	                cleanSnapshot: snapshot,
251	                savedSettings: snapshot,
252	                pendingTextFieldKeys: stillDirty ? entity.pendingTextFieldKeys : [],
253	              },
254	            },
255	          };
256	        });
257	      } catch (error) {
258	        set((state) => {
259	          const entity = state.entities[entityId];
260	          if (!entity) {
261	            return state;
262	          }
263	
264	          return {
265	            entities: {
266	              ...state.entities,
267	              [entityId]: {
268	                ...entity,
269	                status: 'error',
270	                error: asError(error),
271	              },
272	            },
273	          };
274	        });
275	        throw error;
276	      }
277	    };
278	
279	    const scheduleSave = (entityId: string): void => {
280	      clearPendingTimer(entityId);
281	      pendingTimers.set(
282	        entityId,
283	        setTimeout(() => {
284	          pendingTimers.delete(entityId);
285	          void persistEntity(entityId);
286	        }, debounceMs)
287	      );
288	    };
289	
290	    const applyPatch = (
291	      entityId: string,
292	      patch: Partial<T>,
293	      options?: { deferKeys?: readonly (keyof T)[] }
294	    ): void => {
295	      const current = get().entities[entityId] ?? ensureStoredEntity(entityId);
296	      const patchEntries = Object.entries(patch) as Array<[keyof T, T[keyof T]]>;
297	      const changedEntries = patchEntries.filter(([key, value]) => !deepEqual(current.settings[key], value));
298	
299	      if (changedEntries.length === 0) {
300	        return;
301	      }
302	
303	      const nextSettings = {
304	        ...current.settings,
305	        ...Object.fromEntries(changedEntries),
306	      } as T;
307	      const explicitDeferredKeys = new Set<keyof T>(options?.deferKeys ?? []);
308	      const deferredKeys = uniqueKeys(
309	        changedEntries
310	          .map(([key]) => key)
311	          .filter((key) => textFieldKeySet.has(key) || explicitDeferredKeys.has(key))
312	      );
313	      const shouldScheduleSave =
314	        current.status !== 'loading'
315	        && changedEntries.some(([key]) => !deferredKeys.includes(key));
316	
317	      set((state) => {
318	        const entity = state.entities[entityId] ?? current;
319	        return {
320	          entities: {
321	            ...state.entities,
322	            [entityId]: {
323	              ...entity,
324	              settings: nextSettings,
325	              status:
326	                entity.status === 'loading'
327	                  ? 'loading'
328	                  : entity.status === 'saving'
329	                    ? 'saving'
330	                    : 'ready',
331	              error: null,
332	              pendingTextFieldKeys: uniqueKeys([
333	                ...entity.pendingTextFieldKeys,
334	                ...deferredKeys,
335	              ]),
336	            },
337	          },
338	        };
339	      });
340	
341	      if (shouldScheduleSave) {
342	        scheduleSave(entityId);
343	      }
344	    };
345	
346	    return {
347	      entities: {},
348	
349	      bootstrapEntity: ({ entityId, db, lastUsed }) => {
350	        clearPendingTimer(entityId);
351	        const seed = getSeed({ db, lastUsed });
352	        const current = get().entities[entityId];
353	        const currentHasPendingPersistence = hasPendingPersistenceRecord(entityId, current);
354	        const currentIsDirty = isDirtyRecord(current);
355	        const shouldPreserveLocalState = !!current && (currentHasPendingPersistence || currentIsDirty);
356	        const changedKeys = shouldPreserveLocalState
357	          ? getChangedKeys(current.settings, seed)
358	          : [];
359	        const pendingTextFieldKeys = shouldPreserveLocalState
360	          ? uniqueKeys([
361	              ...current.pendingTextFieldKeys,
362	              ...changedKeys.filter((key) => textFieldKeySet.has(key)),
363	            ])
364	          : [];
365	        const shouldScheduleSave = shouldPreserveLocalState
366	          && changedKeys.some((key) => !pendingTextFieldKeys.includes(key));
367	
368	        set((state) => ({
369	          entities: {
370	            ...state.entities,
371	            [entityId]: createRecord(seed, shouldPreserveLocalState
372	              ? {
373	                  settings: cloneValue(current.settings),
374	                  status: 'ready',
375	                  error: null,
376	                  hasPersistedData: db !== null,
377	                  cleanSnapshot: cloneValue(seed),
378	                  savedSettings: cloneValue(seed),
379	                  pendingTextFieldKeys,
380	                  loaded: true,
381	                }
382	              : {
383	                  status: 'ready',
384	                  error: null,
385	                  hasPersistedData: db !== null,
386	                  savedSettings: cloneValue(seed),
387	                  pendingTextFieldKeys: [],
388	                  loaded: true,
389	                }),
390	          },
391	        }));
392	
393	        if (shouldScheduleSave) {
394	          scheduleSave(entityId);
395	        }
396	      },
397	
398	      ensureEntity: async (entityId) => {
399	        const current = get().entities[entityId];
400	        if (current?.loaded) {
401	          return;
402	        }
403	
404	        const inFlight = loadPromises.get(entityId);
405	        if (inFlight) {
406	          return inFlight;
407	        }
408	
409	        set((state) => {
410	          const existing = state.entities[entityId] ?? createRecord(config.defaults);
411	          return {
412	            entities: {
413	              ...state.entities,
414	              [entityId]: {
415	                ...existing,
416	                status: 'loading',
417	                error: null,
418	              },
419	            },
420	          };
421	        });
422	
423	        const loadPromise = config
424	          .load(entityId)
425	          .then((result) => {
426	            get().bootstrapEntity({
427	              entityId,
428	              db: result.db,
429	              lastUsed: result.lastUsed,
430	            });
431	          })
432	          .catch((error) => {
433	            set((state) => {
434	              const existing = state.entities[entityId] ?? createRecord(config.defaults);
435	              return {
436	                entities: {
437	                  ...state.entities,
438	                  [entityId]: {
439	                    ...existing,
440	                    status: 'error',
441	                    error: asError(error),
442	                  },
443	                },
444	              };
445	            });
446	            throw error;
447	          })
448	          .finally(() => {
449	            loadPromises.delete(entityId);
450	          });
451	
452	        loadPromises.set(entityId, loadPromise);
453	        return loadPromise;
454	      },
455	
456	      reloadEntity: async (entityId) => {
457	        clearPendingTimer(entityId);
458	        loadPromises.delete(entityId);
459	        set((state) => {
460	          const existing = state.entities[entityId] ?? createRecord(config.defaults);
461	          return {
462	            entities: {
463	              ...state.entities,
464	              [entityId]: {
465	                ...existing,
466	                status: 'loading',
467	                error: null,
468	                loaded: false,
469	              },
470	            },
471	          };
472	        });
473	
474	        await get().ensureEntity(entityId);
475	      },
476	
477	      updateField: (entityId, key, value, options) => {
478	        get().updateFields(
479	          entityId,
480	          { [key]: value } as Partial<T>,
481	          options?.deferPersistence ? { deferKeys: [key] } : undefined
482	        );
483	      },
484	
485	      updateFields: (entityId, patch, options) => {
486	        applyPatch(entityId, patch, options);
487	      },
488	
489	      updateTextField: (entityId, key, value) => {
490	        get().updateFields(entityId, { [key]: value } as Partial<T>, { deferKeys: [key] });
491	      },
492	
493	      flushTextFields: async (entityId) => {
494	        const entity = get().entities[entityId];
495	        if (!entity) {
496	          return;
497	        }
498	
499	        if (!hasPendingPersistenceRecord(entityId, entity) && !isDirtyRecord(entity)) {
500	          return;
501	        }
502	
503	        await persistEntity(entityId);
504	      },
505	
506	      save: async (entityId) => {
507	        await persistEntity(entityId);
508	      },
509	
510	      saveImmediate: async (entityId) => {
511	        await persistEntity(entityId);
512	      },
513	
514	      revert: (entityId) => {
515	        clearPendingTimer(entityId);
516	        const current = get().entities[entityId] ?? ensureStoredEntity(entityId);
517	        set((state) => ({
518	          entities: {
519	            ...state.entities,
520	            [entityId]: {
521	              ...state.entities[entityId],
522	              settings: cloneValue(current.cleanSnapshot),
523	              status: 'ready',
524	              error: null,
525	              pendingTextFieldKeys: [],
526	            },
527	          },
528	        }));
529	      },
530	
531	      reset: (entityId, nextSettings) => {
532	        clearPendingTimer(entityId);
533	        const current = get().entities[entityId] ?? ensureStoredEntity(entityId);
534	        const resetSnapshot = cloneValue(nextSettings ?? config.defaults);
535	        set((state) => ({
536	          entities: {
537	            ...state.entities,
538	            [entityId]: {
539	              ...state.entities[entityId],
540	              settings: resetSnapshot,
541	              cleanSnapshot: cloneValue(resetSnapshot),
542	              savedSettings: cloneValue(resetSnapshot),
543	              status: 'ready',
544	              error: null,
545	              hasPersistedData: current.hasPersistedData,
546	              pendingTextFieldKeys: [],
547	            },
548	          },
549	        }));
550	      },
551	
552	      isDirty: (entityId) => {
553	        return isDirtyRecord(get().entities[entityId]);
554	      },
555	
556	      hasPendingPersistence: (entityId) => {
557	        return hasPendingPersistenceRecord(entityId, get().entities[entityId]);
558	      },
559	
560	      syncExternalEntity: ({ entityId, db, lastUsed }) => {
561	        const current = get().entities[entityId];
562	        if (!current) {
563	          get().bootstrapEntity({ entityId, db, lastUsed });
564	          return true;
565	        }
566	
567	        if (isDirtyRecord(current) || hasPendingPersistenceRecord(entityId, current)) {
568	          return false;
569	        }
570	
571	        clearPendingTimer(entityId);
572	        get().bootstrapEntity({ entityId, db, lastUsed });
573	        return true;
574	      },
575	    };
576	  });
577	
578	  function useEntity(entityId: string): EntityView<T> {
579	    const entitySlice = useStoreWithEqualityFn(
580	      store,
581	      (state) => {
582	        const entity = state.entities[entityId] ?? getPlaceholder(entityId);
583	        return {
584	          settings: entity.settings,
585	          status: entity.status,
586	          error: entity.error,
587	          hasPersistedData: entity.hasPersistedData,
588	        };
589	      },
590	      shallow
591	    );
592	
593	    useEffect(() => {
594	      void store.getState().ensureEntity(entityId).catch(() => {});
595	    }, [entityId]);
596	
597	    const actions = useMemo(
598	      () => ({
599	        updateField: <K extends keyof T>(key: K, value: T[K]) => {
600	          store.getState().updateField(entityId, key, value);
601	        },
602	        updateFields: (patch: Partial<T>) => {
603	          store.getState().updateFields(entityId, patch);
604	        },
605	        updateTextField: <K extends keyof T>(key: K, value: T[K]) => {
606	          store.getState().updateTextField(entityId, key, value);
607	        },
608	        flushTextFields: () => store.getState().flushTextFields(entityId),
609	        save: () => store.getState().save(entityId),
610	        saveImmediate: () => store.getState().saveImmediate(entityId),
611	        revert: () => {
612	          store.getState().revert(entityId);
613	        },
614	        reset: (nextSettings?: T) => {
615	          store.getState().reset(entityId, nextSettings);
616	        },
617	      }),
618	      [entityId]
619	    );
620	
621	    return useMemo(
622	      () => ({
623	        ...entitySlice,
624	        ...actions,
625	      }),
626	      [actions, entitySlice]
627	    );
628	  }
629	
630	  return {
631	    useEntity,
632	    getState: store.getState,
633	  };
634	}
635
```

```
39	    tasks: EMPTY_TASKS,
40	    taskGenerationIds: EMPTY_GENERATION_IDS,

46	function isPendingTaskStatus(status: Task['status']): boolean {
47	  return status === TASK_STATUS.QUEUED || status === TASK_STATUS.IN_PROGRESS;
48	}
49	
50	function addGenerationId(target: Set<string>, value: unknown): void {
51	  if (typeof value !== 'string') {
52	    return;
53	  }
54	
55	  const trimmed = value.trim();
56	  if (trimmed) {
57	    target.add(trimmed);
58	  }
59	}
60	
61	function addGenerationIdsFromArray(target: Set<string>, value: unknown): void {
62	  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
63	    return;
64	  }
65	
66	  value.forEach((item) => addGenerationId(target, item));
67	}
68	
69	function addGenerationIdsFromRecord(target: Set<string>, value: unknown): void {
70	  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) {
71	    return;
72	  }
73	
74	  const record = value as Record<string, unknown>;
75	  addGenerationId(target, record.based_on);
76	  addGenerationId(target, record.source_generation_id);
77	  addGenerationId(target, record.generation_id);
78	  addGenerationId(target, record.input_generation_id);
79	  addGenerationId(target, record.parent_generation_id);
80	  addGenerationId(target, record.start_image_generation_id);
81	  addGenerationId(target, record.end_image_generation_id);
82	  addGenerationId(target, record.pair_shot_generation_id);
83	  addGenerationIdsFromArray(target, record.input_image_generation_ids);
84	  addGenerationIdsFromArray(target, record.pair_shot_generation_ids);
85	}

87	export function collectTaskGenerationIds(task: Task): readonly string[] {
88	  if (!isPendingTaskStatus(task.status)) {
89	    return EMPTY_STRING_ARRAY;
90	  }
91	
92	  const generationIds = new Set<string>();
93	  addGenerationIdsFromRecord(generationIds, task.params);
94	
95	  const params = task.params as Record<string, unknown>;
96	  addGenerationIdsFromRecord(generationIds, params.orchestrator_details);
97	  addGenerationIdsFromRecord(generationIds, params.full_orchestrator_payload);
98	  addGenerationIdsFromRecord(generationIds, params.individual_segment_params);
99	
100	  if (generationIds.size === 0) {
101	    return EMPTY_STRING_ARRAY;
102	  }
103	
104	  return Object.freeze(Array.from(generationIds));
105	}

107	function areStringArraysEqual(left: readonly string[], right: readonly string[]): boolean {
108	  if (left === right) {
109	    return true;
110	  }
111	
112	  if (left.length !== right.length) {
113	    return false;
114	  }
115	
116	  for (let index = 0; index < left.length; index += 1) {
117	    if (left[index] !== right[index]) {
118	      return false;
119	    }
120	  }
121	
122	  return true;
123	}

125	function buildPendingTaskSummaries(
126	  taskIds: readonly string[],
127	  tasks: Record<string, Task>,
128	  previous: readonly PendingGenerationTaskSnapshot[],
129	): readonly PendingGenerationTaskSnapshot[] {
130	  if (taskIds.length === 0) {
131	    return EMPTY_PENDING_TASK_ARRAY;
132	  }
133	
134	  const previousById = new Map(previous.map((task) => [task.id, task]));
135	  const next = taskIds.flatMap((taskId) => {
136	    const task = tasks[taskId];
137	    if (!task || !isPendingTaskStatus(task.status)) {
138	      return [];
139	    }
140	
141	    const existing = previousById.get(task.id);
142	    if (existing && existing.status === task.status && existing.task_type === task.taskType) {
143	      return [existing];
144	    }
145	
146	    return [{
147	      id: task.id,
148	      status: task.status,
149	      task_type: task.taskType,
150	    }];
151	  });
152	
153	  if (next.length === 0) {
154	    return EMPTY_PENDING_TASK_ARRAY;
155	  }
156	
157	  if (next.length === previous.length && next.every((task, index) => task === previous[index])) {
158	    return previous;
159	  }
160	
161	  return Object.freeze(next);
162	}
```
