166	}
167	
168	function resolveTaskScopeKey(task: Task, projectId?: string | null): string | null {
169	  return resolveTaskProjectScope(projectId ?? task.projectId);
170	}
171	
172	function getScopeState(
173	  scopes: Record<string, RealtimeTaskScopeState>,
174	  scopeKey: string,
175	): RealtimeTaskScopeState {
176	  return scopes[scopeKey] ?? createEmptyScopeState();
177	}
178	
179	function buildUpdatedScopeState(scopeState: RealtimeTaskScopeState, task: Task): RealtimeTaskScopeState {
180	  const previousTask = scopeState.tasks[task.id];
181	  const nextTask = previousTask && deepEqual(previousTask, task) ? previousTask : task;
182	  const previousGenerationIds = scopeState.taskGenerationIds[task.id] ?? EMPTY_STRING_ARRAY;
183	  const nextGenerationIds = collectTaskGenerationIds(nextTask);
184	  const taskChanged = previousTask !== nextTask;
185	  const generationIdsChanged = !areStringArraysEqual(previousGenerationIds, nextGenerationIds);
186	
187	  if (!taskChanged && !generationIdsChanged) {
188	    return scopeState;
189	  }
190	
191	  const nextTasks = taskChanged
192	    ? { ...scopeState.tasks, [task.id]: nextTask }
193	    : scopeState.tasks;
194	
195	  const nextTaskGenerationIds = { ...scopeState.taskGenerationIds };
196	  if (nextGenerationIds.length > 0) {
197	    nextTaskGenerationIds[task.id] = nextGenerationIds;
198	  } else {
199	    delete nextTaskGenerationIds[task.id];
200	  }
201	
202	  const nextPendingTaskIdsByGeneration = { ...scopeState.pendingTaskIdsByGeneration };
203	  const nextPendingTasksByGeneration = { ...scopeState.pendingTasksByGeneration };
204	
205	  const affectedGenerationIds = new Set<string>([
206	    ...previousGenerationIds,
207	    ...nextGenerationIds,
208	  ]);
209	
210	  affectedGenerationIds.forEach((generationId) => {
211	    const previousTaskIds = scopeState.pendingTaskIdsByGeneration[generationId] ?? EMPTY_STRING_ARRAY;
212	    const shouldInclude = nextGenerationIds.includes(generationId);
213	    const alreadyIncluded = previousTaskIds.includes(task.id);
214	
215	    let nextTaskIds = previousTaskIds;
216	    if (shouldInclude && !alreadyIncluded) {
217	      nextTaskIds = Object.freeze([...previousTaskIds, task.id]);
218	    } else if (!shouldInclude && alreadyIncluded) {
219	      nextTaskIds = Object.freeze(previousTaskIds.filter((pendingTaskId) => pendingTaskId !== task.id));
220	    }
221	
222	    if (nextTaskIds.length === 0) {
223	      delete nextPendingTaskIdsByGeneration[generationId];
224	    } else {
225	      nextPendingTaskIdsByGeneration[generationId] = nextTaskIds;
226	    }
227	
228	    const previousPendingTasks = scopeState.pendingTasksByGeneration[generationId] ?? EMPTY_PENDING_TASK_ARRAY;
229	    const nextPendingTasks = buildPendingTaskSummaries(nextTaskIds, nextTasks, previousPendingTasks);
230	
231	    if (nextPendingTasks.length === 0) {
232	      delete nextPendingTasksByGeneration[generationId];
233	    } else {
234	      nextPendingTasksByGeneration[generationId] = nextPendingTasks;
235	    }
236	  });
237	
238	  return {
239	    tasks: nextTasks,
240	    taskGenerationIds: nextTaskGenerationIds,
241	    pendingTaskIdsByGeneration: nextPendingTaskIdsByGeneration,
242	    pendingTasksByGeneration: nextPendingTasksByGeneration,
243	  };
244	}
245	
246	function upsertTaskIntoScopes(
247	  scopes: Record<string, RealtimeTaskScopeState>,
248	  task: Task,
249	  projectId?: string | null,
250	): Record<string, RealtimeTaskScopeState> {
251	  const [REDACTED](task, projectId);
252	  if (!scopeKey) {
253	    return scopes;
254	  }
255	
256	  const scopeState = getScopeState(scopes, scopeKey);
257	  const nextScopeState = buildUpdatedScopeState(scopeState, task);
258	  if (nextScopeState === scopeState) {
259	    return scopes;
260	  }
261	
262	  return {
263	    ...scopes,
264	    [scopeKey]: nextScopeState,
265	  };
266	}
267	
268	const realtimeTaskStore = createStore<RealtimeStoreState>((set, get) => ({
269	  scopes: {},
270	  upsertTask: (task, projectId) => {
271	    const [REDACTED](task, projectId);
272	    if (!scopeKey) {
273	      return null;
274	    }
275	
276	    set((state) => ({
277	      scopes: upsertTaskIntoScopes(state.scopes, task, projectId),
278	    }));
279	
280	    return get().scopes[scopeKey]?.tasks[task.id] ?? null;
281	  },
282	  upsertTasks: (tasks, projectId) => {
283	    if (tasks.length === 0) {
284	      return [];
285	    }
286	
287	    set((state) => {
288	      let nextScopes = state.scopes;
289	      tasks.forEach((task) => {
290	        nextScopes = upsertTaskIntoScopes(nextScopes, task, projectId);
291	      });
292	
293	      return nextScopes === state.scopes
294	        ? state
295	        : { scopes: nextScopes };
296	    });
297	
298	    return tasks.flatMap((task) => {
299	      const [REDACTED](task, projectId);
300	      if (!scopeKey) {
301	        return [];
302	      }
303	
304	      const seededTask = get().scopes[scopeKey]?.tasks[task.id];
305	      return seededTask ? [seededTask] : [];
306	    });
307	  },
308	  resetScope: (projectId) => {
309	    const scopeKey = resolveScopeKey(projectId);
310	    if (!scopeKey) {
311	      return;
312	    }
313	
314	    set((state) => {
315	      if (!(scopeKey in state.scopes)) {
316	        return state;
317	      }
318	
319	      const nextScopes = { ...state.scopes };
320	      delete nextScopes[scopeKey];
321	      return { scopes: nextScopes };
322	    });
323	  },
324	  resetAll: () => {
325	    set({ scopes: {} });
326	  },
327	}));
328	
329	function useRealtimeTaskStore<T>(
330	  selector: (state: RealtimeStoreState) => T,
331	  equalityFn?: (left: T, right: T) => boolean,
332	): T {
333	  return useStoreWithEqualityFn(realtimeTaskStore, selector, equalityFn);
334	}
335	
336	export function useRealtimeTask(taskId: string, projectId?: string | null): Task | null {
337	  return useRealtimeTaskStore((state) => {
338	    const scopeKey = resolveScopeKey(projectId);
339	    if (!scopeKey || !taskId) {
340	      return null;
341	    }
342	
343	    return state.scopes[scopeKey]?.tasks[taskId] ?? null;
344	  });
345	}
346	
347	export function getRealtimeTaskSnapshot(taskId: string, projectId?: string | null): Task | null {
348	  const scopeKey = resolveScopeKey(projectId);
349	  if (!scopeKey || !taskId) {
350	    return null;
351	  }
352	
353	  return realtimeTaskStore.getState().scopes[scopeKey]?.tasks[taskId] ?? null;
354	}
355	
356	export function useRealtimePendingGenerationTasks(
357	  generationId: string | null | undefined,
358	  projectId?: string | null,
359	): { pendingCount: number; pendingTasks: readonly PendingGenerationTaskSnapshot[] } {
360	  return useRealtimeTaskStore((state) => {
361	    const scopeKey = resolveScopeKey(projectId);
362	    if (!scopeKey || !generationId) {
363	      return {
364	        pendingCount: 0,
365	        pendingTasks: EMPTY_PENDING_TASK_ARRAY,
366	      };
367	    }
368	
369	    const pendingTasks = state.scopes[scopeKey]?.pendingTasksByGeneration[generationId] ?? EMPTY_PENDING_TASK_ARRAY;
370	    return {
371	      pendingCount: pendingTasks.length,
372	      pendingTasks,
373	    };
374	  }, shallow);
375	}
376	
377	export function upsertRealtimeTaskSnapshot(task: Task, projectId?: string | null): Task | null {
378	  return realtimeTaskStore.getState().upsertTask(task, projectId);
379	}
380	
381	export function upsertRealtimeTaskSnapshots(tasks: readonly Task[], projectId?: string | null): Task[] {
382	  return realtimeTaskStore.getState().upsertTasks(tasks, projectId);
383	}
384	
385	export function seedRealtimeTaskFromRow(row: unknown, projectId?: string | null): Task | null {
386	  if (!isTaskDbRow(row)) {
387	    return null;
388	  }
389	
390	  return upsertRealtimeTaskSnapshot(mapTaskDbRowToTask(row), projectId);
391	}
392	
393	export function seedRealtimeTasksFromRows(rows: readonly unknown[], projectId?: string | null): Task[] {
394	  const tasks = rows.flatMap((row) => (isTaskDbRow(row) ? [mapTaskDbRowToTask(row)] : []));
395	  return upsertRealtimeTaskSnapshots(tasks, projectId);
396	}
397	
398	export function resetRealtimeTaskScope(projectId?: string | null): void {
399	  realtimeTaskStore.getState().resetScope(projectId);
400	}
401	
402	export function __resetRealtimeTaskStoreForTests(): void {
403	  realtimeTaskStore.getState().resetAll();
404	}
405

99	function getEffectivePaneHeights(params: {

377	  setActiveTaskId: (taskId) => {
378	    set((state) => (
379	      state.activeTaskId === taskId
380	        ? state
381	        : { activeTaskId: taskId }
382	    ));
383	  },
384	  setIsTasksPaneOpen: (isOpen) => {
385	    activeBootstrapRuntime?.setIsTasksPaneOpen(isOpen);
386	  },
387	  resetAllPaneLocks: () => {
388	    activeBootstrapRuntime?.resetAllPaneLocks();
389	  },
390	  bootstrap: (input) => {
391	    if (areBootstrapInputsEqual(activeBootstrapSnapshot, input)) {
392	      console.log('[PanesBoot] bootstrap short-circuit (inputs equal)', { owner: input.owner.toString() });
393	      return;
394	    }
395	
396	    console.log('[PanesBoot] bootstrap APPLY set()', {
397	      owner: input.owner.toString(),
398	      prevOwner: activeBootstrapOwner?.toString() ?? null,
399	      locks: input.locks,
400	      isGenerationsPaneOpen: input.isGenerationsPaneOpen,
401	      isEditorPaneOpen: input.isEditorPaneOpen,
402	      isTasksPaneOpen: input.isTasksPaneOpen,
403	    });
404	    activeBootstrapOwner = input.owner;
405	    activeBootstrapSnapshot = input;
406	    updateBootstrapRuntime(input);
407	
408	    set((state) => buildBootstrappedState(state, input));
409	  },
410	  clearBootstrap: (owner) => {
411	    if (owner && activeBootstrapOwner && owner !== activeBootstrapOwner) {
412	      return;
413	    }
414	
415	    activeBootstrapOwner = null;
416	    activeBootstrapSnapshot = null;
417	    updateBootstrapRuntime(null);
418	    set(() => createInitialSnapshot());
419	  },
420	  resetStore: () => {
421	    activeBootstrapOwner = null;
422	    activeBootstrapSnapshot = null;
423	    updateBootstrapRuntime(null);
424	    set(() => createInitialSnapshot());
425	  },
426	}));
427	
428	export function usePanesStoreApi() {
429	  return panesStore;
430	}
431	
432	export function usePanesStore<T>(
433	  selector: (state: PanesStoreState) => T,
434	  equalityFn?: (left: T, right: T) => boolean,
435	): T {
436	  return useStoreWithEqualityFn(panesStore, selector, equalityFn);
437	}
438	
439	export function usePanesStoreAvailability(): PanesStoreAvailability {
440	  return usePanesStore((state) => state.availability, shallow);
441	}
442	
443	export function bootstrapPanesStore(input: PanesStoreBootstrapInput): void {
444	  panesStore.getState().bootstrap(input);
445	}
446	
447	export function clearPanesStoreBootstrap(owner?: symbol): void {
448	  panesStore.getState().clearBootstrap(owner);
449	}
450	
451	export function resetPanesStore(): void {
452	  panesStore.getState().resetStore();
453	}
454	
455	/**
456	 * Singleton panes lifecycle contract:
457	 * - exactly one mounted bootstrap owner should drive runtime-backed pane state
458	 * - repeated bootstraps from that owner are idempotent
459	 * - reads outside bootstrap intentionally return the unlocked default snapshot
460	 */
461	export function useBootstrapPanesStore(): void {
462	  const ownerRef = useRef<symbol | null>(null);
463	  if (ownerRef.current === null) {
464	    ownerRef.current = Symbol('panes-store-bootstrap-owner');
465	  }
466	
467	  const {
468	    locks,
469	    isGenerationsPaneOpenState,
470	    isEditorPaneOpenState,
471	    isTasksPaneOpenState,
472	    setIsGenerationsPaneLocked,
473	    setIsEditorPaneLocked,
474	    setIsShotsPaneLocked,
475	    setIsTasksPaneLocked,
476	    setIsGenerationsPaneOpen,
477	    setIsEditorPaneOpen,
478	    setIsTasksPaneOpen,
479	    resetAllPaneLocks,
480	  } = usePaneLockPolicyState();
481	
482	  const bootstrapInput = useMemo<PanesStoreBootstrapInput>(() => ({
483	    owner: ownerRef.current as symbol,
484	    viewportHeight: getViewportHeight(),
485	    locks,
486	    isGenerationsPaneOpen: isGenerationsPaneOpenState,
487	    isEditorPaneOpen: isEditorPaneOpenState,
488	    isTasksPaneOpen: isTasksPaneOpenState,
489	    setIsGenerationsPaneLocked,
490	    setIsEditorPaneLocked,
491	    setIsShotsPaneLocked,
492	    setIsTasksPaneLocked,
493	    setIsGenerationsPaneOpen,
494	    setIsEditorPaneOpen,
495	    setIsTasksPaneOpen,
496	    resetAllPaneLocks,
497	  }), [
498	    locks,
499	    isGenerationsPaneOpenState,
500	    isEditorPaneOpenState,
501	    isTasksPaneOpenState,
502	    setIsGenerationsPaneLocked,
503	    setIsEditorPaneLocked,
504	    setIsShotsPaneLocked,
505	    setIsTasksPaneLocked,
506	    setIsGenerationsPaneOpen,
507	    setIsEditorPaneOpen,
508	    setIsTasksPaneOpen,
509	    resetAllPaneLocks,
510	  ]);
511	
512	  console.log('[PanesBoot] useBootstrapPanesStore render', {
513	    owner: ownerRef.current?.toString(),
514	    inputId: bootstrapInput && (bootstrapInput as unknown as { __id?: number }).__id,
515	  });
516	
517	  useEffect(() => {
518	    console.log('[PanesBoot] effect fires (bootstrapInput changed identity)');
519	    bootstrapPanesStore(bootstrapInput);
520	  }, [bootstrapInput]);
521	
522	  useEffect(() => {
523	    return () => {
524	      console.log('[PanesBoot] CLEANUP unmount', { owner: ownerRef.current?.toString() });
525	      clearPanesStoreBootstrap(ownerRef.current ?? undefined);
526	    };
527	  }, []);
528	}
529	
530	export function PanesStoreBootstrapBoundary({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
531	  console.log('[PanesBoot] PanesStoreBootstrapBoundary render');
532	  useBootstrapPanesStore();
533	  return children;
534	}
535	
536	export function __resetPanesStoreForTests(): void {
537	  resetPanesStore();
538	}
539

54	type OverlayStackSnapshot = Pick<OverlayStackState, 'overlays'>;
55	
56	function isHTMLElement(value: unknown): value is HTMLElement {
57	  return value instanceof HTMLElement;
58	}
59	
60	function normalizeElements(
61	  elements?: Iterable<Element | null | undefined>,
62	): readonly HTMLElement[] {
63	  if (!elements) {
64	    return [];
65	  }
66	
67	  const nextElements: HTMLElement[] = [];
68	  for (const element of elements) {
69	    if (!isHTMLElement(element) || nextElements.includes(element)) {
70	      continue;
71	    }
72	    nextElements.push(element);
73	  }
74	
75	  return nextElements;
76	}
77	
78	function getTopOverlay(overlays: readonly OverlayStackEntry[]): OverlayStackEntry | null {
79	  return overlays.at(-1) ?? null;
80	}
81	
82	function isOverlayActuallyOpen(overlay: OverlayStackEntry): boolean {
83	  if (overlay.elements.length === 0) {
84	    return false;
85	  }
86	  return overlay.elements.some((element) => {
87	    const state = element.getAttribute('data-state');
88	    if (state === 'closed') return false;
89	    if (element.hasAttribute('data-open')) return true;
90	    if (state === 'open') return true;
91	    // Fallback for overlays without base-ui open/close state markers:
92	    // treat as open only when rendered with non-zero box.
93	    return element.offsetParent !== null || element.getClientRects().length > 0;
94	  });
95	}
96	
97	function getTopModalOverlay(overlays: readonly OverlayStackEntry[]): OverlayStackEntry | null {
98	  for (let index = overlays.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
99	    const overlay = overlays[index];
100	    if (overlay.modal && isOverlayActuallyOpen(overlay)) {
101	      return overlay;
102	    }
103	  }
104	
105	  return null;
106	}
107	
108	function canRestoreFocusTo(element: HTMLElement | null): element is HTMLElement {
109	  return isHTMLElement(element) && element.isConnected && typeof element.focus === 'function';
110	}
111	
112	function restoreFocus(element: HTMLElement | null): void {
113	  if (!canRestoreFocusTo(element)) {
114	    return;
115	  }
116	
117	  queueMicrotask(() => {
118	    if (!canRestoreFocusTo(element)) {
119	      return;
120	    }
121	
122	    try {
123	      element.focus({ preventScroll: true });
124	    } catch {
125	      element.focus();
126	    }
127	  });
128	}
129	
130	function createOverlayEntry(input: OverlayStackOpenInput): OverlayStackEntry {
131	  return {
132	    id: input.id,
133	    type: input.type,
134	    modal: input.modal ?? false,
135	    restoreFocusTo: input.restoreFocusTo ?? null,
136	    elements: normalizeElements(input.elements),
137	  };
138	}
139	
140	function containsElement(
141	  overlay: OverlayStackEntry | undefined,
142	  element: Element | null,
143	): boolean {
144	  if (!overlay || !element) {
145	    return false;
146	  }
147	
148	  return overlay.elements.some((candidate) => candidate === element || candidate.contains(element));
149	}
150	
151	const overlayStackStore = createStore<OverlayStackState>((set, get) => ({
152	  overlays: [],
153	
154	  pushOverlay: (input) => {
155	    const nextEntry = createOverlayEntry(input);
156	    set((state) => {
157	      const nextOverlays = [
158	        ...state.overlays.filter((overlay) => overlay.id !== input.id),
159	        nextEntry,
160	      ];
161	      return { overlays: nextOverlays };
162	    });
163	  },
164	
165	  updateOverlay: (id, patch) => {
166	    set((state) => {
167	      const index = state.overlays.findIndex((overlay) => overlay.id === id);
168	      if (index === -1) {
169	        return state;
170	      }
171	
172	      const current = state.overlays[index];
173	      const nextOverlay: OverlayStackEntry = {
174	        ...current,
175	        ...(patch.type === undefined ? null : { type: patch.type }),
176	        ...(patch.modal === undefined ? null : { modal: patch.modal }),
177	        ...(patch.restoreFocusTo === undefined
178	          ? null
179	          : { restoreFocusTo: patch.restoreFocusTo }),
180	        ...(patch.elements === undefined
181	          ? null
182	          : { elements: normalizeElements(patch.elements) }),
183	      };
184	      const nextOverlays = [...state.overlays];
185	      nextOverlays[index] = nextOverlay;
186	      return { overlays: nextOverlays };
187	    });
188	  },
189	
190	  popOverlay: (id) => {
191	    const previousOverlays = get().overlays;
192	    const removedOverlay = previousOverlays.find((overlay) => overlay.id === id);
193	    if (!removedOverlay) {
194	      return;
195	    }
196	
197	    const previousTopModal = getTopModalOverlay(previousOverlays);
198	    const nextOverlays = previousOverlays.filter((overlay) => overlay.id !== id);
199	
200	    set({ overlays: nextOverlays });
201	
202	    if (previousTopModal?.id === id && removedOverlay.modal) {
203	      restoreFocus(removedOverlay.restoreFocusTo);
204	    }
205	  },
206	
207	  getTopOverlay: () => getTopOverlay(get().overlays),
208	
209	  getTopModalOverlay: () => getTopModalOverlay(get().overlays),
210	
211	  isOverlayTopmost: (id) => getTopOverlay(get().overlays)?.id === id,
212	
213	  isTopmostModalOverlay: (id) => getTopModalOverlay(get().overlays)?.id === id,
214	
215	  getOverlayLayer: (id) => {
216	    const index = get().overlays.findIndex((overlay) => overlay.id === id);
217	    return index === -1 ? null : index + 1;
218	  },
219	
220	  overlayContainsElement: (id, element) => {
221	    return containsElement(
222	      get().overlays.find((overlay) => overlay.id === id),
223	      element,
224	    );
225	  },
226	
227	  isElementWithinAnyOverlay: (element) => {
228	    return get().overlays.some((overlay) => containsElement(overlay, element));
229	  },
230	
231	  getTopmostOverlayContainingElement: (element) => {
232	    const { overlays } = get();
233	    for (let index = overlays.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
234	      const overlay = overlays[index];
235	      if (containsElement(overlay, element)) {
236	        return overlay;
237	      }
238	    }
239	
240	    return null;
241	  },
242	}));

244	/** Returns the vanilla store API for imperative overlay registration and queries. */
245	export function useOverlayStackApi() {
246	  return overlayStackStore;
247	}
248	
249	function useOverlayStack<T>(
250	  selector: (state: OverlayStackState) => T,
251	  equalityFn?: (left: T, right: T) => boolean,
252	): T {
253	  return useStoreWithEqualityFn(overlayStackStore, selector, equalityFn);
254	}
255	
256	/** Subscribes to the current ordered overlay snapshot. */
257	export function useOverlayStackState(): OverlayStackSnapshot {

259	}

264	}

269	}

274	}

279	}
280

284	}

171	    return existing?.url === item.url

173	      && existing.generationId === item.generationId

214	  const selectedGalleryIds = new Set(selectionMap.keys());
215	  const selectedGalleryClips: SelectedMediaClip[] = Array.from(selectionMap.entries()).map(([id, item]) => ({
216	    clipId: id,
217	    [REDACTED],
218	    url: item.url,
219	    mediaType: item.mediaType,
220	    isTimelineBacked: false,
221	    generationId: item.generationId,
222	    variantId: item.variantId,
223	  }));
224	
225	  return {
226	    gallerySelectionMap: selectionMap,
227	    selectedGalleryIds,
228	    selectedGalleryClips,
229	    gallerySummary: buildSummary(selectedGalleryClips),
230	  };
231	}
232	
233	function buildTimelineState(
234	  selectedClipIds: ReadonlySet<string>,
235	  preferredPrimaryClipId: string | null,
236	  selectedTrackId: string | null,
237	  additiveSelection: boolean,
238	): TimelineSliceState {
239	  const primaryClipId = getPrimaryClipId(selectedClipIds, preferredPrimaryClipId);
240	  return {
241	    selectedClipId: primaryClipId,
242	    selectedTrackId,
243	    selectedClipIds,
244	    primaryClipId,
245	    additiveSelection: additiveSelection && selectedClipIds.size > 1,
246	  };
247	}
248	
249	function clearTimelineClipSelection(
250	  timeline: TimelineSliceState,
251	): TimelineSliceState {
252	  if (
253	    timeline.selectedClipIds.size === 0
254	    && timeline.selectedClipId === null
255	    && timeline.primaryClipId === null
256	    && !timeline.additiveSelection
257	  ) {
258	    return timeline;
259	  }
260	
261	  return {
262	    ...timeline,
263	    selectedClipId: null,
264	    selectedClipIds: new Set(),
265	    primaryClipId: null,
266	    additiveSelection: false,
267	  };
268	}

270	const selectionStore = createStore<SelectionStoreState>((set, get) => ({
271	  gallery: initialGalleryState(),

275	  clearGallerySelection: () => {
276	    set((state) => (
277	      state.gallery.gallerySelectionMap.size === 0
278	        ? state

280	    ));
281	  },
282	
283	  selectGalleryItem: (id, meta, options) => {
284	    const normalized = normalizeSelectionItem({ id, ...meta });
285	    if (!normalized) {
286	      return;
287	    }
288	
289	    set((state) => {
290	      const previous = state.gallery.gallerySelectionMap;
291	      if (!options?.toggle) {
292	        if (hasSameSelection(previous, [normalized])) {
293	          return state;
294	        }
295	
296	        return {
297	          gallery: buildGalleryState(new Map([
298	            [
299	              normalized.id,
300	              {
301	                url: normalized.url,
302	                mediaType: normalized.mediaType,
303	                generationId: normalized.generationId,
304	                variantId: normalized.variantId,
305	              },
306	            ],
307	          ])),
308	          timeline: clearTimelineClipSelection(state.timeline),
309	        };
310	      }
311	
312	      const next = new Map(previous);
313	      if (next.has(normalized.id)) {
314	        next.delete(normalized.id);
315	      } else {
316	        next.set(normalized.id, {
317	          url: normalized.url,
318	          mediaType: normalized.mediaType,
319	          generationId: normalized.generationId,
320	          variantId: normalized.variantId,
321	        });
322	      }
323	
324	      return { gallery: buildGalleryState(next) };
325	    });
326	  },
327	
328	  selectGalleryItems: (items, options) => {
329	    const normalizedItems = items
330	      .map(normalizeSelectionItem)
331	      .filter((item): item is GallerySelectionEntry & { id: string } => item !== null);
332	
333	    set((state) => {
334	      const previous = state.gallery.gallerySelectionMap;
335	      if (normalizedItems.length === 0) {
336	        if (options?.append || previous.size === 0) {
337	          return state;
338	        }
339	
340	        return {
341	          gallery: initialGalleryState(),
342	          timeline: clearTimelineClipSelection(state.timeline),
343	        };
344	      }
345	
346	      const next = options?.append ? new Map(previous) : new Map<string, GallerySelectionEntry>();
347	      let changed = !options?.append && !hasSameSelection(previous, normalizedItems);
348	
349	      for (const item of normalizedItems) {
350	        const nextEntry = {
351	          url: item.url,
352	          mediaType: item.mediaType,
353	          generationId: item.generationId,
354	          variantId: item.variantId,
355	        };
356	        const previousEntry = previous.get(item.id);
357	        if (
358	          !previousEntry
359	          || previousEntry.url !== nextEntry.url
360	          || previousEntry.mediaType !== nextEntry.mediaType
361	          || previousEntry.generationId !== nextEntry.generationId
362	          || previousEntry.variantId !== nextEntry.variantId
363	        ) {
364	          changed = true;
365	        }
366	        next.set(item.id, nextEntry);
367	      }
368	
369	      if (!changed && next.size === previous.size) {
370	        return state;
371	      }
372	
373	      return options?.append

376	            gallery: buildGalleryState(next),

379	    });
380	  },
381

387	
388	    set((state) => {
389	      let changed = false;
390	      const next = new Map(state.gallery.gallerySelectionMap);
391	
392	      idsToRemove.forEach((id) => {

394	          changed = true;

396	      });

398	      return changed ? { gallery: buildGalleryState(next) } : state;
399	    });

751	export function useTimelineMultiSelect(): UseTimelineMultiSelectResult {
752	  const {
753	    selectedClipIds,
754	    primaryClipId,
755	    additiveSelection,
756	    clearSelection,
757	    selectClip,
758	    selectClips,
759	    addToSelection,
760	    pruneSelection,
761	  } = useTimelineSelectionStore();
762	
763	  const selectedClipIdsRef = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set(selectedClipIds));
764	  const additiveSelectionRef = useRef(additiveSelection);
765	
766	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
767	    selectedClipIdsRef.current = new Set(selectedClipIds);
768	    additiveSelectionRef.current = additiveSelection;
769	  }, [additiveSelection, selectedClipIds]);
770	
771	  const isClipSelected = useCallback((clipId: string) => {
772	    return selectedClipIdsRef.current.has(clipId);
773	  }, []);
774	
775	  return useMemo(() => ({
776	    selectedClipIds,
777	    selectedClipIdsRef,
778	    additiveSelectionRef,
779	    primaryClipId,
780	    selectClip: (clipId: string, opts?: SelectClipOptions) => {
781	      selectionStore.getState().selectTimelineClip(clipId, opts, { clearGallery: false });
782	    },
783	    selectClips: (clipIds: Iterable<string>) => {
784	      selectionStore.getState().selectTimelineClips(clipIds, { clearGallery: false });
785	    },
786	    addToSelection,
787	    clearSelection: () => {
788	      selectionStore.getState().clearTimelineSelection({ clearGallery: false });
789	    },
790	    isClipSelected,
791	    pruneSelection,
792	  }), [
793	    addToSelection,
794	    additiveSelection,
795	    clearSelection,
796	    isClipSelected,
797	    primaryClipId,
798	    pruneSelection,
799	    selectClip,
800	    selectClips,
801	    selectedClipIds,
802	  ]);
803	}

## Zustand Usage Audit: Reigh App

I found **6 Zustand stores** in active use. Here's the detailed analysis:

### 1. **panesStore**
- **File**: `/shared/state/panesStore.ts`
- **Store name**: `panesStore` (exported via `usePanesStore()` hook)
- **State held**:
  - Pane visibility (gens, editor, shots, tasks) + locked/open toggles
  - Pane dimensions (height/width, effective heights)
  - Active task ID
  - Bootstrap/availability tracking

- **How consumed**: Custom hook `usePanesStore(selector, equalityFn)` with `shallow` equality; vanilla API via `bootstrapPanesStore()` + `usePanesStoreApi()` for imperative lifecycle control
- **Scope**: `global-ui` (layout state across app)
- **Verdict**: **appropriate** — this is exactly Zustand's sweet spot: client-only UI state with no server source-of-truth. The Provider pattern (`PanesStoreBootstrapBoundary`) and symbol-based owner tracking show sophisticated ownership semantics. Uses `useStoreWithEqualityFn` to stabilize selectors.

**Note**: Wrapper component (`PanesStoreBootstrapBoundary`) owns initialization — matches project pattern.

---

### 2. **overlayStack**
- **File**: `/shared/state/overlayStack.ts`
- **Store name**: `overlayStackStore` (via `useOverlayStackApi()`)
- **State held**:
  - Ordered stack of overlay entries (id, type, modal flag, focus restoration, tracked elements)
  - Queries: topmost, topmost modal, layer number, element containment

- **How consumed**: Hooks like `useTopOverlay()`, `useOverlayStackState()` with `shallow` equality; vanilla queries via `useOverlayStackApi().getState()`; imperative stack ops (`pushOverlay`, `popOverlay`) via API
- **Scope**: `global-ui` (dialog/popover lifecycle management)
- **Verdict**: **appropriate** — modal/focus/stacking state is ephemeral and read-heavy. No subscription side effects noted. `useStoreWithEqualityFn` with equality functions shows disciplined selector usage.

---

### 3. **realtimeStore** (realtimeTaskStore)
- **File**: `/shared/state/realtimeStore.ts`
- **Store name**: `realtimeTaskStore`
- **State held**:
  - Task cache per project scope (tasks dict, generation ID mappings)
  - Pending task summaries by generation ID
  - Task status tracking (for UI optimism / realtime sync)

- **How consumed**: Hooks `useRealtimeTask()`, `useRealtimePendingGenerationTasks()` with `shallow` equality; vanilla getters `getRealtimeTaskSnapshot()`; imperative upsert via `upsertRealtimeTaskSnapshot()`
- **Scope**: `global-domain` (task data, scoped by project)
- **Verdict**: **questionable** — this **duplicates server state** (tasks come from Supabase). While it's a performance optimization (avoid re-fetching), it's living alongside React Query data. The cache is seeded from DB rows (`seedRealtimeTaskFromRow`) and upserted imperatively. **Consider**: Is this truly needing Zustand, or should these be React Query mutations + cache invalidations? The "realtime" aspect (generation dependencies) could be a computed selector on top of React Query's task cache.

**Risk**: Source-of-truth split between Supabase (canonical) and Zustand (derived cache). If task sync fails, the store goes stale with no recovery signal.

---

### 4. **selectionStore**
- **File**: `/shared/state/selectionStore.ts`
- **Store name**: `selectionStore`
- **State held**:
  - Gallery selection (map of IDs → metadata, clips, summary)
  - Timeline selection (selected/primary clip IDs, selected track, additive flag)
  - Shot selection (current shot, last affected, shot-addition selection)

- **How consumed**: Specialized hooks (`useGallerySelection()`, `useTimelineSelectionStore()`, `useCurrentShot()`) with `shallow` equality; wrapper `useTimelineMultiSelect()` exposes refs for mutation tracking; vanilla API via `useSelectionStoreApi()`
- **Scope**: `feature-scoped` (video-editor tool)
- **Verdict**: **appropriate** — ephemeral UI selection state (no server source-of-truth). Heavy use of `shallow` equality and ref-based sync for perf. The multi-select hook exposes layout refs and memoization — fine for tool-scoped state. **Concern**: Cross-slice clear-on-select logic (`clearTimelineSelection` clears gallery) suggests tight coupling; monitor for feature creep.

**Good pattern**: Gallery + Timeline slices can clear each other but are logically separated, suggesting the store is nearing complexity ceiling.

---

### 5. **createEntityStore** (factory)
- **File**: `/shared/state/createEntityStore.ts`
- **Store name**: Creates typed entity stores (e.g., color presets, settings)
- **State held**:
  - Per-entity records: settings, status (idle/loading/ready/saving/error), error, persisted flag
  - Dirty state tracking, pending persistence timers, text field deferred keys
  - Clean snapshot + saved settings for revert

- **How consumed**: Factory returns `useEntity(entityId)` hook and `getState()` API; hooks return `EntityView<T>` with actions (updateField, save, revert, reset); `bootstrap` for hydration from DB
- **Scope**: `infrastructure` (generic entity lifecycle — settings, prefs, form state)
- **Verdict**: **appropriate** — this is a well-designed abstraction for client-side entity editing with deferred persistence. Handles dirty state, revert, debounced saves, and text-field buffering. No server duplication — the store is the working copy, DB is source. **Good signs**: explicit `hasPersistedData` flag, `isDirty()`/`hasPendingPersistence()` queries, clear mutation/save semantics. Uses `shallow` in selector.

**Note**: Debounce + persistence scheduling is in-store, not middleware — fine for this level of concern.

---

### 6. **timelineStore**
- **File**: `/tools/video-editor/hooks/timelineStore.ts`
- **Store name**: `timelineStore` (created per-instance via `createTimelineStore()`)
- **State held**:
  - Data slice: resolvedConfig, device class, input modality, interaction policy, selected clips/track, composition size, scale, preferences (refs + state)
  - Ops slice: all timeline operations (select, drag, drop, resize, track management, generation asset registry)
  - Chrome slice: save status, render status, undo/redo, checkpoints
  - Playback slice: current time, preview ref, player container ref

- **How consumed**: Context provider (`TimelineStoreProvider`) wraps tree; hooks `useTimelineStoreApi()` (required), `useTimelineStoreApiSafe()` (optional); slice-specific hooks like `useTimelineDataSlice()`, `useTimelineOpsSlice()`; subscriber hooks with selectors + equality functions; safe variants return null if store not mounted
- **Scope**: `feature-scoped` (video-editor tool) but **cross-cutting within tool** (data, ops, chrome, playback are tightly coupled)
- **Verdict**: **questionable** — this is a **god store**. It holds UI state (interaction, refs), business logic (operations), render state, and playback state all in one tree. The context-provider pattern + sync methods suggest it's trying to bridge React Context and Zustand. 

**Issues**:
  - Ops slice holds 40+ functions (select, drag, delete, split, track ops, asset registry) — suggests it's a command bus, not state
  - Data slice mixes state (selectedClipId) with refs (dataRef, interactionStateRef) — some values should be local React state
  - Safe variants return null on unmount — breaks the required-provider pattern (see CLAUDE.md)
  - Bootstrap is manual (sync* methods called from parent), not automatic

**Consider**: Split into smaller stores (data state, playback, render status) + use React Context for ops dispatch. The current design loads 30+ no-op functions at rest; refactor toward lazy binding.

---

## Summary Table

| Store Name | Scope | Verdict | Key Issue |
|---|---|---|---|
| panesStore | global-ui | appropriate | None — well-designed lifecycle ownership |
| overlayStack | global-ui | appropriate | None — clean query API |
| realtimeStore | global-domain | questionable | Duplicates server state; consider React Query cache |
| selectionStore | feature-scoped | appropriate | Monitor for creep; slices are loosely coupled |
| createEntityStore | infrastructure | appropriate | Generic entity edit lifecycle works well |
| timelineStore | feature-scoped | questionable | God store; ops slice is 40+ functions; safe variants break required-provider pattern |

---

## Cross-Cutting Observations

1. **Equality discipline**: All stores using `shallow` or custom `equalityFn` in selectors — good practice prevents thrashing.
2. **No `subscribe` side effects**: None of the stores couple to imperative lifecycle (e.g., `.subscribe((state) => doSideEffect())`).
3. **No `useStoreWithEqualityFn` wrappers**: Stores expose purpose-built hooks (e.g., `useGallerySelection()`) rather than forcing consumers to write selectors — reduces boilerplate.
4. **Bootstrap pattern**: panesStore and timelineStore use manual bootstrap (via Provider or sync methods); createEntityStore uses explicit `bootstrapEntity()`. This is consistent but not automatic — consider a reusable Provider HOC.
5. **Migration targets (M1a/M1b/M1c)**: realtimeStore and timelineStore are the best candidates for review. realtimeStore should either commit to being a React Query cache layer or move fully to Query. timelineStore needs decomposition.

---

## Recommendations

- **realtimeStore**: If the "realtime" aspect is truly generation→task dependency tracking, that's valid. If it's just task caching, merge with React Query's `tasks` query.
- **timelineStore**: Extract Ops as a separate dispatch context. Move Refs to local state where possible. Break Data/Playback/Chrome into smaller stores if they have independent lifecycles.
- **selectionStore**: Watch slice coupling; if gallery ↔ timeline clears grow, consider event-driven reset or a unified "workspace clear" action.
- **panesStore + overlayStack**: Keep as-is; both are exemplary.

3	 *
4	 * This provider owns all shot-specific settings state, making it accessible to
5	 * any child component without prop drilling. Settings are persisted via useShotSettings.
6	 *
7	 * Architecture:
8	 * - Wraps useShotSettings (state + persistence)
9	 * - Wraps useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers (all update handlers)
10	 * - Exposes focused hooks for each settings domain
11	 *
12	 * Usage:

19	 * // In any child component

21	 * const { motionMode, setMotionMode } = useMotionSettings();
22	 * ```
23	 */

30	}

33	  canRenderMediaOnWeb: (options: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<{
34	    canRender?: boolean;
35	    issues?: CanRenderIssue[];
36	    resolvedVideoCodec?: string;
37	    resolvedAudioCodec?: string | null;

42	const FREE_LICENSE_KEY = 'free-license';

44	let webRendererCache: WebRendererModule | null = null;
45	
46	const getWebRendererModule = async (): Promise<WebRendererModule> => {
47	  if (webRendererCache) return webRendererCache;
48	
49	  try {
50	    const mod = await import('@remotion/web-renderer') as unknown as WebRendererModule;
51	    webRendererCache = mod;
52	    return mod;
53	  } catch (err) {
54	    console.error('[useClientRender] Failed to import @remotion/web-renderer:', err);
55	    throw new Error(
56	      'Could not load @remotion/web-renderer. Make sure the package is installed: npm install @remotion/web-renderer',
57	    );
58	  }
59	};
60	
61	const appendLogLine = (setRenderLog: UseClientRenderOptions['setRenderLog'], message: string) => {
62	  setRenderLog((current) => current ? `${current}\n${message}` : message);
63	};
64	
65	const getFileExtension = (videoCodec: string | undefined) => {
66	  if (!videoCodec) {
67	    return 'mp4';
68	  }
69	
70	  if (videoCodec.startsWith('vp') || videoCodec.includes('av1')) {
71	    return 'webm';
72	  }
73	
74	  return 'mp4';
75	};
76	
77	const getMimeType = (extension: string) => extension === 'webm' ? 'video/webm' : 'video/mp4';
78	
79	const getBlobFromResult = async (
80	  result: unknown,
81	  extension: string,
82	): Promise<Blob | null> => {
83	  if (result instanceof Blob) {
84	    return result;
85	  }
86	
87	  if (result instanceof ArrayBuffer) {
88	    return new Blob([result], { type: getMimeType(extension) });
89	  }
90	
91	  if (ArrayBuffer.isView(result)) {
92	    return new Blob([result.buffer.slice(result.byteOffset, result.byteOffset + result.byteLength)], {
93	      type: getMimeType(extension),
94	    });
95	  }
96	
97	  if (!result || typeof result !== 'object') {
98	    return null;
99	  }
100	
101	  const record = result as Record<string, unknown>;
102	
103	  // @remotion/web-renderer returns { getBlob: () => Promise<Blob> }
104	  if (typeof record.getBlob === 'function') {
105	    return await (record.getBlob as () => Promise<Blob>)();
106	  }
107	
108	  const blobCandidate = record.blob ?? record.result ?? record.data;
109	  if (blobCandidate instanceof Blob) {
110	    return blobCandidate;
111	  }
112	
113	  const bufferCandidate = record.arrayBuffer ?? record.buffer;
114	  if (bufferCandidate instanceof ArrayBuffer) {
115	    return new Blob([bufferCandidate], { type: getMimeType(extension) });
116	  }
117	
118	  if (typeof record.save === 'function') {
119	    const saveResult = await (record.save as () => Promise<unknown>)();
120	    return getBlobFromResult(saveResult, extension);
121	  }
122	
123	  return null;
124	};
125	
126	const getProgressUpdate = (
127	  value: unknown,
128	  fallbackTotal: number,
129	): NonNullable<RenderProgress> => {
130	  if (typeof value === 'number') {
131	    const current = Math.min(fallbackTotal, Math.max(1, Math.floor(value) + 1));
132	    return {
133	      current,
134	      total: fallbackTotal,
135	      percent: Math.round((current / Math.max(1, fallbackTotal)) * 100),
136	      phase: 'rendering',
137	    };
138	  }
139	
140	  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object') {
141	    return {
142	      current: 0,
143	      total: fallbackTotal,
144	      percent: 0,
145	      phase: 'rendering',
146	    };
147	  }
148	
149	  const record = value as Record<string, unknown>;
150	
151	  // @remotion/web-renderer passes { progress: 0..1, renderedFrames, encodedFrames, ... }
152	  if (typeof record.progress === 'number' && record.progress >= 0 && record.progress <= 1) {
153	    const percent = Math.round(record.progress * 100);
154	    const current = typeof record.renderedFrames === 'number' ? record.renderedFrames : Math.round(record.progress * fallbackTotal);
155	    return {
156	      current,
157	      total: fallbackTotal,
158	      percent,
159	      phase: 'rendering',
160	    };
161	  }
162	
163	  const total = typeof record.total === 'number'
164	    ? record.total
165	    : typeof record.totalFrames === 'number'
166	      ? record.totalFrames
167	      : fallbackTotal;
168	  const current = typeof record.current === 'number'
169	    ? record.current
170	    : typeof record.frame === 'number'
171	      ? record.frame + 1
172	      : 0;
173	  const percent = typeof record.percent === 'number'
174	    ? record.percent
175	    : Math.round((current / Math.max(1, total)) * 100);
176	  const phase = typeof record.phase === 'string' ? record.phase : 'rendering';
177	
178	  return {
179	    current: Math.min(total, Math.max(0, current)),
180	    total,
181	    percent: Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(percent))),
182	    phase,
183	  };
184	};
185	
186	export function useClientRender({
187	  resolvedConfig,
188	  metadata,
189	  setRenderStatus,
190	  setRenderProgress,
191	  setRenderLog,
192	  setRenderDirty,
193	  setRenderResult,
194	}: UseClientRenderOptions) {
195	  return useCallback(async () => {
196	    if (!resolvedConfig || !metadata) {
197	      toast.error('Timeline is not ready to render yet');
198	      return;
199	    }
200	
201	    if (typeof VideoEncoder === 'undefined') {
202	      const message = 'WebCodecs not supported in this browser';
203	      setRenderStatus('error');
204	      appendLogLine(setRenderLog, message);
205	      toast.error(message);
206	      return;
207	    }
208	
209	    setRenderStatus('rendering');
210	    setRenderProgress({
211	      current: 0,
212	      total: metadata.durationInFrames,
213	      percent: 0,
214	      phase: 'preparing',
215	    });
216	    setRenderResult({ url: null, filename: null });
217	    setRenderLog('');
218	    try {
219	      const { canRenderMediaOnWeb, renderMediaOnWeb } = await getWebRendererModule();
220	
221	      // Ensure dimensions are even (H264 requires multiples of 2)
222	      const width = metadata.compositionWidth % 2 === 0 ? metadata.compositionWidth : metadata.compositionWidth + 1;
223	      const height = metadata.compositionHeight % 2 === 0 ? metadata.compositionHeight : metadata.compositionHeight + 1;
224	
225	      // canRenderMediaOnWeb expects width/height as top-level options
226	      const canRenderOptions = {
227	        width,
228	        height,
229	        videoCodec: 'h264',
230	        audioCodec: 'aac',
231	      } satisfies Record<string, unknown>;
232	
233	      const canRender = await canRenderMediaOnWeb(canRenderOptions);
234	      if (!canRender.canRender) {
235	        const message = canRender.issues?.map((issue) => issue.message).filter(Boolean).join('\n')
236	          || 'This browser cannot render the selected video format.';
237	        throw new Error(message);
238	      }
239	
240	      const resolvedVideoCodec = canRender.resolvedVideoCodec ?? 'h264';
241	      const resolvedAudioCodec = canRender.resolvedAudioCodec ?? null;
242	      const extension = getFileExtension(resolvedVideoCodec);
243	      appendLogLine(setRenderLog, `Rendering ${width}x${height} @ ${metadata.fps}fps with ${resolvedVideoCodec}${resolvedAudioCodec ? ` + ${resolvedAudioCodec}` : ''}`);
244	
245	      // renderMediaOnWeb expects a composition object
246	      const composition = {
247	        id: 'video-editor-timeline-renderer',
248	        component: TimelineRenderer as ComponentType<{ config: ResolvedTimelineConfig }>,
249	        fps: metadata.fps,
250	        width,
251	        height,
252	        durationInFrames: metadata.durationInFrames,
253	      };
254	
255	      const renderResult = await renderMediaOnWeb({
256	        composition,
257	        inputProps: { config: resolvedConfig },
258	        [REDACTED],
259	        videoCodec: resolvedVideoCodec,
260	        audioCodec: resolvedAudioCodec,
261	        onProgress: (progress: unknown) => {
262	          setRenderProgress(getProgressUpdate(progress, metadata.durationInFrames));
263	        },
264	      } satisfies Record<string, unknown>);
265	
266	      const blob = await getBlobFromResult(renderResult, extension);
267	      if (!blob) {
268	        throw new Error('Browser renderer completed without a downloadable file');
269	      }
270	
271	      const filename = `timeline-render-${new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-')}.${extension}`;
272	      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
273	      setRenderProgress({
274	        current: metadata.durationInFrames,
275	        total: metadata.durationInFrames,
276	        percent: 100,
277	        phase: 'done',
278	      });
279	      setRenderDirty(false);
280	      setRenderResult({ url, filename });
281	      setRenderStatus('done');
282	      appendLogLine(setRenderLog, `Saved ${filename}`);
283	      toast.success('Render complete');
284	    } catch (error) {
285	      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown render error';
286	      setRenderStatus('error');
287	      appendLogLine(setRenderLog, message);
288	      toast.error('Render failed', { description: message });
289	    }
290	  }, [
291	    metadata,
292	    resolvedConfig,
293	    setRenderDirty,
294	    setRenderLog,
295	    setRenderProgress,
296	    setRenderResult,
297	    setRenderStatus,
298	  ]);
299	}
300

1	import React, { createContext, useMemo } from 'react';
2	import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
3	import { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';
4	import type { Task } from '@/types/tasks';
5	import {
6	  preloadGenerationTaskMappings,
7	  mergeGenerationsWithTaskData,
8	} from '@/domains/generation/hooks/tasks/generationTaskCache';
9	import { getProjectSelectionFallbackId } from '@/shared/contexts/projectSelectionStore';
10	
11	// ================================================================
12	// GENERATION-TASK INTEGRATION CONTEXT
13	// ================================================================
14	// This context provides the canonical generation -> task mapping preloader plus
15	// generation/task cache merging helpers used by media surfaces.
16	
17	interface GenerationTaskContextValue {
18	  // Methods for working with generation-task mappings
19	  preloadTaskMappings: (generationIds: string[]) => Promise<void>;
20	  enhanceWithTaskData: (generations: GenerationRow[]) => (GenerationRow & { taskId?: string | null; taskData?: Task | null })[];
21	  
22	  // Configuration
23	  isPreloadingEnabled: boolean;
24	  setPreloadingEnabled: (enabled: boolean) => void;
25	}
26	
27	const GenerationTaskContext = createContext<GenerationTaskContextValue | null>(null);
28	
29	interface GenerationTaskProviderProps {
30	  children: React.ReactNode;
31	  
32	  // Configuration options
33	  enableBackgroundPreloading?: boolean;
34	  preloadBatchSize?: number;
35	  preloadDelay?: number;
36	}
37	
38	export function GenerationTaskProvider({ 
39	  children, 
40	  enableBackgroundPreloading = true,
41	  preloadBatchSize = 5,
42	  preloadDelay = 200 
43	}: GenerationTaskProviderProps) {
44	  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
45	  const [isPreloadingEnabled, setPreloadingEnabled] = React.useState(enableBackgroundPreloading);
46	
47	  const preloadTaskMappings = React.useCallback(async (generationIds: string[]) => {
48	    if (!isPreloadingEnabled || generationIds.length === 0) return;
49	    const projectId = getProjectSelectionFallbackId();
50	
51	    try {
52	      await preloadGenerationTaskMappings(queryClient, generationIds, projectId, {
53	        batchSize: preloadBatchSize,
54	        delayBetweenBatches: preloadDelay,
55	        preloadFullTaskData: true, // Preload full task data for better UX
56	      });
57	    } catch {
58	      // Best effort preloading: the main UI can still function without prefetched task data.
59	    }
60	  }, [queryClient, isPreloadingEnabled, preloadBatchSize, preloadDelay]);
61	
62	  const enhanceWithTaskData = React.useCallback((generations: GenerationRow[]) => {
63	    const projectId = getProjectSelectionFallbackId();
64	    return mergeGenerationsWithTaskData(generations, queryClient, projectId);
65	  }, [queryClient]);
66	
67	  // Memoize context value to prevent unnecessary re-renders of consumers
68	  const contextValue = useMemo<GenerationTaskContextValue>(() => ({
69	    preloadTaskMappings,
70	    enhanceWithTaskData,
71	    isPreloadingEnabled,
72	    setPreloadingEnabled,
73	  }), [preloadTaskMappings, enhanceWithTaskData, isPreloadingEnabled, setPreloadingEnabled]);
74	
75	  return (
76	    <GenerationTaskContext.Provider value={contextValue}>
77	      {children}
78	    </GenerationTaskContext.Provider>
79	  );
80	}
81

Perfect! Now I have enough information to compile the findings. Let me create a final summary report:

## Top 10 Zustand Migration Candidates

**1. VideoEditorShell (FullEditorLayout)** — `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/components/VideoEditorShell.tsx` (lines 92–795, nested in 808-line file)
- **State**: 7 useState hooks managing layout state (timelineHeight, isTimelineMaximized, condensedRightPanel, isMobilePropertiesOpen, previewHostEl, tooSmall, previewActionButtonClass)
- **Why zustand**: State is deeply local to one component but frequently updated on user interaction (resizing, mode changes, preview toggling). No prop drilling, but encapsulates significant logic worth extracting for reuse across video-editor variants.
- **Risk**: `moderate` (150–200 lines changed; would split into layout + interaction substores)

**2. UserSettingsContext (mutable server state)** — `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/contexts/UserSettingsContext.tsx` (lines 43–154)
- **State**: userSettings object + isLoadingSettings, mutable via updateUserSettings callback
- **Consumers**: 4 direct uses found; context-as-store anti-pattern (updateUserSettings + setters)
- **Why zustand**: Server-persisted settings that sync cross-device. Zustand would separate read (query) from write (action) more cleanly; currently conflates fetch/update in one context.
- **Risk**: `moderate` (requires separating server sync from UI binding; ~100 lines)

**3. IncomingTasksContext (mutable placeholder queue)** — `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/contexts/IncomingTasksContext.tsx` (lines 65–159)
- **State**: incomingTasks array + cancellation tracking (useRef), 8 mutable actions (add/remove/resolve/cancel)
- **Consumers**: 7 direct uses (multiple generation creation flows)
- **Why zustand**: Classic state-as-queue anti-pattern. Many writers (task creation, cancellation) and readers (gallery, panes). Zustand would give atomic updates + computed cancellation flags.
- **Risk**: `trivial` (<50 lines; state shape fits zustand perfectly)

**4. ProjectContext (heavy tree of selection + derived state)** — `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/contexts/ProjectContext.tsx` (140 lines)
- **State**: selectedProjectId, projects list, computed derived selectors
- **Consumers**: 64 direct uses; re-renders all on selection change
- **Why zustand**: Very high consumer count. Selection is a core app singleton; zustand would decouple ID selector from list queries.
- **Risk**: `moderate` (64 consumers means broad change; but clean migration path)

**5. VideoTravelSettingsProvider (4-context tower)** — `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/providers/VideoTravelSettingsProvider.tsx` (150+ lines shown)
- **State**: settings object (150+ fields), status, isDirty, loading, handlers, save ops; split into 4 nested contexts
- **Why zustand**: Multi-domain settings with complex derived state (phase configs, LoRA selection, form validation). Four context layers screams "extract to store".
- **Risk**: `moderate` (state is well-shaped for zustand; would collapse 4 contexts to 1 store + selectors)

**6. LightboxStateContext (4-context value tree, no mutations)** — `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/contexts/LightboxStateContext.tsx` (lines 119–134)
- **State**: core, media, variants, navigation domains; handlers are no-ops when provider missing (pattern violation per CLAUDE.md)
- **Why zustand**: Safe hooks with fallback no-ops defeat provider-safety contracts. Zustand selectors would clarify intent (optional vs required).
- **Risk**: `moderate` (clean refactor; adds type safety)

**7. webRendererCache singleton** — `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useClientRender.ts` (line 44)
- **State**: `let webRendererCache: WebRendererModule | null = null` — module-level singleton holding dynamically loaded renderer
- **Why zustand**: Lifetime leaks across renders. No React lifecycle management. Should use zustand + cleanup on unmount, or move to useMemo.
- **Risk**: `trivial` (<30 lines; simple migration)

**8. ImageEditFormContext (form state with setters)** — `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/contexts/ImageEditFormContext.tsx` (lines 54–117)
- **State**: 10+ form fields (prompts, strengths, LoRA, model) with individual setters
- **Pattern**: `{ state, setState }` repeated 10× (antipattern per your brief)
- **Why zustand**: Form state belongs in a store. Zustand action `updateForm({ inpaintPrompt, img2imgStrength, ... })` beats 10 separate setters.
- **Risk**: `trivial` (form shape is simple; few consumers)

**9. AIInputModeContext (mode + handlers)** — `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/contexts/AIInputModeContext.tsx` (42 lines)
- **State**: inputMode string + handler, updates on user mode switch
- **Consumers**: 14 direct uses
- **Why zustand**: Simple enum + handler. High consumer count for low state complexity. Zustand atoms would be more efficient.
- **Risk**: `trivial` (rename hook, move to zustand store)

**10. GenerationTaskContext (stateful caching)** — `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/contexts/GenerationTaskContext.tsx` (lines 38–80)
- **State**: isPreloadingEnabled boolean + mutable callbacks
- **Consumers**: Implicit (generation gallery surfaces consume cached task mappings)
- **Why zustand**: Preload configuration should be a persistent store setting, not re-created on every render. Zustand would let components memoize it.
- **Risk**: `trivial` (<30 lines)

---

**High-value candidates** (most migration impact):
1. **ProjectContext** (64 consumers, app-level selection)
2. **IncomingTasksContext** (7 consumers, queue anti-pattern)
3. **VideoTravelSettingsProvider** (nested towers, multi-domain)

**Quick wins** (easiest migrations):
- webRendererCache singleton
- AIInputModeContext
- ImageEditFormContext

**Existing zustand stores** to reference: `overlayStack`, `panesStore`, `selectionStore` — use their patterns for new stores.

Perfect! Now let me check line 45-46 of selectionStore more carefully and search for the `activeTaskId` field and its usage:

Based on my thorough analysis of the zustand stores in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src`, here are the anti-patterns I found:

## Zustand Anti-Pattern Findings

### D. Global state that should be per-instance

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/state/selectionStore.ts`
**Line:** 45-46, 377-382
**Pattern:** Pattern D – Global state holding instance IDs
```typescript
activeTaskId: string | null;
setActiveTaskId: (taskId: string | null) => void;
```
**Evidence:** The store holds `activeTaskId` (line 45), `currentShotId` (line 59), and `lastAffectedShotId` (line 60) at module scope. These are entity IDs that depend on route context. If two task modals or shot editors can open in parallel (e.g., sidebar + main view), this state forces them to conflict since only one `activeTaskId` exists globally.
**Severity:** `bug` – Visible incorrect behavior when multiple instances of the same entity type are active.
**Suggested fix:** Move `activeTaskId` to route params or a per-modal Context; keep only selection-related state (clips, gallery items) in the global store.

---

### D. Global state that should be per-instance

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/state/panesStore.ts`
**Line:** 45-46, 377-382
**Pattern:** Pattern D – Global state holding instance IDs
```typescript
activeTaskId: string | null;
setActiveTaskId: (taskId: string | null) => void;
```
**Evidence:** `panesStore` also duplicates `activeTaskId` (line 45) and setter (line 46), making it a second source of truth for the same entity ID. Updates to one store don't sync with the other, causing desync between pane layout and task selection state.
**Severity:** `bug` – State divergence between two stores tracking the same ID.
**Suggested fix:** Consolidate `activeTaskId` into a single store (or URL/Context); remove it from `panesStore`.

---

### A. Duplicating server state

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/state/realtimeStore.ts`
**Line:** 15–20, 268–327
**Pattern:** Pattern A – Store duplicates Task rows fetched from Supabase
```typescript
interface RealtimeTaskScopeState {
  tasks: Record<string, Task>;  // Task[] from Supabase has id, created_at, *_id fields
  ...
}
const realtimeTaskStore = createStore<RealtimeStoreState>((set, get) => ({
  scopes: {},
  upsertTask: (task, projectId) => { ... },
  upsertTasks: (tasks, projectId) => { ... },
  ...
}));
```
**Evidence:** Lines 268–327 define a vanilla store holding `tasks: Record<string, Task>`, where `Task` is a Supabase row with fields like `id`, `created_at`, `status`, etc. This is cache-level state duplication. React Query should own `Task` entities; zustand should hold only derived/local state (e.g., "pending pending tasks for a generation"). The store calls `mapTaskDbRowToTask()` (line 390) to convert rows, indicating direct database-to-store flow with no React Query involvement.
**Severity:** `perf` + `smell` – Unnecessary duplication creates a stale-data risk; React Query is the correct owner.
**Suggested fix:** Remove Task entities from zustand; use React Query for server state; keep only generation-to-pending-task mappings in zustand.

---

### C. Missing `shallow` / equality function on composite selectors

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/state/panesStore.ts`
**Line:** 440
**Pattern:** Pattern C – Selector returns object literal without shallow
```typescript
export function usePanesStoreAvailability(): PanesStoreAvailability {
  return usePanesStore((state) => state.availability, shallow);
}
```
**Evidence:** This hook correctly uses `shallow` (line 440), but is listed for context. However, examining line 467–510, the `useBootstrapPanesStore()` hook builds a large bootstrap input object (`bootstrapInput`) with many function references. When these callbacks change, the object is recreated. The effect at line 517–520 then re-runs, calling `bootstrapPanesStore()` again even though the underlying pane state hasn't changed. This is a reference-identity leak, not directly a selector problem, but a related anti-pattern.
**Severity:** `perf` – Unnecessary re-bootstraps on every prop change.
**Suggested fix:** Memoize callbacks or use a stable key to deduplicate bootstrap updates (already partially mitigated by `areBootstrapInputsEqual` check at line 391, but callback references still cause thrashing).

---

### G. Redundant stores

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/state/selectionStore.ts` and `panesStore.ts`
**Line:** selectionStore line 45, panesStore line 45
**Pattern:** Pattern G – Two stores holding the same field
**Evidence:** Both `selectionStore` and `panesStore` define `activeTaskId`. This violates single-source-of-truth; updates to one are invisible to the other. This is a design smell that forces callers to check both stores or manually sync them.
**Severity:** `smell` – Maintenance burden; easy to forget to update both.
**Suggested fix:** Remove `activeTaskId` from one store; use a single source of truth (ideally URL or Context).

---

### Summary (by severity)

| Issue | File | Line | Pattern | Severity |
|-------|------|------|---------|----------|
| `activeTaskId` global state conflicts | selectionStore.ts | 45–46, 377 | D | **bug** |
| `activeTaskId` duplicate in panesStore | panesStore.ts | 45–46, 377 | D + G | **bug** |
| Task rows duplicated from Supabase | realtimeStore.ts | 15–20, 268 | A | **perf** |
| Bootstrap object ref churn | panesStore.ts | 482–510 | C (related) | **perf** |

**Patterns NOT found:**
- **B (over-subscription):** All hook calls use explicit selectors (e.g., `useTimelineDataSelector((state) => ({}), shallow)`); no bare `useStore()` calls detected.
- **E (module-scope `.subscribe()` side effects):** All store subscriptions are inside React Effects or hooks; no module-level side effects detected.
- **F (missing reset/lifecycle):** All stores have `reset`/`clear`/`resetForProjectChange` methods (e.g., `__resetSelectionStoreForTests`, `__resetPanesStoreForTests`).

280	    activeTaskId: currentState.activeTaskId,
281	    isTasksPaneOpen: input.isTasksPaneOpen,
282	  };
283	}
284	
285	function buildStateWithUpdatedGenerationsHeight(
286	  state: Pick<
287	    PanesStoreState,
288	    | 'availability'
289	    | 'generationsPaneHeight'
290	    | 'shotsPaneWidth'
291	    | 'tasksPaneWidth'
292	    | 'activeTaskId'
293	    | 'isGenerationsPaneLocked'
294	    | 'isGenerationsPaneOpen'
295	    | 'isEditorPaneLocked'
296	    | 'isEditorPaneOpen'
297	    | 'isShotsPaneLocked'
298	    | 'isTasksPaneLocked'
299	    | 'isTasksPaneOpen'
300	  >,
301	  generationsPaneHeight: number,
302	) {
303	  const viewportHeight = activeBootstrapSnapshot?.viewportHeight ?? getViewportHeight();
304	  const layout = deriveLayout({
305	    viewportHeight,
306	    generationsPaneHeight,
307	    isGenerationsPaneLocked: state.isGenerationsPaneLocked,
308	    isGenerationsPaneOpen: state.isGenerationsPaneOpen,
309	    isEditorPaneLocked: state.isEditorPaneLocked,
310	    isEditorPaneOpen: state.isEditorPaneOpen,
311	  });
312	
313	  return {
314	    availability: state.availability,
315	    isGenerationsPaneLocked: state.isGenerationsPaneLocked,
316	    isGenerationsPaneOpen: state.isGenerationsPaneOpen,
317	    generationsPaneHeight,
318	    effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight: layout.effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight,
319	    isEditorPaneLocked: state.isEditorPaneLocked,
320	    isEditorPaneOpen: state.isEditorPaneOpen,
321	    editorPaneHeight: layout.editorPaneHeight,
322	    effectiveEditorPaneHeight: layout.effectiveEditorPaneHeight,
323	    isShotsPaneLocked: state.isShotsPaneLocked,
324	    shotsPaneWidth: state.shotsPaneWidth,
325	    isTasksPaneLocked: state.isTasksPaneLocked,
326	    tasksPaneWidth: state.tasksPaneWidth,
327	    activeTaskId: state.activeTaskId,
328	    isTasksPaneOpen: state.isTasksPaneOpen,
329	  };
330	}
331	
332	const initialSnapshot = createInitialSnapshot();
333	
334	const panesStore = createStore<PanesStoreState>((set, get) => ({
335	  ...initialSnapshot,
336	  setIsGenerationsPaneLocked: (isLocked) => {
337	    activeBootstrapRuntime?.setIsGenerationsPaneLocked(isLocked);
338	  },
339	  setIsGenerationsPaneOpen: (isOpen) => {
340	    activeBootstrapRuntime?.setIsGenerationsPaneOpen(isOpen);
341	  },
342	  setGenerationsPaneHeight: (height) => {
343	    set((state) => {
344	      if (state.generationsPaneHeight === height) {
345	        return state;
346	      }
347	
348	      return buildStateWithUpdatedGenerationsHeight(state, height);
349	    });
350	  },
351	  setIsEditorPaneLocked: (isLocked) => {
352	    activeBootstrapRuntime?.setIsEditorPaneLocked(isLocked);
353	  },
354	  setIsEditorPaneOpen: (isOpen) => {
355	    activeBootstrapRuntime?.setIsEditorPaneOpen(isOpen);
356	  },
357	  setIsShotsPaneLocked: (isLocked) => {
358	    activeBootstrapRuntime?.setIsShotsPaneLocked(isLocked);
359	  },
360	  setShotsPaneWidth: (width) => {
361	    set((state) => (
362	      state.shotsPaneWidth === width
363	        ? state
364	        : { shotsPaneWidth: width }
365	    ));
366	  },
367	  setIsTasksPaneLocked: (isLocked) => {
368	    activeBootstrapRuntime?.setIsTasksPaneLocked(isLocked);
369	  },
370	  setTasksPaneWidth: (width) => {
371	    set((state) => (
372	      state.tasksPaneWidth === width
373	        ? state
374	        : { tasksPaneWidth: width }
375	    ));
376	  },
377	  setActiveTaskId: (taskId) => {
378	    set((state) => (
379	      state.activeTaskId === taskId
380	        ? state
381	        : { activeTaskId: taskId }
382	    ));
383	  },
384	  setIsTasksPaneOpen: (isOpen) => {
385	    activeBootstrapRuntime?.setIsTasksPaneOpen(isOpen);
386	  },
387	  resetAllPaneLocks: () => {
388	    activeBootstrapRuntime?.resetAllPaneLocks();
389	  },
390	  bootstrap: (input) => {
391	    if (areBootstrapInputsEqual(activeBootstrapSnapshot, input)) {
392	      console.log('[PanesBoot] bootstrap short-circuit (inputs equal)', { owner: input.owner.toString() });
393	      return;
394	    }
395	
396	    console.log('[PanesBoot] bootstrap APPLY set()', {
397	      owner: input.owner.toString(),
398	      prevOwner: activeBootstrapOwner?.toString() ?? null,
399	      locks: input.locks,
400	      isGenerationsPaneOpen: input.isGenerationsPaneOpen,
401	      isEditorPaneOpen: input.isEditorPaneOpen,
402	      isTasksPaneOpen: input.isTasksPaneOpen,
403	    });
404	    activeBootstrapOwner = input.owner;
405	    activeBootstrapSnapshot = input;
406	    updateBootstrapRuntime(input);
407	
408	    set((state) => buildBootstrappedState(state, input));
409	  },
410	  clearBootstrap: (owner) => {
411	    if (owner && activeBootstrapOwner && owner !== activeBootstrapOwner) {
412	      return;
413	    }
414	
415	    activeBootstrapOwner = null;
416	    activeBootstrapSnapshot = null;
417	    updateBootstrapRuntime(null);
418	    set(() => createInitialSnapshot());
419	  },
420	  resetStore: () => {
421	    activeBootstrapOwner = null;
422	    activeBootstrapSnapshot = null;
423	    updateBootstrapRuntime(null);
424	    set(() => createInitialSnapshot());
425	  },
426	}));
427	
428	export function usePanesStoreApi() {
429	  return panesStore;
430	}
431	
432	export function usePanesStore<T>(
433	  selector: (state: PanesStoreState) => T,
434	  equalityFn?: (left: T, right: T) => boolean,
435	): T {
436	  return useStoreWithEqualityFn(panesStore, selector, equalityFn);
437	}
438	
439	export function usePanesStoreAvailability(): PanesStoreAvailability {
440	  return usePanesStore((state) => state.availability, shallow);
441	}
442	
443	export function bootstrapPanesStore(input: PanesStoreBootstrapInput): void {
444	  panesStore.getState().bootstrap(input);
445	}
446	
447	export function clearPanesStoreBootstrap(owner?: symbol): void {
448	  panesStore.getState().clearBootstrap(owner);
449	}
450	
451	export function resetPanesStore(): void {
452	  panesStore.getState().resetStore();
453	}
454	
455	/**
456	 * Singleton panes lifecycle contract:
457	 * - exactly one mounted bootstrap owner should drive runtime-backed pane state
458	 * - repeated bootstraps from that owner are idempotent
459	 * - reads outside bootstrap intentionally return the unlocked default snapshot
460	 */
461	export function useBootstrapPanesStore(): void {
462	  const ownerRef = useRef<symbol | null>(null);
463	  if (ownerRef.current === null) {
464	    ownerRef.current = Symbol('panes-store-bootstrap-owner');
465	  }
466	
467	  const {
468	    locks,
469	    isGenerationsPaneOpenState,
470	    isEditorPaneOpenState,
471	    isTasksPaneOpenState,
472	    setIsGenerationsPaneLocked,
473	    setIsEditorPaneLocked,
474	    setIsShotsPaneLocked,
475	    setIsTasksPaneLocked,
476	    setIsGenerationsPaneOpen,
477	    setIsEditorPaneOpen,
478	    setIsTasksPaneOpen,
479	    resetAllPaneLocks,
480	  } = usePaneLockPolicyState();
481	
482	  const bootstrapInput = useMemo<PanesStoreBootstrapInput>(() => ({
483	    owner: ownerRef.current as symbol,
484	    viewportHeight: getViewportHeight(),
485	    locks,
486	    isGenerationsPaneOpen: isGenerationsPaneOpenState,
487	    isEditorPaneOpen: isEditorPaneOpenState,
488	    isTasksPaneOpen: isTasksPaneOpenState,
489	    setIsGenerationsPaneLocked,
490	    setIsEditorPaneLocked,
491	    setIsShotsPaneLocked,
492	    setIsTasksPaneLocked,
493	    setIsGenerationsPaneOpen,
494	    setIsEditorPaneOpen,
495	    setIsTasksPaneOpen,
496	    resetAllPaneLocks,
497	  }), [
498	    locks,
499	    isGenerationsPaneOpenState,
500	    isEditorPaneOpenState,
501	    isTasksPaneOpenState,
502	    setIsGenerationsPaneLocked,
503	    setIsEditorPaneLocked,
504	    setIsShotsPaneLocked,
505	    setIsTasksPaneLocked,
506	    setIsGenerationsPaneOpen,
507	    setIsEditorPaneOpen,
508	    setIsTasksPaneOpen,
509	    resetAllPaneLocks,
510	  ]);
511	
512	  console.log('[PanesBoot] useBootstrapPanesStore render', {
513	    owner: ownerRef.current?.toString(),
514	    inputId: bootstrapInput && (bootstrapInput as unknown as { __id?: number }).__id,
515	  });
516	
517	  useEffect(() => {
518	    console.log('[PanesBoot] effect fires (bootstrapInput changed identity)');
519	    bootstrapPanesStore(bootstrapInput);
520	  }, [bootstrapInput]);
521	
522	  useEffect(() => {
523	    return () => {
524	      console.log('[PanesBoot] CLEANUP unmount', { owner: ownerRef.current?.toString() });
525	      clearPanesStoreBootstrap(ownerRef.current ?? undefined);
526	    };
527	  }, []);
528	}
529	
530	export function PanesStoreBootstrapBoundary({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
531	  console.log('[PanesBoot] PanesStoreBootstrapBoundary render');
532	  useBootstrapPanesStore();
533	  return children;
534	}
535	
536	export function __resetPanesStoreForTests(): void {
537	  resetPanesStore();
538	}
539

1	/**
2	 * LightboxShell - Dialog/Overlay container for MediaLightbox
3	 *
4	 * Encapsulates all the complex event handling for the lightbox modal:
5	 * - Dialog root/portal/overlay
6	 * - Pointer/touch/click event handling with z-index awareness
7	 * - Body scroll locking
8	 * - Accessibility elements
9	 *
10	 * Note: Tasks pane controls are handled by the existing PaneControlTab from TasksPane,
11	 * which is visible above the lightbox at z-[100001]. The overlay adjusts its size
12	 * to account for the pane when it's open or locked.
13	 *
14	 * This allows the main MediaLightbox to focus on content orchestration.
15	 */
16	
17	import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
18	import { cn } from '@/shared/components/ui/contracts/cn';
19	import {
20	  LightboxDialog,
21	  LightboxDialogBackdrop,
22	  LightboxDialogDescription,
23	  LightboxDialogPopup,
24	  LightboxDialogPortal,
25	  LightboxDialogTitle,
26	} from '@/shared/components/ui/overlay';
27	import { useLightboxShellInteractionHandlers } from '@/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useLightboxShellInteractionHandlers';
28	import { useLightboxViewportLock } from '@/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useLightboxViewportLock';
29	import { useLightboxPaneLayout } from '@/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useLightboxPaneLayout';
30	import type { OverlayViewportConstraints } from '@/shared/lib/layout/overlayViewportConstraints';
31	import { useRenderBudget } from '@/shared/dev/useRenderBudget';
32	
33	interface LightboxShellProps {
34	  children: React.ReactNode;
35	  onClose: () => void;
36	
37	  // Edit mode
38	  /** Canvas/brush edit overlay is active (inpaint, annotate, reposition, text).
39	   *  Used to allow canvas touch events to pass through. */
40	  hasCanvasOverlay: boolean;
41	  /** Reposition (move) mode — the only edit mode that blocks background click-to-close
42	   *  (because dragging could cause accidental closes). */
43	  isRepositionMode: boolean;
44	
45	  // Responsive
46	  isMobile: boolean;
47	  isTabletOrLarger: boolean;
48	
49	  // Layout constraints from the layout subsystem.
50	  overlayViewport: OverlayViewportConstraints;
51	
52	  // Ref for content
53	  contentRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>;
54	
55	  // Accessibility
56	  accessibilityTitle: string;
57	  accessibilityDescription: string;
58	}
59	
60	export const LightboxShell: React.FC<LightboxShellProps> = ({
61	  children,
62	  onClose,
63	  hasCanvasOverlay,
64	  isRepositionMode,
65	  isMobile,
66	  isTabletOrLarger,
67	  overlayViewport,
68	  contentRef,
69	  accessibilityTitle,
70	  accessibilityDescription,
71	}) => {
72	  useRenderBudget('LightboxShell', 5);
73	  const isActuallyModal = isMobile && !isTabletOrLarger;
74	  useLightboxViewportLock({ isActuallyModal });
75	
76	  const {
77	    handleOverlayPointerDown,
78	    handleOverlayPointerUp,
79	    handleBgPointerDownCapture,
80	    handleBgClickCapture,
81	    handleContentPointerDown,
82	    handleContentClick,
83	    handleTouchEvent,
84	    handleTouchCancel,
85	  } = useLightboxShellInteractionHandlers({
86	    hasCanvasOverlay,
87	    isRepositionMode,
88	    isMobile,
89	    onClose,
90	    popupRef: contentRef,
91	  });
92	
93	  // Focus the content element on mount
94	  useEffect(() => {
95	    contentRef.current?.focus();
96	  }, [contentRef]);
97	
98	  const { overlayStyle, contentStyle } = useLightboxPaneLayout({
99	    overlayViewport,
100	  });
101	
102	  return (
103	    <LightboxDialog
104	      open={true}
105	      modal={isActuallyModal}
106	      disablePointerDismissal
107	      onOpenChange={(_open, eventDetails) => {
108	        // Prevent Base UI from updating internal state (which corrupts controlled open={true})
109	        // and from calling stopPropagation on the native event (which blocks our custom handlers).
110	        // Our own handlers in useLightboxNavigation (Escape) and our overlay dismissal handlers
111	        // manage all closing logic.
112	        eventDetails.cancel();
113	        eventDetails.allowPropagation();
114	      }}
115	    >
116	      <LightboxDialogPortal>
117	        <LightboxDialogBackdrop
118	          data-dialog-backdrop
119	          className={cn(
120	            "fixed bg-black/80 p-0 border-none shadow-none",
121	            "data-[open]:animate-in data-[ending-style]:animate-out data-[ending-style]:fade-out-0 data-[open]:fade-in-0",
122	            !isMobile && "duration-200"
123	          )}
124	          onPointerDown={handleOverlayPointerDown}
125	          onPointerUp={handleOverlayPointerUp}
126	          onTouchStart={handleTouchEvent}
127	          onTouchMove={handleTouchEvent}
128	          onTouchEnd={handleTouchEvent}
129	          onTouchCancel={handleTouchCancel}
130	          style={{
131	            ...overlayStyle,
132	          }}
133	        />
134	
135	        {/* Task pane handle removed - the existing PaneControlTab from TasksPane
136	            is now visible above the lightbox and handles all pane controls.
137	            The overlay correctly accounts for the pane via shouldAccountForTasksPane. */}
138	
139	        <LightboxDialogPopup
140	          ref={contentRef}
141	          data-lightbox-popup
142	          tabIndex={-1}
143	          onPointerDownCapture={handleBgPointerDownCapture}
144	          onClickCapture={handleBgClickCapture}
145	          onPointerDown={handleContentPointerDown}
146	          onClick={handleContentClick}
147	          onTouchStart={handleTouchEvent}
148	          onTouchMove={handleTouchEvent}
149	          onTouchEnd={handleTouchEvent}
150	          onTouchCancel={handleTouchCancel}
151	          className={cn(
152	            "fixed",
153	            isMobile
154	              ? ""
155	              : "duration-200 data-[open]:animate-in data-[ending-style]:animate-out data-[ending-style]:fade-out-0 data-[open]:fade-in-0 data-[ending-style]:zoom-out-95 data-[open]:zoom-in-95",
156	            "p-0 border-none bg-transparent shadow-none",
157	            overlayViewport.needsFullscreenLayout
158	              ? "inset-0 w-full h-full"
159	              : "left-[50%] top-[50%] translate-x-[-50%] translate-y-[-50%] w-auto h-auto data-[ending-style]:slide-out-to-left-1/2 data-[ending-style]:slide-out-to-top-[48%] data-[open]:slide-in-from-left-1/2 data-[open]:slide-in-from-top-[48%]"
160	          )}
161	          style={{
162	            ...contentStyle,
163	          }}
164	        >
165	          {/* Accessibility: Hidden dialog title for screen readers */}
166	          <LightboxDialogTitle className="sr-only">
167	            {accessibilityTitle}
168	          </LightboxDialogTitle>
169	          <LightboxDialogDescription className="sr-only">
170	            {accessibilityDescription}
171	          </LightboxDialogDescription>
172	
173	          {children}
174	        </LightboxDialogPopup>
175	      </LightboxDialogPortal>
176	    </LightboxDialog>
177	  );
178	};
179

1	import * as React from 'react';
2	
3	const OVERLAY_BASE_Z_INDEX = 1000;
4	const OVERLAY_LAYER_STEP = 10;
5	
6	const OVERLAY_LAYER_SLOT_OFFSET = {
7	  backdrop: 0,
8	  positioner: 1,
9	  popup: 2,
10	} as const;
11	
12	type OverlayLayerSlot = keyof typeof OVERLAY_LAYER_SLOT_OFFSET;
13	
14	export function composeRefs<T>(
15	  ...refs: Array<React.Ref<T> | undefined | null>
16	): React.RefCallback<T> {
17	  return (node) => {
18	    refs.forEach((ref) => {
19	      if (typeof ref === 'function') {
20	        ref(node);
21	      } else if (ref) {
22	        (ref as React.MutableRefObject<T | null>).current = node;
23	      }
24	    });
25	  };
26	}
27	
28	export function getOverlayLayerStyle(
29	  layer: number | null,
30	  slot: OverlayLayerSlot,
31	  style?: React.CSSProperties,
32	): React.CSSProperties {
33	  const resolvedLayer = Math.max(layer ?? 1, 1);
34	
35	  return {
36	    ...(style ?? {}),
37	    zIndex:
38	      OVERLAY_BASE_Z_INDEX +
39	      resolvedLayer * OVERLAY_LAYER_STEP +
40	      OVERLAY_LAYER_SLOT_OFFSET[slot],
41	  };
42	}
43
