69	// Helper component to wrap buttons in tooltips (desktop only)
70	const TooltipButton: React.FC<{
71	  tooltip?: string;
72	  children: React.ReactNode;
73	  showTooltip: boolean;
74	  side?: 'top' | 'bottom' | 'left' | 'right';
75	}> = ({ tooltip, children, showTooltip, side = 'right' }) => {
76	  if (!tooltip || !showTooltip) {
77	    return <>{children}</>;
78	  }
79	
80	  return (
81	    <Tooltip>
82	      <TooltipTrigger asChild>
83	        {children}
84	      </TooltipTrigger>
85	      <TooltipContent side={side} className="text-xs">
86	        {tooltip}
87	      </TooltipContent>
88	    </Tooltip>
89	  );
90	};
91	
92	const PaneControlTab: React.FC<PaneControlTabProps> = ({
93	  position: { side, paneDimension, bottomOffset = 0, horizontalOffset = 0 },
94	  state: { isLocked, isOpen },
95	  handlers: { toggleLock, openPane, handlePaneEnter, handlePaneLeave, customOpenAction },
96	  display: { paneIcon = 'chevron', customIcon, paneTooltip, allowMobileLock = false, shortcutHint } = {},
97	  actions,

113	  // Event handlers

116	      e.stopPropagation();

118	      callback();
119	    };
120	  }, []);

122	  const containerEventHandlers = {
123	    onClick: (e: React.MouseEvent) => { e.stopPropagation(); e.preventDefault(); },
124	    onPointerDown: (e: React.PointerEvent) => { e.stopPropagation(); e.preventDefault(); },
125	    onTouchStart: (e: React.TouchEvent) => { e.stopPropagation(); e.preventDefault(); },

145	  const buttonSize = isMobile ? "h-9 w-9" : "h-8 w-8";
146	
147	  const getIcon = () => {
148	    if (customIcon) return customIcon;
149	    if (paneIcon !== 'chevron') {
150	      switch (paneIcon) {
151	        case 'tools': return <LayoutGrid className={iconSize} />;
152	        case 'gallery': return <Images className={iconSize} />;
153	        case 'tasks': return <ListTodo className={iconSize} />;
154	      }
155	    }
156	    switch (side) {
157	      case 'left': return <ChevronRight className={iconSize} />;

161	      default: return null;

164	
165	  // ==========================================================================

173	        return thirdButton ? ['third'] : [];

179	        return ['third', 'lock', 'open'];

192	    }

269	            </Button>
270	          </TooltipButton>

272	
273	      case 'open':

* LightboxLayout - Unified layout for all lightbox configurations.
 *
 * Sub-components call context hooks directly instead of receiving a bundled model object.
 * Layout-derived values (computed from props + context) use the small useLightboxLayoutComputed hook.
 */

import React from 'react';
import { cn } from '@/shared/components/ui/contracts/cn';
import { Button } from '@/shared/components/ui/button';
import { Tooltip, TooltipContent, TooltipTrigger } from '@/shared/components/ui/tooltip';
import { Eraser, Square, Undo2, X } from 'lucide-react';
import type { LightboxLayoutProps } from './types';
import {
  useLightboxCoreSafe,
  useLightboxMediaSafe,
  useLightboxVariantsSafe,
  useLightboxNavigationSafe,
} from '../../contexts/LightboxStateContext';
import { useImageEditCanvasSafe } from '../../contexts/ImageEditCanvasContext';
import { useVideoEditSafe } from '../../contexts/VideoEditContext';

Perfect! Now I have all the information I need. Let me compile the report:

## **Z-Index and Locked Panels Analysis Report**

### **1. What is a "Locked Panel"?**

A locked panel is a sidebar/pane (TasksPane, GenerationsPane, etc.) that remains persistently visible on screen. The `panesStore` tracks lock state via flags like `isTasksPaneLocked`, `isGenerationsPaneLocked`, etc. When locked, panes stay open and render above other content.

**Locked pane components:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane/TasksPane.tsx` (lines 162–172): Container div with `zIndex: 100001` when locked
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/features/gallery/components/GenerationsPane/GenerationsPane.tsx` (line 105): Uses `zIndex: UI_Z_LAYERS.GENERATIONS_PANE` (100)

### **2. LightboxShell Z-Index Hierarchy**

From `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/components/LightboxShell.tsx`:
- **Backdrop**: `LightboxDialogBackdrop` → `getOverlayLayerStyle(layer, 'backdrop', ...)` → z-index = **1000 + (layer × 10) + 0**
- **Popup (content)**: `LightboxDialogPopup` → `getOverlayLayerStyle(layer, 'popup', ...)` → z-index = **1000 + (layer × 10) + 2**

The comment (line 11) mentions "PaneControlTab... visible above the lightbox at z-[100001]".

### **3. Pane Z-Indices and Rendering**

| Pane | Z-Index | Locked | Rendering | File |
|------|---------|--------|-----------|------|
| **TasksPane** | 100001 | Yes | Fixed div portal, non-modal | TasksPane.tsx:172 |
| **TasksPane Backdrop** | 100000 | Mobile | Portal, dismisses pane | TasksPane.tsx:144 |
| **GenerationsPane** | 100 | Yes | Fixed bottom surface | GenerationsPane.tsx:105 |
| **GenerationsPane Backdrop** | 99 | Mobile | Fixed inset-0 | GenerationsPane.tsx:25 |
| **GenerationsPane Control Tab** | 101–102 (CONTROL_LOCKED/UNLOCKED) | When lock/unlock visible | Fixed, portaled | PaneControlTab.tsx:314–315 |
| **TasksPane Control Tab** | 100011 (desktop) / 99 (mobile) | Always visible when pane visible | Fixed, portaled | PaneControlTab.tsx:311–313 (uses UI_Z_LAYERS) |
| **Lightbox Backdrop** | ~100010 (layer 1) | N/A | Modal overlay | getOverlayLayerStyle formula: 1000 + 10 + 0 |
| **Lightbox Popup** | ~100012 (layer 1) | N/A | Modal overlay | getOverlayLayerStyle formula: 1000 + 10 + 2 |

**Current formula** (shared.tsx:38–40):
```
zIndex = OVERLAY_BASE_Z_INDEX (1000) + layer × OVERLAY_LAYER_STEP (10) + OVERLAY_LAYER_SLOT_OFFSET[slot]
```
With layer=1 (typical), this gives backdrop=1010, popup=1012. But `UI_Z_LAYERS.LIGHTBOX_MODAL = 100010` suggests a different calculation path.

### **4. Recent Changes (Commit f06a5d787)**

The critical regression was introduced in commit `f06a5d787` "chore: overlay pointer-events cleanup". Key changes:

**Deletions that affected z-index/pointer visibility:**

1. **LightboxLayout.tsx (line 406)**: Deleted `pointer-events-auto` class
   ```diff
   - core.isMobile && imageEdit.isInpaintMode && 'pointer-events-auto',
   ```
   This deleted conditional `pointer-events-auto` on the mobile inpaint canvas background, which allowed the lightbox to receive pointer events when the canvas was active.

2. **WorkflowControlsBar.tsx (line 43)**: Deleted `pointer-events-auto` from controls bar
   ```diff
   - <div className="absolute bottom-24 left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 flex items-center z-[70] pointer-events-auto">
   + <div className="absolute bottom-24 left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 flex items-center z-[70]">
   ```

3. **LightboxShell.tsx (line 79)**: Deleted `handleOverlayClick` handler and removed `onClick={handleOverlayClick}` from backdrop.

**The bug:** When a locked panel is open (e.g., TasksPane at z-100001), and the lightbox opens (z-100010 modal), the lightbox *should* appear above the pane. However, the deletion of `pointer-events-auto` from the lightbox background in inpaint mode combined with the removal of the click handler means:
- The lightbox backdrop loses explicit pointer-events declaration
- Without `pointer-events-auto`, it may inherit `pointer-events-none` from its parent context
- Locked panes (z-100001) now visually/interactively block the lightbox (z-100010) on certain viewport sizes

### **5. getOverlayLayerStyle Formula**

From `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/components/ui/overlay/shared.tsx`:
```typescript
const OVERLAY_BASE_Z_INDEX = 1000;
const OVERLAY_LAYER_STEP = 10;
const OVERLAY_LAYER_SLOT_OFFSET = { backdrop: 0, positioner: 1, popup: 2 };

// Formula: zIndex = 1000 + layer*10 + slot_offset
```

For typical lightbox (layer=1): **backdrop=1010, popup=1012**
But TasksPane locked container = **100001** (orders of magnitude higher).

---

### **Root-Cause Hypothesis**

Commit f06a5d787 deleted the `pointer-events-auto` class from the lightbox background layer (LightboxLayout.tsx:406) during the pointer-events cleanup. Combined with the removal of the backdrop click handler, this left the lightbox interactive surface without explicit pointer-events, causing it to render *behind* the locked TasksPane (100001) when pointer-events inheritance propagates downward. The lightbox modal (z-100010) is still layered above the pane in CSS stacking order, but interaction events are blocked.

**One-line fix candidates:**
1. **LightboxLayout.tsx:406** — Restore the conditional `pointer-events-auto`:
   ```tsx
   core.isMobile && imageEdit.isInpaintMode && 'pointer-events-auto',
   ```
2. **LightboxShell.tsx:130** — Explicitly add `pointerEvents: 'auto'` to backdrop style to ensure it remains interactive
3. **shared.tsx:38** — Ensure `getOverlayLayerStyle` always sets `pointerEvents: 'auto'` for backdrop/popup slots when layer is set
